-- BEGIN SmallIntegralLattices.lean










/-!
The short-vector step for integral unimodular lattices of rank at most three.
The covolume and integrality hypotheses below are the actual hypotheses of
the small-lattice theorem; no lattice classification is assumed.
-/

noncomputable section

open MeasureTheory Metric Module Set
open scoped RealInnerProductSpace

namespace SmallIntegralLattices

variable {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [InnerProductSpace ℝ E]
  [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] [MeasurableSpace E] [BorelSpace E]

/-- Integrality of every lattice inner product, including squared norms. -/
def Integral (L : Submodule ℤ E) : Prop :=
  ∀ x ∈ L, ∀ y ∈ L, ∃ z : ℤ, ⟪x, y⟫ = (z : ℝ)

private theorem ball_four_thirds_large
    (hr : 1 ≤ finrank ℝ E) (hr' : finrank ℝ E ≤ 3) :
    (2 : ENNReal) ^ finrank ℝ E < volume (ball (0 : E) (4 / 3 : ℝ)) := by
  haveI : Nontrivial E := Module.nontrivial_of_finrank_pos (R := ℝ) (by omega)
  have hcases : finrank ℝ E = 1 ∨ finrank ℝ E = 2 ∨ finrank ℝ E = 3 := by omega
  rcases hcases with hd | hd | hd
  · rw [InnerProductSpace.volume_ball_of_dim_odd (k := 0) (by omega)]
    apply (ENNReal.toReal_lt_toReal (by finiteness) (by finiteness)).mp
    simp only [ENNReal.toReal_pow, ENNReal.toReal_mul, ENNReal.toReal_ofNat]
    rw [ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (by norm_num), ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (by positivity)]
    norm_num [hd]
  · rw [InnerProductSpace.volume_ball_of_dim_even (k := 1) (by omega)]
    apply (ENNReal.toReal_lt_toReal (by finiteness) (by finiteness)).mp
    simp only [ENNReal.toReal_pow, ENNReal.toReal_mul, ENNReal.toReal_ofNat]
    rw [ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (by norm_num), ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (by positivity)]
    norm_num [hd]
    nlinarith [Real.pi_gt_three]
  · rw [InnerProductSpace.volume_ball_of_dim_odd (k := 1) (by omega)]
    apply (ENNReal.toReal_lt_toReal (by finiteness) (by finiteness)).mp
    simp only [ENNReal.toReal_pow, ENNReal.toReal_mul, ENNReal.toReal_ofNat]
    rw [ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (by norm_num), ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (by positivity)]
    norm_num [hd]
    nlinarith [Real.pi_gt_three]

/-- A genuine Minkowski consequence: every integral covolume-one lattice
of rank one, two, or three contains a vector of squared norm one. -/
theorem exists_unit_vector (L : Submodule ℤ E) [DiscreteTopology L] [IsZLattice ℝ L]
    (hL : Integral L) (hvol : ZLattice.covolume L = 1)
    (hr : 1 ≤ finrank ℝ E) (hr' : finrank ℝ E ≤ 3) :
    ∃ u : E, u ∈ L ∧ ⟪u, u⟫ = 1 := by
  let b := Module.Free.chooseBasis ℤ L
  let F := ZSpan.fundamentalDomain (b.ofZLatticeBasis ℝ)
  have hfund : IsAddFundamentalDomain L F volume :=
    ZLattice.isAddFundamentalDomain b volume
  have hFreal : volume.real F = 1 := by
    rw [← ZLattice.covolume_eq_measure_fundamentalDomain L volume hfund]
    exact hvol
  have hFfinite : volume F ≠ ⊤ := by
    exact ne_of_lt ((ZSpan.fundamentalDomain_isBounded _).measure_lt_top)
  have hF : volume F = 1 := by
    apply (ENNReal.toReal_eq_toReal_iff' hFfinite ENNReal.one_ne_top).mp
    simpa only [ENNReal.toReal_one] using hFreal
  have hlarge : volume F * 2 ^ finrank ℝ E < volume (ball (0 : E) (4 / 3 : ℝ)) := by
    rw [hF, one_mul]
    exact ball_four_thirds_large hr hr'
  letI : Countable L.toAddSubgroup := inferInstanceAs (Countable L)
  obtain ⟨u, hu0, hu⟩ :=
    exists_ne_zero_mem_lattice_of_measure_mul_two_pow_lt_measure
      (L := L.toAddSubgroup) hfund
      (fun x hx => by simpa only [mem_ball_zero_iff, norm_neg] using hx)
      (convex_ball (0 : E) (4 / 3 : ℝ)) hlarge
  have hu0' : (u : E) ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    exact hu0 (Subtype.ext h)
  have hnorm : ‖(u : E)‖ < (4 / 3 : ℝ) := by
    simpa only [mem_ball_zero_iff] using hu
  obtain ⟨z, hz⟩ := hL (u : E) u.property (u : E) u.property
  have hzpos : (0 : ℝ) < (z : ℝ) := by
    rw [← hz, real_inner_self_eq_norm_sq]
    exact sq_pos_of_pos (norm_pos_iff.mpr hu0')
  have hzlt : (z : ℝ) < 2 := by
    rw [← hz, real_inner_self_eq_norm_sq]
    nlinarith [norm_nonneg (u : E)]
  have hzpos' : (0 : ℤ) < z := by exact_mod_cast hzpos
  have hzlt' : z < (2 : ℤ) := by exact_mod_cast hzlt
  have hz1 : z = 1 := by omega
  exact ⟨u, u.property, by simpa only [hz1, Int.cast_one] using hz⟩

/-- Subtracting the integral coordinate along a unit lattice vector stays
inside the lattice and is orthogonal to that vector. -/
theorem exists_integral_orthogonal_remainder
    (L : Submodule ℤ E) (hL : Integral L) {u x : E}
    (hu : u ∈ L) (hu1 : ⟪u, u⟫ = 1) (hx : x ∈ L) :
    ∃ n : ℤ, x - (n : ℝ) • u ∈ L ∧ ⟪x - (n : ℝ) • u, u⟫ = 0 := by
  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := hL x hx u hu
  refine ⟨n, ?_, ?_⟩
  · apply L.sub_mem hx
    simpa only [Int.cast_smul_eq_zsmul] using L.smul_mem n hu
  · rw [inner_sub_left, inner_smul_left, hu1, hn]
    simp

/-- The part of a lattice orthogonal to a specified vector, still regarded
as an integral submodule of the original Euclidean space. -/
def orthogonalSlice (L : Submodule ℤ E) (u : E) : Submodule ℤ E where
  carrier := {x | x ∈ L ∧ ⟪x, u⟫ = 0}
  zero_mem' := by simp
  add_mem' := by
    intro x y hx hy
    exact ⟨L.add_mem hx.1 hy.1, by simp [inner_add_left, hx.2, hy.2]⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    intro n x hx
    refine ⟨L.smul_mem n hx.1, ?_⟩
    rw [← Int.cast_smul_eq_zsmul ℝ, inner_smul_left, hx.2, mul_zero]

@[simp] theorem mem_orthogonalSlice (L : Submodule ℤ E) (u x : E) :
    x ∈ orthogonalSlice L u ↔ x ∈ L ∧ ⟪x, u⟫ = 0 := Iff.rfl

theorem orthogonalSlice_integral (L : Submodule ℤ E) (hL : Integral L) (u : E) :
    Integral (orthogonalSlice L u) := by
  intro x hx y hy
  exact hL x hx.1 y hy.1

/-- The unit direction and its integral orthogonal slice generate the whole
lattice. This is the algebraic direct-sum step of the classification. -/
theorem span_unit_sup_orthogonalSlice
    (L : Submodule ℤ E) (hL : Integral L) {u : E}
    (hu : u ∈ L) (hu1 : ⟪u, u⟫ = 1) :
    Submodule.span ℤ {u} ⊔ orthogonalSlice L u = L := by
  apply le_antisymm
  · apply sup_le
    · exact Submodule.span_le.mpr (by simpa using hu)
    · exact fun x hx => hx.1
  · intro x hx
    obtain ⟨n, hmem, horth⟩ := exists_integral_orthogonal_remainder L hL hu hu1 hx
    have hnu : (n : ℝ) • u ∈ Submodule.span ℤ ({u} : Set E) := by
      simpa only [Int.cast_smul_eq_zsmul] using
        (Submodule.span ℤ ({u} : Set E)).smul_mem n
          (Submodule.subset_span (Set.mem_singleton u))
    exact Submodule.mem_sup.mpr ⟨(n : ℝ) • u, hnu, x - (n : ℝ) • u,
      ⟨hmem, horth⟩, by simp⟩

/-- A unit direction intersects its orthogonal slice trivially. -/
theorem span_unit_inf_orthogonalSlice
    (L : Submodule ℤ E) {u : E} (hu1 : ⟪u, u⟫ = 1) :
    Submodule.span ℤ {u} ⊓ orthogonalSlice L u = ⊥ := by
  apply eq_bot_iff.mpr
  intro x hx
  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := Submodule.mem_span_singleton.mp hx.1
  have hzero := hx.2.2
  rw [← hn, ← Int.cast_smul_eq_zsmul ℝ, inner_smul_left, hu1] at hzero
  have hn0 : n = 0 := by exact_mod_cast (by simpa using hzero : (n : ℝ) = 0)
  simpa [hn0] using hn.symm

/-- The explicit integral splitting map along a unit lattice direction. -/
def unitSplitMap (L : Submodule ℤ E) {u : E} (hu : u ∈ L) :
    ℤ × orthogonalSlice L u →ₗ[ℤ] L where
  toFun v := ⟨v.1 • u + (v.2 : E), L.add_mem (L.smul_mem v.1 hu) v.2.property.1⟩
  map_add' := by
    intro v w
    ext
    simp [add_smul, add_add_add_comm]
  map_smul' := by
    intro a v
    ext
    simp [smul_add, mul_smul]

theorem unitSplitMap_bijective (L : Submodule ℤ E) (hL : Integral L)
    {u : E} (hu : u ∈ L) (hu1 : ⟪u, u⟫ = 1) :
    Function.Bijective (unitSplitMap L hu) := by
  constructor
  · intro v w h
    have hv : ⟪((unitSplitMap L hu v : L) : E), u⟫ = (v.1 : ℝ) := by
      change ⟪v.1 • u + (v.2 : E), u⟫ = (v.1 : ℝ)
      rw [inner_add_left, ← Int.cast_smul_eq_zsmul ℝ, inner_smul_left,
        hu1, v.2.property.2]
      simp
    have hw : ⟪((unitSplitMap L hu w : L) : E), u⟫ = (w.1 : ℝ) := by
      change ⟪w.1 • u + (w.2 : E), u⟫ = (w.1 : ℝ)
      rw [inner_add_left, ← Int.cast_smul_eq_zsmul ℝ, inner_smul_left,
        hu1, w.2.property.2]
      simp
    have hfst : v.1 = w.1 := by
      apply Int.cast_injective (α := ℝ)
      rw [← hv, ← hw, h]
    have hval : v.1 • u + (v.2 : E) = w.1 • u + (w.2 : E) :=
      congrArg (fun x : L => (x : E)) h
    have hsnd : v.2 = w.2 := by
      apply Subtype.ext
      rw [hfst] at hval
      exact add_left_cancel hval
    exact Prod.ext hfst hsnd
  · intro x
    obtain ⟨n, hmem, horth⟩ :=
      exists_integral_orthogonal_remainder L hL hu hu1 x.property
    refine ⟨(n, ⟨(x : E) - (n : ℝ) • u, hmem, horth⟩), ?_⟩
    apply Subtype.ext
    change n • u + ((x : E) - (n : ℝ) • u) = (x : E)
    rw [Int.cast_smul_eq_zsmul]
    simp

/-- The lattice is integrally isomorphic to a copy of `ℤ` and its orthogonal
slice. This constructs the basis-splitting equivalence, rather than assuming
that a unit vector has already been extended to an integral basis. -/
def unitSplitEquiv (L : Submodule ℤ E) (hL : Integral L)
    {u : E} (hu : u ∈ L) (hu1 : ⟪u, u⟫ = 1) :
    (ℤ × orthogonalSlice L u) ≃ₗ[ℤ] L :=
  LinearEquiv.ofBijective (unitSplitMap L hu) (unitSplitMap_bijective L hL hu hu1)

/-- Extending a basis of the integral orthogonal slice by the chosen unit
vector, through the explicit integral splitting equivalence. -/
def unitExtensionBasis {ι : Type*} (L : Submodule ℤ E) (hL : Integral L)
    {u : E} (hu : u ∈ L) (hu1 : ⟪u, u⟫ = 1)
    (c : Basis ι ℤ (orthogonalSlice L u)) : Basis (Unit ⊕ ι) ℤ L :=
  ((Basis.singleton Unit ℤ).prod c).map (unitSplitEquiv L hL hu hu1)

@[simp] theorem unitExtensionBasis_inl {ι : Type*} (L : Submodule ℤ E)
    (hL : Integral L) {u : E} (hu : u ∈ L) (hu1 : ⟪u, u⟫ = 1)
    (c : Basis ι ℤ (orthogonalSlice L u)) (a : Unit) :
    ((unitExtensionBasis L hL hu hu1 c (.inl a) : L) : E) = u := by
  simp [unitExtensionBasis, Basis.prod_apply, unitSplitEquiv, unitSplitMap]

@[simp] theorem unitExtensionBasis_inr {ι : Type*} (L : Submodule ℤ E)
    (hL : Integral L) {u : E} (hu : u ∈ L) (hu1 : ⟪u, u⟫ = 1)
    (c : Basis ι ℤ (orthogonalSlice L u)) (i : ι) :
    ((unitExtensionBasis L hL hu hu1 c (.inr i) : L) : E) = (c i : E) := by
  simp [unitExtensionBasis, Basis.prod_apply, unitSplitEquiv, unitSplitMap]

/-- The real orthogonal complement, as a genuine lower-dimensional
Euclidean ambient space. -/
def unitPerp (u : E) : Submodule ℝ E := (innerSL ℝ u).ker

/-- The orthogonal slice, now viewed as a lattice inside its own real span. -/
def perpendicularLattice (L : Submodule ℤ E) (u : E) : Submodule ℤ (unitPerp u) :=
  L.comap ((unitPerp u).subtype.restrictScalars ℤ)

/-- Orthogonal projection off a unit direction, with its range restricted
to the real orthogonal complement. -/
def unitPerpMap {u : E} (hu1 : ⟪u, u⟫ = 1) : E →ₗ[ℝ] unitPerp u where
  toFun x := ⟨x - ⟪u, x⟫ • u, by
    change ⟪u, x - ⟪u, x⟫ • u⟫ = 0
    rw [inner_sub_right, inner_smul_right, hu1]
    simp⟩
  map_add' := by
    intro x y
    apply Subtype.ext
    simp [inner_add_right, add_smul, add_sub_add_comm]
  map_smul' := by
    intro c x
    apply Subtype.ext
    simp [inner_smul_right, smul_sub, smul_smul, mul_comm]

@[simp] theorem unitPerpMap_apply_perp {u : E} (hu1 : ⟪u, u⟫ = 1)
    (x : unitPerp u) : unitPerpMap hu1 (x : E) = x := by
  apply Subtype.ext
  have hx : ⟪u, (x : E)⟫ = 0 := x.property
  simp [unitPerpMap, hx]

theorem unitPerpMap_mem_lattice (L : Submodule ℤ E) (hL : Integral L)
    {u : E} (hu : u ∈ L) (hu1 : ⟪u, u⟫ = 1) {x : E} (hx : x ∈ L) :
    unitPerpMap hu1 x ∈ perpendicularLattice L u := by
  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := hL u hu x hx
  change x - ⟪u, x⟫ • u ∈ L
  rw [hn]
  apply L.sub_mem hx
  simpa only [Int.cast_smul_eq_zsmul] using L.smul_mem n hu

instance perpendicularLattice_discrete (L : Submodule ℤ E) [DiscreteTopology L] (u : E) :
    DiscreteTopology (perpendicularLattice L u) := by
  let f : perpendicularLattice L u → L := fun x => ⟨((x : unitPerp u) : E), x.property⟩
  have hf : Continuous f := by
    exact (continuous_subtype_val.comp continuous_subtype_val).subtype_mk _
  have hinj : Function.Injective f := by
    intro x y h
    apply Subtype.ext
    apply Subtype.ext
    exact congrArg (fun z : L => (z : E)) h
  exact DiscreteTopology.of_continuous_injective hf hinj

theorem perpendicularLattice_isZLattice (L : Submodule ℤ E)
    [DiscreteTopology L] [IsZLattice ℝ L] (hL : Integral L)
    {u : E} (hu : u ∈ L) (hu1 : ⟪u, u⟫ = 1) :
    IsZLattice ℝ (perpendicularLattice L u) := by
  constructor
  let P := Submodule.span ℝ (perpendicularLattice L u : Set (unitPerp u))
  have hspan : Submodule.span ℝ (L : Set E) ≤ P.comap (unitPerpMap hu1) := by
    apply Submodule.span_le.mpr
    intro x hx
    exact Submodule.subset_span (unitPerpMap_mem_lattice L hL hu hu1 hx)
  apply top_unique
  intro x hx
  have hx' : (x : E) ∈ Submodule.span ℝ (L : Set E) := by
    rw [IsZLattice.span_top (K := ℝ)]
    trivial
  have h := hspan hx'
  simpa only [Submodule.mem_comap, unitPerpMap_apply_perp] using h

theorem perpendicularLattice_integral (L : Submodule ℤ E) (hL : Integral L) (u : E) :
    Integral (perpendicularLattice L u) := by
  intro x hx y hy
  exact hL (x : E) hx (y : E) hy

/-- Changing only the ambient subtype does not change the integral slice. -/
def perpendicularEquivSlice (L : Submodule ℤ E) (u : E) :
    perpendicularLattice L u ≃ₗ[ℤ] orthogonalSlice L u where
  toFun x := ⟨((x : unitPerp u) : E), x.property, by
    have h : ⟪u, ((x : unitPerp u) : E)⟫ = 0 := (x : unitPerp u).property
    simpa only [real_inner_comm] using h⟩
  invFun x := ⟨⟨(x : E), by
    change ⟪u, (x : E)⟫ = 0
    simpa only [real_inner_comm] using x.property.2⟩, x.property.1⟩
  left_inv _ := rfl
  right_inv _ := rfl
  map_add' _ _ := rfl
  map_smul' _ _ := rfl

/-- An orthonormal real coordinate basis with the same index as an arbitrary
integral basis. The matching cardinality follows from the lattice spanning
the real ambient space. -/
def orthonormalCoordinates {ι : Type*} [Fintype ι]
    (L : Submodule ℤ E) [DiscreteTopology L] [IsZLattice ℝ L]
    (b : Basis ι ℤ L) : OrthonormalBasis ι ℝ E :=
  (stdOrthonormalBasis ℝ E).reindex (Fintype.equivOfCardEq (by
    rw [Fintype.card_fin, finrank_eq_card_basis (b.ofZLatticeBasis ℝ)]))

/-- The square of a Euclidean lattice's covolume is the determinant of its
Gram matrix. The orthonormal coordinate basis is arbitrary. This identity
is the volume bridge needed after splitting off an integral unit vector. -/
theorem covolume_sq_eq_gram_det
    {ι : Type*} [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι]
    (L : Submodule ℤ E) [DiscreteTopology L] [IsZLattice ℝ L]
    (b : Basis ι ℤ L) (o : OrthonormalBasis ι ℝ E) :
    ZLattice.covolume L ^ 2 =
      (Matrix.of fun i j => ⟪(b i : E), (b j : E)⟫).det := by
  let A : Matrix ι ι ℝ := fun i j => o.repr (b j : E) i
  have hfund : volume.real (ZSpan.fundamentalDomain o.toBasis) = 1 := by
    rw [measureReal_congr (ZSpan.fundamentalDomain_ae_parallelepiped o.toBasis volume),
      measureReal_def, OrthonormalBasis.coe_toBasis, o.volume_parallelepiped]
    rfl
  have hcov : ZLattice.covolume L = |A.det| := by
    rw [ZLattice.covolume_eq_det_mul_measureReal L volume b o.toBasis, hfund, mul_one,
      Basis.det_apply]
    congr 2
  have hgram : (Matrix.of fun i j => ⟪(b i : E), (b j : E)⟫) = A.transpose * A := by
    ext i j
    simp only [Matrix.of_apply, Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.transpose_apply, A]
    symm
    simpa only [o.repr_apply_apply, real_inner_comm (b i : E)] using
      o.sum_inner_mul_inner (b i : E) (b j : E)
  rw [hcov, hgram, Matrix.det_mul, Matrix.det_transpose, sq_abs, pow_two]

/-- Complete classification in rank one: the lattice consists exactly of
integer multiples of a unit vector. -/
theorem rank_one_classification
    (L : Submodule ℤ E) [DiscreteTopology L] [IsZLattice ℝ L]
    (hL : Integral L) (hvol : ZLattice.covolume L = 1)
    (hr : finrank ℝ E = 1) :
    ∃ u : E, ⟪u, u⟫ = 1 ∧ L = Submodule.span ℤ {u} := by
  obtain ⟨u, hu, hu1⟩ := exists_unit_vector L hL hvol (by omega) (by omega)
  have hu0 : u ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    simp [h] at hu1
  refine ⟨u, hu1, le_antisymm ?_ ?_⟩
  · intro x hx
    obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := (finrank_eq_one_iff_of_nonzero' (K := ℝ) u hu0).mp hr x
    obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := hL x hx u hu
    have hcn : c = (n : ℝ) := by
      simpa only [← hc, real_inner_smul_left, hu1, mul_one] using hn
    apply Submodule.mem_span_singleton.mpr
    exact ⟨n, by simpa only [hcn, Int.cast_smul_eq_zsmul] using hc⟩
  · exact Submodule.span_le.mpr (by simpa using hu)

/-- An orthogonal unit extension preserves covolume one. Here `b` is the
actual extended integral basis and `c` is the basis of its orthogonal part;
the hypotheses record their concrete Gram entries, rather than assume a
classification or a covolume conclusion. -/
theorem orthogonal_extension_covolume_one
    {F : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup F] [InnerProductSpace ℝ F]
    [FiniteDimensional ℝ F] [MeasurableSpace F] [BorelSpace F]
    {ι : Type*} [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι]
    (L : Submodule ℤ E) [DiscreteTopology L] [IsZLattice ℝ L]
    (K : Submodule ℤ F) [DiscreteTopology K] [IsZLattice ℝ K]
    (b : Basis (Unit ⊕ ι) ℤ L) (c : Basis ι ℤ K)
    (o : OrthonormalBasis (Unit ⊕ ι) ℝ E) (p : OrthonormalBasis ι ℝ F)
    (hunit : ⟪(b (.inl ()) : E), (b (.inl ()) : E)⟫ = 1)
    (horth : ∀ i, ⟪(b (.inl ()) : E), (b (.inr i) : E)⟫ = 0)
    (hgram : ∀ i j, ⟪(b (.inr i) : E), (b (.inr j) : E)⟫ =
      ⟪(c i : F), (c j : F)⟫)
    (hvol : ZLattice.covolume L = 1) : ZLattice.covolume K = 1 := by
  have hblock : (Matrix.of fun i j => ⟪(b i : E), (b j : E)⟫) =
      Matrix.fromBlocks (fun _ _ : Unit => (1 : ℝ)) 0 0
        (Matrix.of fun i j => ⟪(c i : F), (c j : F)⟫) := by
    ext i j
    rcases i with ⟨⟩ | i <;> rcases j with ⟨⟩ | j
    · simpa using hunit
    · simpa using horth j
    · simpa only [Matrix.of_apply, Matrix.fromBlocks_apply₂₁, Matrix.zero_apply,
        real_inner_comm] using horth i
    · simpa using hgram i j
  have hsqL := covolume_sq_eq_gram_det L b o
  have hsqK := covolume_sq_eq_gram_det K c p
  rw [hblock, Matrix.det_fromBlocks_zero₂₁, Matrix.det_unique] at hsqL
  norm_num only [one_mul, hvol, one_pow] at hsqL
  have hpos := ZLattice.covolume_pos K volume
  nlinarith

/-- Splitting off a unit direction preserves the covolume of the actual
orthogonal lattice. This discharges the volume step needed for recursion. -/
theorem perpendicularLattice_covolume_one
    (L : Submodule ℤ E) [DiscreteTopology L] [IsZLattice ℝ L]
    (hL : Integral L) (hvol : ZLattice.covolume L = 1)
    {u : E} (hu : u ∈ L) (hu1 : ⟪u, u⟫ = 1) :
    ZLattice.covolume (perpendicularLattice L u) = 1 := by
  let K := perpendicularLattice L u
  letI : IsZLattice ℝ K := perpendicularLattice_isZLattice L hL hu hu1
  let c := Module.Free.chooseBasis ℤ K
  let b := unitExtensionBasis L hL hu hu1 (c.map (perpendicularEquivSlice L u))
  apply orthogonal_extension_covolume_one L K b c
    (orthonormalCoordinates L b) (orthonormalCoordinates K c)
  · simpa only [b, unitExtensionBasis_inl] using hu1
  · intro i
    simp only [b, unitExtensionBasis_inl, unitExtensionBasis_inr, Basis.map_apply]
    exact (c i : unitPerp u).property
  · intro i j
    simp only [b, unitExtensionBasis_inr, Basis.map_apply]
    rfl
  · exact hvol

theorem unitPerp_finrank {u : E} (hu1 : ⟪u, u⟫ = 1) :
    finrank ℝ (unitPerp u) + 1 = finrank ℝ E := by
  have hsurj : Function.Surjective ((innerSL ℝ u).toLinearMap) := by
    intro r
    refine ⟨r • u, ?_⟩
    change ⟪u, r • u⟫ = r
    rw [inner_smul_right, hu1, mul_one]
  have h := (innerSL ℝ u).toLinearMap.finrank_range_add_finrank_ker
  rw [LinearMap.range_eq_top.mpr hsurj, finrank_top, finrank_self] at h
  change 1 + finrank ℝ (unitPerp u) = finrank ℝ E at h
  omega

theorem unitExtensionBasis_orthonormal
    {ι : Type*} [DecidableEq ι]
    (L : Submodule ℤ E) (hL : Integral L)
    {u : E} (hu : u ∈ L) (hu1 : ⟪u, u⟫ = 1)
    (c : Basis ι ℤ (orthogonalSlice L u))
    (hc : Orthonormal ℝ (fun i => (c i : E))) :
    Orthonormal ℝ (fun i => ((unitExtensionBasis L hL hu hu1 c i : L) : E)) := by
  classical
  rw [orthonormal_iff_ite] at hc ⊢
  intro i j
  rcases i with ⟨⟩ | i <;> rcases j with ⟨⟩ | j
  · simp only [unitExtensionBasis_inl, hu1, ↓reduceIte]
  · have h := (c j).property.2
    simp only [unitExtensionBasis_inl, unitExtensionBasis_inr, Sum.inl_ne_inr,
      ↓reduceIte]
    rw [real_inner_comm]
    exact h
  · simpa only [unitExtensionBasis_inl, unitExtensionBasis_inr, Sum.inr_ne_inl,
      ↓reduceIte] using (c i).property.2
  · simpa only [unitExtensionBasis_inr, Sum.inr.injEq] using hc i j

/-- Integral positive-definite Euclidean lattices of rank at most three and
covolume one admit an orthonormal integral basis. The proof constructs the
orthogonal sublattice and its covolume before invoking induction. -/
theorem classification_rank
    (n : ℕ) (L : Submodule ℤ E) [DiscreteTopology L] [IsZLattice ℝ L]
    (hL : Integral L) (hvol : ZLattice.covolume L = 1)
    (hn : finrank ℝ E = n) (hn3 : n ≤ 3) :
    ∃ b : Basis (Fin n) ℤ L, Orthonormal ℝ (fun i => (b i : E)) := by
  classical
  induction n using Nat.strong_induction_on generalizing E with
  | h n ih =>
    rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos n with rfl | hnpos
    · haveI : Subsingleton E := (Module.finrank_zero_iff (R := ℝ) (M := E)).mp hn
      refine ⟨Basis.empty L, ?_⟩
      rw [orthonormal_iff_ite]
      intro i
      exact Fin.elim0 i
    · obtain ⟨u, hu, hu1⟩ := exists_unit_vector L hL hvol (by omega) (by omega)
      let K := perpendicularLattice L u
      letI : IsZLattice ℝ K := perpendicularLattice_isZLattice L hL hu hu1
      have hKvol : ZLattice.covolume K = 1 := perpendicularLattice_covolume_one L hL hvol hu hu1
      have hKrank : finrank ℝ (unitPerp u) = n - 1 := by
        have h := unitPerp_finrank hu1
        omega
      obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := ih (n - 1) (by omega) K
        (perpendicularLattice_integral L hL u) hKvol hKrank (by omega)
      let cs := c.map (perpendicularEquivSlice L u)
      have hcs : Orthonormal ℝ (fun i => (cs i : E)) := by
        rw [orthonormal_iff_ite] at hc ⊢
        intro i j
        exact hc i j
      let b := unitExtensionBasis L hL hu hu1 cs
      have hb : Orthonormal ℝ (fun i => (b i : E)) :=
        unitExtensionBasis_orthonormal L hL hu hu1 cs hcs
      let e : Unit ⊕ Fin (n - 1) ≃ Fin n := Fintype.equivOfCardEq (by simp; omega)
      refine ⟨b.reindex e, ?_⟩
      simpa only [Basis.reindex_apply, Function.comp_apply] using
        hb.comp e.symm e.symm.injective

theorem classification
    (L : Submodule ℤ E) [DiscreteTopology L] [IsZLattice ℝ L]
    (hL : Integral L) (hvol : ZLattice.covolume L = 1)
    (hr : finrank ℝ E ≤ 3) :
    ∃ b : Basis (Fin (finrank ℝ E)) ℤ L, Orthonormal ℝ (fun i => (b i : E)) :=
  classification_rank (finrank ℝ E) L hL hvol rfl hr

/-- Parseval's identity in the integer coordinates of an orthonormal
integral basis. This is the concrete norm identity used by primitive-shell
equivalences. -/
theorem orthonormal_integral_coordinates_norm
    {ι : Type*} [Fintype ι]
    (L : Submodule ℤ E) [DiscreteTopology L] [IsZLattice ℝ L]
    (b : Basis ι ℤ L) (hb : Orthonormal ℝ (fun i => (b i : E))) (x : L) :
    ∑ i, ((b.equivFun x i : ℤ) : ℝ) ^ 2 = ‖(x : E)‖ ^ 2 := by
  let bR := b.ofZLatticeBasis ℝ
  have hbR : Orthonormal ℝ (fun i => bR i) := by
    simpa only [bR, Basis.ofZLatticeBasis_apply] using hb
  let o := bR.toOrthonormalBasis hbR
  have hcoords (i : ι) : ⟪o i, (x : E)⟫ = ((b.equivFun x i : ℤ) : ℝ) := by
    rw [← o.repr_apply_apply, ← o.coe_toBasis_repr_apply]
    dsimp only [o]
    rw [Basis.toBasis_toOrthonormalBasis]
    simpa only [bR, Basis.equivFun_apply] using
      b.ofZLatticeBasis_repr_apply ℝ L x i
  simpa only [hcoords] using o.sum_sq_inner_right (x : E)

/-- A concrete integral linear equivalence to the standard integer lattice,
preserving squared Euclidean norm, for every integral unimodular lattice of
rank at most three. No isometry/classification is supplied as a hypothesis. -/
theorem exists_norm_preserving_integral_coordinates
    (L : Submodule ℤ E) [DiscreteTopology L] [IsZLattice ℝ L]
    (hL : Integral L) (hvol : ZLattice.covolume L = 1)
    (hr : finrank ℝ E ≤ 3) :
    ∃ e : L ≃ₗ[ℤ] (Fin (finrank ℝ E) → ℤ),
      ∀ x : L, ∑ i, ((e x i : ℤ) : ℝ) ^ 2 = ‖(x : E)‖ ^ 2 := by
  obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := classification L hL hvol hr
  exact ⟨b.equivFun, orthonormal_integral_coordinates_norm L b hb⟩

end SmallIntegralLattices





-- END SmallIntegralLattices.lean

-- BEGIN NeighborRealification.lean




/-!
Embed the rational coordinate model of a neighbor into the Euclidean space
used by the small-lattice theorem. The map is integral-linear and injective;
it identifies the rational dot product with the real inner product.
-/

noncomputable section

open scoped RealInnerProductSpace

namespace A354766Neighbors

abbrev RationalVector := Fin 3 → ℚ
abbrev RealTernarySpace := EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 3)

def rationalDot (v w : RationalVector) : ℚ :=
  v 0 * w 0 + v 1 * w 1 + v 2 * w 2

def rationalToReal : RationalVector →ₗ[ℤ] RealTernarySpace where
  toFun v := WithLp.toLp 2 (fun i => (v i : ℝ))
  map_add' := by
    intro v w
    ext i
    simp
  map_smul' := by
    intro n v
    ext i
    simp [Pi.smul_apply, PiLp.smul_apply, zsmul_eq_mul]

@[simp] theorem rationalToReal_apply (v : RationalVector) (i : Fin 3) :
    rationalToReal v i = (v i : ℝ) := rfl

theorem rationalToReal_injective : Function.Injective rationalToReal := by
  intro v w h
  funext i
  have hi := congrArg (fun x : RealTernarySpace => x i) h
  change (v i : ℝ) = (w i : ℝ) at hi
  exact Rat.cast_injective hi

theorem inner_rationalToReal (v w : RationalVector) :
    ⟪rationalToReal v, rationalToReal w⟫ = (rationalDot v w : ℝ) := by
  simp [rationalToReal, EuclideanSpace.inner_eq_star_dotProduct, dotProduct,
    rationalDot, Fin.sum_univ_succ, mul_comm] <;> ring

def realify (L : Submodule ℤ RationalVector) : Submodule ℤ RealTernarySpace :=
  L.map rationalToReal

def realifyMap (L : Submodule ℤ RationalVector) : L →ₗ[ℤ] realify L where
  toFun x := ⟨rationalToReal x.1, ⟨x.1, x.property, rfl⟩⟩
  map_add' := by
    intro x y
    apply Subtype.ext
    exact rationalToReal.map_add x.1 y.1
  map_smul' := by
    intro n x
    apply Subtype.ext
    exact rationalToReal.map_smul n x.1

theorem realifyMap_bijective (L : Submodule ℤ RationalVector) :
    Function.Bijective (realifyMap L) := by
  constructor
  · intro x y h
    apply Subtype.ext
    apply rationalToReal_injective
    exact congrArg (fun z : realify L => (z : RealTernarySpace)) h
  · rintro ⟨x, hx⟩
    rcases hx with ⟨v, hv, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨⟨v, hv⟩, rfl⟩

def realifyEquiv (L : Submodule ℤ RationalVector) : L ≃ₗ[ℤ] realify L :=
  LinearEquiv.ofBijective (realifyMap L) (realifyMap_bijective L)

theorem realify_integral (L : Submodule ℤ RationalVector)
    (hL : ∀ v ∈ L, ∀ w ∈ L, ∃ z : ℤ, rationalDot v w = (z : ℚ)) :
    ∀ x ∈ realify L, ∀ y ∈ realify L, ∃ z : ℤ, ⟪x, y⟫ = (z : ℝ) := by
  rintro x ⟨v, hv, rfl⟩ y ⟨w, hw, rfl⟩
  obtain ⟨z, hz⟩ := hL v hv w hw
  refine ⟨z, ?_⟩
  rw [inner_rationalToReal, hz]
  simp

end A354766Neighbors




-- END NeighborRealification.lean

-- BEGIN NeighborTopology.lean


/-!
Topological and covolume consequences of the explicit neighbor inclusions.
The quotient indices themselves are supplied by the concrete construction.
-/

noncomputable section

open MeasureTheory

namespace A354766Neighbors

section Topological

variable {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E]

theorem natReal_smul_eq_natInt_smul (p : ℕ) (x : E) :
    (p : ℝ) • x = (p : ℤ) • x := by
  simpa only [Int.cast_natCast] using (Int.cast_smul_eq_zsmul ℝ (p : ℤ) x)

theorem discrete_of_prime_multiple_inclusion
    (L N : Submodule ℤ E) [DiscreteTopology L]
    (p : ℕ) (hp : p ≠ 0)
    (hpN : ∀ x ∈ N, (p : ℤ) • x ∈ L) : DiscreteTopology N := by
  have hpR : (p : ℝ) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr hp
  let f : N → L := fun x => ⟨(p : ℝ) • (x : E), by
    rw [natReal_smul_eq_natInt_smul]
    exact hpN x.1 x.property⟩
  have hf : Continuous f := by
    exact (continuous_const.smul continuous_subtype_val).subtype_mk _
  have hinj : Function.Injective f := by
    intro x y h
    apply Subtype.ext
    have hval := congrArg (fun z : L => (z : E)) h
    change (p : ℝ) • (x : E) = (p : ℝ) • (y : E) at hval
    calc
      (x : E) = (p : ℝ)⁻¹ • ((p : ℝ) • (x : E)) := by simp [smul_smul, hpR]
      _ = (p : ℝ)⁻¹ • ((p : ℝ) • (y : E)) := by rw [hval]
      _ = (y : E) := by simp [smul_smul, hpR]
  exact DiscreteTopology.of_continuous_injective hf hinj

theorem span_top_of_prime_multiple_inclusion
    (L N : Submodule ℤ E) [DiscreteTopology L] [IsZLattice ℝ L]
    (p : ℕ) (hp : p ≠ 0)
    (hpL : ∀ x ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • x ∈ N) :
    Submodule.span ℝ (N : Set E) = ⊤ := by
  have hpR : (p : ℝ) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr hp
  let S := Submodule.span ℝ (N : Set E)
  have hsub : (L : Set E) ⊆ S := by
    intro x hx
    have hpx : (p : ℝ) • x ∈ S := by
      rw [natReal_smul_eq_natInt_smul]
      exact Submodule.subset_span (hpL x hx)
    have h := S.smul_mem ((p : ℝ)⁻¹) hpx
    simpa [smul_smul, hpR] using h
  have hspan : Submodule.span ℝ (L : Set E) ≤ S := Submodule.span_le.mpr hsub
  rw [IsZLattice.span_top (K := ℝ)] at hspan
  exact top_unique hspan

theorem isZLattice_of_prime_multiple_inclusion
    (L N : Submodule ℤ E) [DiscreteTopology L] [IsZLattice ℝ L] [DiscreteTopology N]
    (p : ℕ) (hp : p ≠ 0)
    (hpL : ∀ x ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • x ∈ N) : IsZLattice ℝ N :=
  ⟨span_top_of_prime_multiple_inclusion L N p hp hpL⟩

end Topological

section Covolume

variable {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [InnerProductSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E]
  [MeasurableSpace E] [BorelSpace E]

/-- Equal indices above a common full lattice force equal covolumes. -/
theorem covolume_eq_of_common_index
    (M L N : Submodule ℤ E)
    [DiscreteTopology M] [IsZLattice ℝ M]
    [DiscreteTopology L] [IsZLattice ℝ L]
    [DiscreteTopology N] [IsZLattice ℝ N]
    (hML : M ≤ L) (hMN : M ≤ N)
    (hindex : M.toAddSubgroup.relIndex L.toAddSubgroup =
      M.toAddSubgroup.relIndex N.toAddSubgroup) :
    ZLattice.covolume L = ZLattice.covolume N := by
  have hMLr := ZLattice.covolume_div_covolume_eq_relIndex' M L hML
  have hMNr := ZLattice.covolume_div_covolume_eq_relIndex' M N hMN
  have hratio : ZLattice.covolume M / ZLattice.covolume L =
      ZLattice.covolume M / ZLattice.covolume N := by
    rw [hMLr, hMNr, hindex]
  have hMpos := ZLattice.covolume_pos M volume
  have hLpos := ZLattice.covolume_pos L volume
  have hNpos := ZLattice.covolume_pos N volume
  have hmul := (div_eq_div_iff hLpos.ne' hNpos.ne').mp hratio
  exact (mul_left_cancel₀ hMpos.ne' hmul).symm

end Covolume
end A354766Neighbors




-- END NeighborTopology.lean

-- BEGIN NeighborConstruction.lean













/-!
# The rational p-neighbor construction for the standard ternary lattice

These are concrete integer submodules of `Fin 3 → ℚ`. No classification of
unimodular lattices, representation-count recurrence, or neighbor count is
assumed. The isotropic lift is a separate checked prerequisite.
-/

namespace A354766Research.Neighbor

abbrev IntVec := Fin 3 → ℤ
abbrev RatVec := Fin 3 → ℚ

/-- The standard bilinear form, with its three coordinates made explicit. -/
def dot3 {R : Type*} [CommRing R] (u v : Fin 3 → R) : R :=
  u 0 * v 0 + u 1 * v 1 + u 2 * v 2

theorem dot3_self_eq_sum_squares (u : IntVec) :
    dot3 u u = u 0 ^ 2 + u 1 ^ 2 + u 2 ^ 2 := by
  simp only [dot3, pow_two]

def castVec (u : IntVec) : RatVec := fun i => (u i : ℚ)

def liftVec (p : ℕ) (u : IntVec) : RatVec := fun i => (u i : ℚ) / (p : ℚ)

@[simp] theorem castVec_zero : castVec 0 = 0 := by
  funext i
  simp [castVec]

@[simp] theorem castVec_add (m n : IntVec) :
    castVec (m + n) = castVec m + castVec n := by
  funext i
  simp [castVec]

@[simp] theorem castVec_smul (a : ℤ) (m : IntVec) :
    castVec (a • m) = a • castVec m := by
  funext i
  simp [castVec, Pi.smul_apply, zsmul_eq_mul]

@[simp] theorem castVec_mul (m n : IntVec) :
    castVec (m * n) = castVec m * castVec n := by
  funext i
  simp [castVec]

@[simp] theorem castVec_intCast (a : ℤ) : castVec (a : IntVec) = (a : RatVec) := by
  funext i
  simp [castVec]

@[simp] theorem castVec_natCast (a : ℕ) : castVec (a : IntVec) = (a : RatVec) := by
  funext i
  simp [castVec]

theorem castVec_injective : Function.Injective castVec := by
  intro m n h
  funext i
  have hi := congrFun h i
  change (m i : ℚ) = (n i : ℚ) at hi
  exact_mod_cast hi

@[simp] theorem dot3_zero_right (u : IntVec) : dot3 u 0 = 0 := by
  simp [dot3]

@[simp] theorem dot3_add_right (u m n : IntVec) :
    dot3 u (m + n) = dot3 u m + dot3 u n := by
  simp only [dot3, Pi.add_apply]
  ring

@[simp] theorem dot3_smul_right (u m : IntVec) (a : ℤ) :
    dot3 u (a • m) = a * dot3 u m := by
  simp only [dot3, Pi.smul_apply, Int.zsmul_eq_mul]
  ring

/-- The standard integral lattice inside rational triples. -/
def standard : Submodule ℤ RatVec where
  carrier := {x | ∃ m : IntVec, castVec m = x}
  zero_mem' := ⟨0, castVec_zero⟩
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨m, rfl⟩ ⟨n, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨m + n, castVec_add m n⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro a x ⟨m, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨a • m, castVec_smul a m⟩

/-- The index-p candidate M, viewed in the same rational ambient space. -/
def midpoint (p : ℕ) (u : IntVec) : Submodule ℤ RatVec where
  carrier := {x | ∃ m : IntVec, (p : ℤ) ∣ dot3 u m ∧ castVec m = x}
  zero_mem' := ⟨0, by simp, castVec_zero⟩
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨m, hm, rfl⟩ ⟨n, hn, rfl⟩
    refine ⟨m + n, ?_, castVec_add m n⟩
    rw [dot3_add_right]
    exact dvd_add hm hn
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro a x ⟨m, hm, rfl⟩
    refine ⟨a • m, ?_, castVec_smul a m⟩
    rw [dot3_smul_right]
    exact dvd_mul_of_dvd_right hm a

/-- The neighbor N=M+ℤ(u/p), with a concrete additive normal form. -/
def neighbor (p : ℕ) (u : IntVec) : Submodule ℤ RatVec where
  carrier := {x | ∃ (m : IntVec) (a : ℤ),
    (p : ℤ) ∣ dot3 u m ∧ castVec m + a • liftVec p u = x}
  zero_mem' := ⟨0, 0, by simp, by simp⟩
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨m, a, hm, rfl⟩ ⟨n, b, hn, rfl⟩
    refine ⟨m + n, a + b, ?_, ?_⟩
    · rw [dot3_add_right]
      exact dvd_add hm hn
    · rw [castVec_add, add_smul]
      ac_rfl
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro a x ⟨m, b, hm, rfl⟩
    refine ⟨a • m, a * b, ?_, ?_⟩
    · rw [dot3_smul_right]
      exact dvd_mul_of_dvd_right hm a
    · rw [castVec_smul, mul_smul, smul_add]

@[simp] theorem castVec_mem_midpoint_iff (p : ℕ) (u m : IntVec) :
    castVec m ∈ midpoint p u ↔ (p : ℤ) ∣ dot3 u m := by
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨n, hn, heq⟩
    have hnm : n = m := castVec_injective heq
    simpa only [hnm] using hn
  · intro hm
    exact ⟨m, hm, rfl⟩

theorem midpoint_le_standard (p : ℕ) (u : IntVec) : midpoint p u ≤ standard := by
  rintro x ⟨m, hm, heq⟩
  exact ⟨m, heq⟩

theorem midpoint_le_neighbor (p : ℕ) (u : IntVec) : midpoint p u ≤ neighbor p u := by
  rintro x ⟨m, hm, rfl⟩
  exact ⟨m, 0, hm, by simp⟩

theorem liftVec_mem_neighbor (p : ℕ) (u : IntVec) : liftVec p u ∈ neighbor p u := by
  exact ⟨0, 1, by simp, by simp⟩

theorem p_smul_liftVec (p : ℕ) (hp0 : p ≠ 0) (u : IntVec) :
    (p : ℤ) • liftVec p u = castVec u := by
  have hpq : (p : ℚ) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr hp0
  funext i
  simp only [Pi.smul_apply, zsmul_eq_mul, liftVec, castVec, Int.cast_natCast]
  field_simp [hpq] <;> ring

/-- Multiplication by p sends the standard lattice into M. -/
theorem p_smul_standard_mem_midpoint (p : ℕ) (u : IntVec) (x : RatVec)
    (hx : x ∈ standard) : (p : ℤ) • x ∈ midpoint p u := by
  rcases hx with ⟨m, rfl⟩
  refine ⟨(p : ℤ) • m, ?_, castVec_smul (p : ℤ) m⟩
  rw [dot3_smul_right]
  exact dvd_mul_right (p : ℤ) (dot3 u m)

/-- Multiplication by p sends N into M whenever p divides Q(u). -/
theorem p_smul_neighbor_mem_midpoint (p : ℕ) (hp0 : p ≠ 0) (u : IntVec)
    (hQ : (p : ℤ) ∣ dot3 u u) (x : RatVec) (hx : x ∈ neighbor p u) :
    (p : ℤ) • x ∈ midpoint p u := by
  rcases hx with ⟨m, a, hm, rfl⟩
  refine ⟨(p : ℤ) • m + a • u, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [dot3_add_right, dot3_smul_right, dot3_smul_right]
    exact dvd_add (dvd_mul_right (p : ℤ) (dot3 u m))
      (dvd_mul_of_dvd_right hQ a)
  · calc
      castVec ((p : ℤ) • m + a • u) =
          (p : ℤ) • castVec m + a • castVec u := by simp
      _ = (p : ℤ) • castVec m + a • ((p : ℤ) • liftVec p u) := by
        rw [p_smul_liftVec p hp0 u]
      _ = (p : ℤ) • (castVec m + a • liftVec p u) := by
        rw [smul_add, smul_comm a (p : ℤ)]

private theorem dot3_neighbor_forms (p : ℕ) (u m n : IntVec) (a b : ℤ) :
    dot3 (castVec m + a • liftVec p u) (castVec n + b • liftVec p u) =
      ((dot3 m n : ℤ) : ℚ) + (a : ℚ) / (p : ℚ) * ((dot3 u n : ℤ) : ℚ) +
      (b : ℚ) / (p : ℚ) * ((dot3 u m : ℤ) : ℚ) +
      ((a : ℚ) / (p : ℚ)) * ((b : ℚ) / (p : ℚ)) * ((dot3 u u : ℤ) : ℚ) := by
  simp only [dot3, castVec, liftVec, Pi.add_apply, Pi.smul_apply, zsmul_eq_mul,
    Int.cast_add, Int.cast_mul, Pi.mul_apply, Pi.intCast_def, Pi.natCast_def]
  ring

/-- Every inner product of two vectors in N is an integer. -/
theorem neighbor_integral (p : ℕ) (hp0 : p ≠ 0) (u : IntVec)
    (hQ : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ dot3 u u) (x y : RatVec)
    (hx : x ∈ neighbor p u) (hy : y ∈ neighbor p u) :
    ∃ k : ℤ, dot3 x y = (k : ℚ) := by
  have hpq : (p : ℚ) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr hp0
  rcases hx with ⟨m, a, hm, rfl⟩
  rcases hy with ⟨n, b, hn, rfl⟩
  rcases hm with ⟨km, hkm⟩
  rcases hn with ⟨kn, hkn⟩
  rcases hQ with ⟨kQ, hkQ⟩
  have hkm' : ((dot3 u m : ℤ) : ℚ) = (p : ℚ) * (km : ℚ) := by
    exact_mod_cast hkm
  have hkn' : ((dot3 u n : ℤ) : ℚ) = (p : ℚ) * (kn : ℚ) := by
    exact_mod_cast hkn
  have hkQ' : ((dot3 u u : ℤ) : ℚ) = (p : ℚ) ^ 2 * (kQ : ℚ) := by
    exact_mod_cast hkQ
  refine ⟨dot3 m n + a * kn + b * km + a * b * kQ, ?_⟩
  rw [dot3_neighbor_forms, hkm', hkn', hkQ']
  push_cast
  field_simp [hpq] <;> ring

/-- The intersection of the standard lattice and its neighbor is exactly M.
Only primality, nonzero reduction of u, and p | Q(u) are needed here. -/
theorem standard_inf_neighbor (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (u : IntVec)
    (hu : ∃ i : Fin 3, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ u i) (hQ : (p : ℤ) ∣ dot3 u u) :
    standard ⊓ neighbor p u = midpoint p u := by
  have hpq : (p : ℚ) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr hp.ne_zero
  apply le_antisymm
  · intro x hx
    rcases hx.1 with ⟨l, hl⟩
    rcases hx.2 with ⟨m, a, hm, heq⟩
    obtain ⟨i, hui⟩ := hu
    have hcoord : (m i : ℚ) + (a : ℚ) * ((u i : ℚ) / (p : ℚ)) = (l i : ℚ) := by
      simpa only [Pi.add_apply, Pi.smul_apply, zsmul_eq_mul, castVec, liftVec]
        using congrFun (heq.trans hl.symm) i
    have hrelationQ : (p : ℚ) * ((l i : ℚ) - (m i : ℚ)) =
        (a : ℚ) * (u i : ℚ) := by
      rw [← hcoord]
      field_simp [hpq] <;> ring
    have hrelation : (p : ℤ) * (l i - m i) = a * u i := by
      exact_mod_cast hrelationQ
    have hdiv : (p : ℤ) ∣ a * u i := ⟨l i - m i, hrelation.symm⟩
    have ha : (p : ℤ) ∣ a :=
      ((Nat.prime_iff_prime_int.mp hp).dvd_or_dvd hdiv).resolve_right hui
    rcases ha with ⟨b, hb⟩
    refine ⟨m + b • u, ?_, ?_⟩
    · rw [dot3_add_right, dot3_smul_right]
      exact dvd_add hm (dvd_mul_of_dvd_right hQ b)
    · calc
        castVec (m + b • u) = castVec m + b • castVec u := by simp
        _ = castVec m + b • ((p : ℤ) • liftVec p u) := by
          rw [p_smul_liftVec p hp.ne_zero u]
        _ = castVec m + ((p : ℤ) * b) • liftVec p u := by
          rw [smul_smul, mul_comm b (p : ℤ)]
        _ = x := by rw [← hb]; exact heq
  · intro x hx
    exact ⟨midpoint_le_standard p u hx, midpoint_le_neighbor p u hx⟩

/-! The two relative indices, proved by quotient/kernel equivalences. -/

def dotMod (p : ℕ) (u : IntVec) : IntVec →ₗ[ℤ] ZMod p where
  toFun m := ((dot3 u m : ℤ) : ZMod p)
  map_add' := by
    intro m n
    simp only [dot3_add_right, Int.cast_add]
  map_smul' := by
    intro a m
    change ((dot3 u (a • m) : ℤ) : ZMod p) = a • ((dot3 u m : ℤ) : ZMod p)
    rw [dot3_smul_right]
    simp only [Int.cast_mul, zsmul_eq_mul]

@[simp] theorem dotMod_apply (p : ℕ) (u m : IntVec) :
    dotMod p u m = ((dot3 u m : ℤ) : ZMod p) := rfl

theorem dot3_single_right (u : IntVec) (i : Fin 3) (a : ℤ) :
    dot3 u (Pi.single i a) = u i * a := by
  fin_cases i <;> simp [dot3, Pi.single_apply]

theorem dotMod_surjective (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (u : IntVec)
    (hu : ∃ i : Fin 3, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ u i) : Function.Surjective (dotMod p u) := by
  letI : Fact p.Prime := ⟨hp⟩
  letI : NeZero p := ⟨hp.ne_zero⟩
  obtain ⟨i, hui⟩ := hu
  have hui' : (u i : ZMod p) ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    exact hui ((ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd (u i) p).mp h)
  intro x
  let t : ZMod p := x / (u i : ZMod p)
  refine ⟨Pi.single i (t.val : ℤ), ?_⟩
  rw [dotMod_apply, dot3_single_right]
  simp only [Int.cast_mul, Int.cast_natCast, ZMod.natCast_zmod_val]
  dsimp [t]
  field_simp [hui'] <;> ring

def castStandard : IntVec →ₗ[ℤ] standard where
  toFun m := ⟨castVec m, ⟨m, rfl⟩⟩
  map_add' := by
    intro m n
    apply Subtype.ext
    exact castVec_add m n
  map_smul' := by
    intro a m
    apply Subtype.ext
    exact castVec_smul a m

theorem castStandard_surjective : Function.Surjective castStandard := by
  rintro ⟨x, m, hm⟩
  refine ⟨m, ?_⟩
  apply Subtype.ext
  exact hm

def midpointInStandard (p : ℕ) (u : IntVec) : Submodule ℤ standard :=
  (midpoint p u).comap standard.subtype

def midpointInNeighbor (p : ℕ) (u : IntVec) : Submodule ℤ (neighbor p u) :=
  (midpoint p u).comap (neighbor p u).subtype

/-- The quotient L0/M has precisely p elements. -/
theorem card_standard_quotient_midpoint (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (u : IntVec)
    (hu : ∃ i : Fin 3, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ u i) :
    Nat.card (standard ⧸ midpointInStandard p u) = p := by
  letI : NeZero p := ⟨hp.ne_zero⟩
  let g : IntVec →ₗ[ℤ] standard ⧸ midpointInStandard p u :=
    (midpointInStandard p u).mkQ.comp castStandard
  have hg : Function.Surjective g :=
    (midpointInStandard p u).mkQ_surjective.comp castStandard_surjective
  have hker : LinearMap.ker g = LinearMap.ker (dotMod p u) := by
    ext m
    change (Submodule.Quotient.mk (castStandard m) :
      standard ⧸ midpointInStandard p u) = 0 ↔ ((dot3 u m : ℤ) : ZMod p) = 0
    rw [Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero]
    change castVec m ∈ midpoint p u ↔ ((dot3 u m : ℤ) : ZMod p) = 0
    rw [castVec_mem_midpoint_iff, ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd]
  calc
    Nat.card (standard ⧸ midpointInStandard p u) = Nat.card (IntVec ⧸ LinearMap.ker g) :=
      (Nat.card_congr (g.quotKerEquivOfSurjective hg).toEquiv).symm
    _ = Nat.card (IntVec ⧸ LinearMap.ker (dotMod p u)) :=
      Nat.card_congr (Submodule.quotEquivOfEq _ _ hker).toEquiv
    _ = Nat.card (ZMod p) :=
      Nat.card_congr ((dotMod p u).quotKerEquivOfSurjective
        (dotMod_surjective p hp u hu)).toEquiv
    _ = p := by simp [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, ZMod.card]

/-- The coefficient of u/p lies in M exactly when it is divisible by p. -/
theorem smul_liftVec_mem_midpoint_iff (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (u : IntVec)
    (hu : ∃ i : Fin 3, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ u i) (hQ : (p : ℤ) ∣ dot3 u u) (a : ℤ) :
    a • liftVec p u ∈ midpoint p u ↔ (p : ℤ) ∣ a := by
  have hpq : (p : ℚ) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr hp.ne_zero
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨m, hm, heq⟩
    obtain ⟨i, hui⟩ := hu
    have hi : (m i : ℚ) = (a : ℚ) * ((u i : ℚ) / (p : ℚ)) := by
      simpa only [castVec, liftVec, Pi.smul_apply, zsmul_eq_mul] using congrFun heq i
    have hrQ : (p : ℚ) * (m i : ℚ) = (a : ℚ) * (u i : ℚ) := by
      rw [hi]
      field_simp [hpq] <;> ring
    have hr : (p : ℤ) * m i = a * u i := by exact_mod_cast hrQ
    have hd : (p : ℤ) ∣ a * u i := ⟨m i, hr.symm⟩
    exact ((Nat.prime_iff_prime_int.mp hp).dvd_or_dvd hd).resolve_right hui
  · rintro ⟨b, rfl⟩
    refine ⟨b • u, ?_, ?_⟩
    · rw [dot3_smul_right]
      exact dvd_mul_of_dvd_right hQ b
    · calc
        castVec (b • u) = b • castVec u := castVec_smul b u
        _ = b • ((p : ℤ) • liftVec p u) := by rw [p_smul_liftVec p hp.ne_zero u]
        _ = ((p : ℤ) * b) • liftVec p u := by rw [smul_smul, mul_comm b (p : ℤ)]

def neighborGenerator (p : ℕ) (u : IntVec) : ℤ →ₗ[ℤ] neighbor p u :=
  LinearMap.toSpanSingleton ℤ (neighbor p u) ⟨liftVec p u, liftVec_mem_neighbor p u⟩

def intCastMod (p : ℕ) : ℤ →ₗ[ℤ] ZMod p where
  toFun a := (a : ZMod p)
  map_add' := by intro a b; simp
  map_smul' := by intro a b; simp [Int.zsmul_eq_mul, zsmul_eq_mul]

theorem intCastMod_surjective (p : ℕ) (hp0 : p ≠ 0) :
    Function.Surjective (intCastMod p) := by
  letI : NeZero p := ⟨hp0⟩
  intro x
  exact ⟨(x.val : ℤ), by simp [intCastMod, ZMod.natCast_zmod_val]⟩

/-- The quotient N/M has precisely p elements. -/
theorem card_neighbor_quotient_midpoint (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (u : IntVec)
    (hu : ∃ i : Fin 3, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ u i) (hQ : (p : ℤ) ∣ dot3 u u) :
    Nat.card ((neighbor p u) ⧸ midpointInNeighbor p u) = p := by
  letI : NeZero p := ⟨hp.ne_zero⟩
  let g : ℤ →ₗ[ℤ] (neighbor p u) ⧸ midpointInNeighbor p u :=
    (midpointInNeighbor p u).mkQ.comp (neighborGenerator p u)
  have hg : Function.Surjective g := by
    intro y
    obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := (midpointInNeighbor p u).mkQ_surjective y
    rcases x.property with ⟨m, a, hm, heq⟩
    refine ⟨a, ?_⟩
    change (Submodule.Quotient.mk (neighborGenerator p u a) :
      (neighbor p u) ⧸ midpointInNeighbor p u) = Submodule.Quotient.mk x
    rw [Submodule.Quotient.eq]
    change a • liftVec p u - (x : RatVec) ∈ midpoint p u
    have heq' : a • liftVec p u - (x : RatVec) = -castVec m := by
      rw [← heq]
      abel
    rw [heq']
    exact (midpoint p u).neg_mem ⟨m, hm, rfl⟩
  have hker : LinearMap.ker g = LinearMap.ker (intCastMod p) := by
    ext a
    change (Submodule.Quotient.mk (neighborGenerator p u a) :
      (neighbor p u) ⧸ midpointInNeighbor p u) = 0 ↔ (a : ZMod p) = 0
    rw [Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero, ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd]
    change a • liftVec p u ∈ midpoint p u ↔ (p : ℤ) ∣ a
    exact smul_liftVec_mem_midpoint_iff p hp u hu hQ a
  calc
    Nat.card ((neighbor p u) ⧸ midpointInNeighbor p u) =
        Nat.card (ℤ ⧸ LinearMap.ker g) :=
      (Nat.card_congr (g.quotKerEquivOfSurjective hg).toEquiv).symm
    _ = Nat.card (ℤ ⧸ LinearMap.ker (intCastMod p)) :=
      Nat.card_congr (Submodule.quotEquivOfEq _ _ hker).toEquiv
    _ = Nat.card (ZMod p) :=
      Nat.card_congr ((intCastMod p).quotKerEquivOfSurjective
        (intCastMod_surjective p hp.ne_zero)).toEquiv
    _ = p := by simp [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, ZMod.card]

theorem midpoint_relIndex_standard (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (u : IntVec)
    (hu : ∃ i : Fin 3, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ u i) :
    (midpoint p u).toAddSubgroup.relIndex standard.toAddSubgroup = p := by
  change Nat.card (standard ⧸ midpointInStandard p u) = p
  exact card_standard_quotient_midpoint p hp u hu

theorem midpoint_relIndex_neighbor (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (u : IntVec)
    (hu : ∃ i : Fin 3, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ u i) (hQ : (p : ℤ) ∣ dot3 u u) :
    (midpoint p u).toAddSubgroup.relIndex (neighbor p u).toAddSubgroup = p := by
  change Nat.card ((neighbor p u) ⧸ midpointInNeighbor p u) = p
  exact card_neighbor_quotient_midpoint p hp u hu hQ

/-! The exterior-vector membership criterion, before projective quotients. -/

theorem dot3_isotropic_decomposition (p : ℕ) (u m : IntVec) (a : ℤ) :
    dot3 (a • u + (p : ℤ) • m) (a • u + (p : ℤ) • m) =
      a ^ 2 * dot3 u u + 2 * a * (p : ℤ) * dot3 u m +
      (p : ℤ) ^ 2 * dot3 m m := by
  simp only [dot3, Pi.add_apply, Pi.smul_apply, Int.zsmul_eq_mul]
  ring

/-- Isotropy modulo p² forces the correction m into the perpendicular kernel. -/
theorem midpoint_condition_of_isotropic_decomposition
    (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (u m : IntVec) (a : ℤ)
    (ha : ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ a) (hQu : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ dot3 u u)
    (hQv : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣
      dot3 (a • u + (p : ℤ) • m) (a • u + (p : ℤ) • m)) :
    (p : ℤ) ∣ dot3 u m := by
  have hpz : (p : ℤ) ≠ 0 := Int.natCast_ne_zero.mpr hp.ne_zero
  have hprime : Prime (p : ℤ) := Nat.prime_iff_prime_int.mp hp
  have h2a : ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ 2 * a := by
    intro h
    rcases hprime.dvd_or_dvd h with htwo | ha'
    · have htwo' : p ∣ 2 := Int.natCast_dvd_natCast.mp htwo
      exact hp2 (Nat.le_antisymm (Nat.le_of_dvd (by decide) htwo') hp.two_le)
    · exact ha ha'
  rcases hQu with ⟨ku, hku⟩
  rcases hQv with ⟨kv, hkv⟩
  have hEq : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 * kv =
      a ^ 2 * ((p : ℤ) ^ 2 * ku) + 2 * a * (p : ℤ) * dot3 u m +
        (p : ℤ) ^ 2 * dot3 m m := by
    rw [← hkv, dot3_isotropic_decomposition, hku]
  have hcancel : (p : ℤ) * (2 * a * dot3 u m) =
      (p : ℤ) * ((p : ℤ) * (kv - a ^ 2 * ku - dot3 m m)) := by
    calc
      (p : ℤ) * (2 * a * dot3 u m) =
          (a ^ 2 * ((p : ℤ) ^ 2 * ku) + 2 * a * (p : ℤ) * dot3 u m +
            (p : ℤ) ^ 2 * dot3 m m) - a ^ 2 * ((p : ℤ) ^ 2 * ku) -
              (p : ℤ) ^ 2 * dot3 m m := by ring
      _ = (p : ℤ) ^ 2 * kv - a ^ 2 * ((p : ℤ) ^ 2 * ku) -
          (p : ℤ) ^ 2 * dot3 m m := by rw [← hEq]
      _ = (p : ℤ) * ((p : ℤ) * (kv - a ^ 2 * ku - dot3 m m)) := by ring
  have hdiv : (p : ℤ) ∣ 2 * a * dot3 u m :=
    ⟨kv - a ^ 2 * ku - dot3 m m, mul_left_cancel₀ hpz hcancel⟩
  exact (hprime.dvd_or_dvd hdiv).resolve_left h2a

theorem p_smul_normal_form (p : ℕ) (hp0 : p ≠ 0) (u m : IntVec) (a : ℤ) :
    (p : ℤ) • (castVec m + a • liftVec p u) =
      castVec ((p : ℤ) • m + a • u) := by
  calc
    (p : ℤ) • (castVec m + a • liftVec p u) =
        (p : ℤ) • castVec m + a • ((p : ℤ) • liftVec p u) := by
      rw [smul_add, smul_comm (p : ℤ) a]
    _ = (p : ℤ) • castVec m + a • castVec u := by rw [p_smul_liftVec p hp0 u]
    _ = castVec ((p : ℤ) • m + a • u) := by simp

theorem divided_decomposition_mem_neighbor (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (u m : IntVec) (a : ℤ) (ha : ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ a)
    (hQu : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ dot3 u u)
    (hQv : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣
      dot3 (a • u + (p : ℤ) • m) (a • u + (p : ℤ) • m)) :
    liftVec p (a • u + (p : ℤ) • m) ∈ neighbor p u := by
  have hpq : (p : ℚ) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr hp.ne_zero
  refine ⟨m, a, midpoint_condition_of_isotropic_decomposition p hp hp2 u m a ha hQu hQv, ?_⟩
  funext i
  simp only [castVec, liftVec, Pi.add_apply, Pi.smul_apply, zsmul_eq_mul,
    Int.cast_add, Int.cast_mul, Int.cast_natCast, Pi.mul_apply, Pi.intCast_def, Pi.natCast_def]
  push_cast
  field_simp [hpq] <;> ring

/-- The concrete normal form of v/p yields v=a*u+p*m over the integers. -/
theorem decomposition_of_divided_mem_neighbor (p : ℕ) (hp0 : p ≠ 0)
    (u v : IntVec) (hv : liftVec p v ∈ neighbor p u) :
    ∃ (a : ℤ) (m : IntVec), (p : ℤ) ∣ dot3 u m ∧ v = a • u + (p : ℤ) • m := by
  rcases hv with ⟨m, a, hm, heq⟩
  have hcasts : castVec ((p : ℤ) • m + a • u) = castVec v := by
    calc
      castVec ((p : ℤ) • m + a • u) =
          (p : ℤ) • (castVec m + a • liftVec p u) := (p_smul_normal_form p hp0 u m a).symm
      _ = (p : ℤ) • liftVec p v := congrArg (fun x : RatVec => (p : ℤ) • x) heq
      _ = castVec v := p_smul_liftVec p hp0 v
  have hInt := castVec_injective hcasts
  refine ⟨a, m, hm, ?_⟩
  rw [add_comm] at hInt
  exact hInt.symm

/-- Exterior vectors belong to the neighbor precisely when their nonzero
reductions lie on the line spanned by the chosen normalized u. -/
theorem exterior_neighbor_membership_iff
    (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (u v : IntVec)
    (hv : ∃ i : Fin 3, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ v i)
    (hQu : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ dot3 u u) (hQv : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ dot3 v v) :
    liftVec p v ∈ neighbor p u ↔
      ∃ a : ℤ, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ a ∧ ∀ i : Fin 3, (p : ℤ) ∣ v i - a * u i := by
  constructor
  · intro hmem
    obtain ⟨a, m, hm, hveq⟩ := decomposition_of_divided_mem_neighbor p hp.ne_zero u v hmem
    have ha : ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ a := by
      rintro ⟨b, hb⟩
      obtain ⟨i, hvi⟩ := hv
      apply hvi
      refine ⟨b * u i + m i, ?_⟩
      have hi := congrFun hveq i
      simp only [Pi.add_apply, Pi.smul_apply, Int.zsmul_eq_mul] at hi
      rw [hi, hb]
      ring
    refine ⟨a, ha, ?_⟩
    intro i
    refine ⟨m i, ?_⟩
    have hi := congrFun hveq i
    simp only [Pi.add_apply, Pi.smul_apply, Int.zsmul_eq_mul] at hi
    rw [hi]
    ring
  · rintro ⟨a, ha, hcong⟩
    let m : IntVec := fun i => (hcong i).choose
    have hm (i : Fin 3) : v i - a * u i = (p : ℤ) * m i := (hcong i).choose_spec
    have hveq : v = a • u + (p : ℤ) • m := by
      funext i
      change v i = a * u i + (p : ℤ) * m i
      rw [← hm i]
      ring
    have hQv' : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣
        dot3 (a • u + (p : ℤ) • m) (a • u + (p : ℤ) • m) := by
      rw [← hveq]
      exact hQv
    rw [hveq]
    exact divided_decomposition_mem_neighbor p hp hp2 u m a ha hQu hQv'

end A354766Research.Neighbor

















-- END NeighborConstruction.lean

-- BEGIN StandardRealLattice.lean





/-!
The standard rational lattice really becomes the standard Euclidean integer
lattice. Its covolume is proved to be one, rather than supplied as a hypothesis.
The actual index-p neighbor construction then has covolume one and admits the
integral norm-preserving coordinates furnished by small-lattice classification.
-/

noncomputable section

open Module MeasureTheory Set
open scoped RealInnerProductSpace

namespace A354766Neighbors

open A354766Research.Neighbor

theorem realify_standard_eq_zspan :
    realify standard = Submodule.span ℤ
      (Set.range (EuclideanSpace.basisFun (Fin 3) ℝ).toBasis) := by
  ext x
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨v, ⟨m, rfl⟩, rfl⟩
    apply ((EuclideanSpace.basisFun (Fin 3) ℝ).toBasis.mem_span_iff_repr_mem ℤ _).mpr
    intro i
    refine ⟨m i, ?_⟩
    simp [castVec, OrthonormalBasis.coe_toBasis_repr_apply]
  · intro hx
    have hcoords :=
      ((EuclideanSpace.basisFun (Fin 3) ℝ).toBasis.mem_span_iff_repr_mem ℤ x).mp hx
    choose m hm using hcoords
    refine ⟨castVec m, ⟨m, rfl⟩, ?_⟩
    ext i
    simpa [castVec, OrthonormalBasis.coe_toBasis_repr_apply] using hm i

instance realify_standard_discrete : DiscreteTopology (realify standard) := by
  rw [realify_standard_eq_zspan]
  infer_instance

instance realify_standard_isZLattice : IsZLattice ℝ (realify standard) := by
  constructor
  rw [realify_standard_eq_zspan]
  exact ZSpan.span_top (EuclideanSpace.basisFun (Fin 3) ℝ).toBasis

theorem realify_standard_covolume_one : ZLattice.covolume (realify standard) = 1 := by
  rw [realify_standard_eq_zspan]
  let b := (EuclideanSpace.basisFun (Fin 3) ℝ).toBasis
  have hfund := ZSpan.isAddFundamentalDomain b volume
  rw [ZLattice.covolume_eq_measure_fundamentalDomain _ volume hfund,
    measureReal_congr (ZSpan.fundamentalDomain_ae_parallelepiped b volume), measureReal_def]
  change (volume (parallelepiped (EuclideanSpace.basisFun (Fin 3) ℝ))).toReal = 1
  rw [(EuclideanSpace.basisFun (Fin 3) ℝ).volume_parallelepiped]
  rfl

theorem realify_mono {L N : Submodule ℤ RationalVector} (h : L ≤ N) :
    realify L ≤ realify N := by
  rintro x ⟨v, hv, rfl⟩
  exact ⟨v, h hv, rfl⟩

theorem realify_smul_inclusion (L N : Submodule ℤ RationalVector) (p : ℕ)
    (h : ∀ x ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • x ∈ N) :
    ∀ x ∈ realify L, (p : ℤ) • x ∈ realify N := by
  rintro x ⟨v, hv, rfl⟩
  exact ⟨(p : ℤ) • v, h v hv, rationalToReal.map_smul (p : ℤ) v⟩

theorem realify_relIndex (M L : Submodule ℤ RationalVector) :
    (realify M).toAddSubgroup.relIndex (realify L).toAddSubgroup =
      M.toAddSubgroup.relIndex L.toAddSubgroup := by
  change (M.toAddSubgroup.map rationalToReal.toAddMonoidHom).relIndex
    (L.toAddSubgroup.map rationalToReal.toAddMonoidHom) = _
  exact AddSubgroup.relIndex_map_map_of_injective M.toAddSubgroup L.toAddSubgroup
    rationalToReal_injective

theorem realified_neighbor_geometry
    (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (u : IntVec)
    (hu : ∃ i : Fin 3, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ u i)
    (hQ : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ dot3 u u) :
    ∃ hdisc : DiscreteTopology (realify (neighbor p u)),
      letI : DiscreteTopology (realify (neighbor p u)) := hdisc
      IsZLattice ℝ (realify (neighbor p u)) ∧
        SmallIntegralLattices.Integral (realify (neighbor p u)) ∧
        ZLattice.covolume (realify (neighbor p u)) = 1 := by
  let L := realify standard
  let M := realify (midpoint p u)
  let N := realify (neighbor p u)
  have hQ' : (p : ℤ) ∣ dot3 u u := by
    apply dvd_trans (dvd_mul_right (p : ℤ) (p : ℤ))
    simpa only [pow_two] using hQ
  have hML : M ≤ L := realify_mono (midpoint_le_standard p u)
  have hMN : M ≤ N := realify_mono (midpoint_le_neighbor p u)
  have hpLM : ∀ x ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • x ∈ M :=
    realify_smul_inclusion standard (midpoint p u) p
      (p_smul_standard_mem_midpoint p u)
  have hpNM : ∀ x ∈ N, (p : ℤ) • x ∈ M :=
    realify_smul_inclusion (neighbor p u) (midpoint p u) p
      (p_smul_neighbor_mem_midpoint p hp.ne_zero u hQ')
  letI : DiscreteTopology M := discrete_of_prime_multiple_inclusion L M 1 (by decide)
    (fun x hx => by simpa only [Nat.cast_one, one_smul] using hML hx)
  letI : IsZLattice ℝ M := isZLattice_of_prime_multiple_inclusion L M p hp.ne_zero hpLM
  letI : DiscreteTopology N := discrete_of_prime_multiple_inclusion L N p hp.ne_zero
    (fun x hx => hML (hpNM x hx))
  letI : IsZLattice ℝ N := isZLattice_of_prime_multiple_inclusion L N p hp.ne_zero
    (fun x hx => hMN (hpLM x hx))
  have hindex : M.toAddSubgroup.relIndex L.toAddSubgroup =
      M.toAddSubgroup.relIndex N.toAddSubgroup := by
    dsimp only [M, L, N]
    rw [realify_relIndex, realify_relIndex, midpoint_relIndex_standard p hp u hu,
      midpoint_relIndex_neighbor p hp u hu hQ']
  have hvol : ZLattice.covolume N = 1 :=
    (covolume_eq_of_common_index M L N hML hMN hindex).symm.trans
      realify_standard_covolume_one
  have hint : SmallIntegralLattices.Integral N := by
    apply realify_integral
    intro x hx y hy
    exact neighbor_integral p hp.ne_zero u hQ x y hx hy
  exact ⟨inferInstance, inferInstance, hint, hvol⟩

/-- Each concrete normalized p-neighbor is integrally isometric to the
standard ternary lattice; the conclusion includes the explicit quadratic
form identity needed to compare primitive representation counts. -/
theorem neighbor_integral_coordinates
    (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (u : IntVec)
    (hu : ∃ i : Fin 3, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ u i)
    (hQ : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ dot3 u u) :
    ∃ e : neighbor p u ≃ₗ[ℤ] (Fin 3 → ℤ),
      ∀ x : neighbor p u,
        ∑ i, ((e x i : ℤ) : ℝ) ^ 2 = ((dot3 (x : RatVec) (x : RatVec) : ℚ) : ℝ) := by
  obtain ⟨hdisc, hlat, hint, hvol⟩ := realified_neighbor_geometry p hp u hu hQ
  letI := hdisc
  letI := hlat
  obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := SmallIntegralLattices.classification_rank 3
    (realify (neighbor p u)) hint hvol (by simp [RealTernarySpace]) (by decide)
  let eR : realify (neighbor p u) ≃ₗ[ℤ] (Fin 3 → ℤ) := b.equivFun
  have heR (x : realify (neighbor p u)) :
      ∑ i, ((eR x i : ℤ) : ℝ) ^ 2 = ‖(x : RealTernarySpace)‖ ^ 2 :=
    SmallIntegralLattices.orthonormal_integral_coordinates_norm _ b hb x
  refine ⟨(realifyEquiv (neighbor p u)).trans eR, ?_⟩
  intro x
  rw [LinearEquiv.trans_apply, heR]
  change ‖rationalToReal (x : RatVec)‖ ^ 2 = ((dot3 (x : RatVec) (x : RatVec) : ℚ) : ℝ)
  rw [← real_inner_self_eq_norm_sq, inner_rationalToReal]
  rfl

end A354766Neighbors




-- END StandardRealLattice.lean

-- BEGIN PrimitiveTransport.lean




/-!
Primitivity transport between submodules whose intersection contains their
prime multiples. These are algebraic components of the neighbor construction;
no representation-count recurrence or lattice classification is assumed.
-/

namespace A354766Neighbors

variable {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V]

/-- Bézout descent into an integer submodule. -/
theorem mem_of_coprime_smul_mem (M : Submodule ℤ V) (x : V)
    (p q : ℕ) (hpq : p.Coprime q)
    (hp : (p : ℤ) • x ∈ M) (hq : (q : ℤ) • x ∈ M) : x ∈ M := by
  have hbez : (1 : ℤ) = (p : ℤ) * Nat.gcdA p q + (q : ℤ) * Nat.gcdB p q := by
    simpa only [hpq.gcd_eq_one, Nat.cast_one] using Nat.gcd_eq_gcd_ab p q
  have hsum : ((p : ℤ) * Nat.gcdA p q + (q : ℤ) * Nat.gcdB p q) • x ∈ M := by
    rw [add_smul, mul_smul, mul_smul,
      smul_comm (p : ℤ) (Nat.gcdA p q) x,
      smul_comm (q : ℤ) (Nat.gcdB p q) x]
    exact M.add_mem (M.smul_mem _ hp) (M.smul_mem _ hq)
  rwa [← hbez, one_smul] at hsum

/-- Divisibility inside the indicated lattice/submodule. -/
def DivisibleIn (L : Submodule ℤ V) (q : ℕ) (x : V) : Prop :=
  ∃ y ∈ L, (q : ℤ) • y = x

/-- Multiplication by a coprime integer preserves divisibility in a lattice. -/
theorem divisibleIn_smul_iff (L : Submodule ℤ V) (y : V) (hy : y ∈ L)
    (p q : ℕ) (hpq : p.Coprime q) :
    DivisibleIn L q ((p : ℤ) • y) ↔ DivisibleIn L q y := by
  have hbez : (Nat.gcdA p q) * (p : ℤ) + (Nat.gcdB p q) * (q : ℤ) = 1 := by
    simpa only [hpq.gcd_eq_one, Nat.cast_one, mul_comm] using
      (Nat.gcd_eq_gcd_ab p q).symm
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨z, hz, hqz⟩
    refine ⟨Nat.gcdA p q • z + Nat.gcdB p q • y,
      L.add_mem (L.smul_mem _ hz) (L.smul_mem _ hy), ?_⟩
    calc
      (q : ℤ) • (Nat.gcdA p q • z + Nat.gcdB p q • y) =
          Nat.gcdA p q • ((q : ℤ) • z) + Nat.gcdB p q • ((q : ℤ) • y) := by
        rw [smul_add, smul_comm (q : ℤ) (Nat.gcdA p q) z,
          smul_comm (q : ℤ) (Nat.gcdB p q) y]
      _ = Nat.gcdA p q • ((p : ℤ) • y) + Nat.gcdB p q • ((q : ℤ) • y) := by
        rw [hqz]
      _ = (Nat.gcdA p q * (p : ℤ) + Nat.gcdB p q * (q : ℤ)) • y := by
        rw [add_smul, mul_smul, mul_smul]
      _ = y := by rw [hbez, one_smul]
  · rintro ⟨z, hz, hqz⟩
    refine ⟨(p : ℤ) • z, L.smul_mem _ hz, ?_⟩
    rw [smul_comm (q : ℤ) (p : ℤ) z, hqz]

/-- At a prime away from `p`, an element of the common submodule has the
same divisibility in both lattices. The hypotheses are the explicit
inclusions that the neighbor construction must prove. -/
theorem divisibleIn_iff_of_prime_multiple_inclusions
    (L N M : Submodule ℤ V) (p q : ℕ) (hpq : p.Coprime q)
    (hML : M ≤ L) (hMN : M ≤ N)
    (hpL : ∀ y ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • y ∈ M)
    (hpN : ∀ y ∈ N, (p : ℤ) • y ∈ M)
    (x : V) (hx : x ∈ M) :
    DivisibleIn L q x ↔ DivisibleIn N q x := by
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨y, hy, hqy⟩
    have hqyM : (q : ℤ) • y ∈ M := by rwa [hqy]
    have hyM := mem_of_coprime_smul_mem M y p q hpq (hpL y hy) hqyM
    exact ⟨y, hMN hyM, hqy⟩
  · rintro ⟨y, hy, hqy⟩
    have hqyM : (q : ℤ) • y ∈ M := by rwa [hqy]
    have hyM := mem_of_coprime_smul_mem M y p q hpq (hpN y hy) hqyM
    exact ⟨y, hML hyM, hqy⟩

def PrimitiveAway (L : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (x : V) : Prop :=
  ∀ q : ℕ, q.Prime → q ≠ p → ¬ DivisibleIn L q x

theorem primitiveAway_iff_of_prime_multiple_inclusions
    (L N M : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hML : M ≤ L) (hMN : M ≤ N)
    (hpL : ∀ y ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • y ∈ M)
    (hpN : ∀ y ∈ N, (p : ℤ) • y ∈ M)
    (x : V) (hx : x ∈ M) :
    PrimitiveAway L p x ↔ PrimitiveAway N p x := by
  have hcop (q : ℕ) (hq : q.Prime) (hne : q ≠ p) : p.Coprime q := by
    apply hp.coprime_iff_not_dvd.mpr
    intro hd
    exact hne ((Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq hp hq).mp hd).symm
  constructor
  · intro h q hq hne hdiv
    apply h q hq hne
    exact (divisibleIn_iff_of_prime_multiple_inclusions
      L N M p q (hcop q hq hne) hML hMN hpL hpN x hx).mpr hdiv
  · intro h q hq hne hdiv
    apply h q hq hne
    exact (divisibleIn_iff_of_prime_multiple_inclusions
      L N M p q (hcop q hq hne) hML hMN hpL hpN x hx).mp hdiv

/-- Away from `p`, multiplication carries neighbor vectors to standard
lattice vectors without changing primitivity. -/
theorem primitiveAway_scaled_iff
    (L N M : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hML : M ≤ L) (hMN : M ≤ N)
    (hpL : ∀ y ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • y ∈ M)
    (hpN : ∀ y ∈ N, (p : ℤ) • y ∈ M)
    (y : V) (hy : y ∈ N) :
    PrimitiveAway L p ((p : ℤ) • y) ↔ PrimitiveAway N p y := by
  have hdiv (q : ℕ) (hq : q.Prime) (hne : q ≠ p) :
      DivisibleIn L q ((p : ℤ) • y) ↔ DivisibleIn N q y := by
    have hpq : p.Coprime q := by
      apply hp.coprime_iff_not_dvd.mpr
      intro hd
      exact hne ((Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq hp hq).mp hd).symm
    exact (divisibleIn_iff_of_prime_multiple_inclusions
      L N M p q hpq hML hMN hpL hpN ((p : ℤ) • y) (hpN y hy)).trans
      (divisibleIn_smul_iff N y hy p q hpq)
  constructor
  · intro h q hq hne hd
    exact h q hq hne ((hdiv q hq hne).mpr hd)
  · intro h q hq hne hd
    exact h q hq hne ((hdiv q hq hne).mp hd)

/-- A vector outside the standard lattice cannot have a `p`-th part in
the neighbor when all neighbor `p`-multiples lie in the standard lattice. -/
theorem not_divisibleIn_of_outside
    (L N : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ)
    (hpN : ∀ z ∈ N, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ L)
    (y : V) (hy : y ∉ L) : ¬ DivisibleIn N p y := by
  rintro ⟨z, hz, hqz⟩
  apply hy
  rw [← hqz]
  exact hpN z hz

/-- The inside vectors with at least two powers of `p` in their content
cannot be primitive in any neighbor containing the standard `p`-multiples. -/
theorem divisibleIn_pow_of_two_le
    (L N : Submodule ℤ V) (p a : ℕ) (ha : 2 ≤ a)
    (hpL : ∀ z ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ N)
    (z : V) (hz : z ∈ L) : DivisibleIn N p (((p : ℤ) ^ a) • z) := by
  refine ⟨(p : ℤ) • (((p : ℤ) ^ (a - 2)) • z),
    hpL _ (L.smul_mem _ hz), ?_⟩
  calc
    (p : ℤ) • ((p : ℤ) • (((p : ℤ) ^ (a - 2)) • z)) =
        (((p : ℤ) * p) * (p : ℤ) ^ (a - 2)) • z := by
      simp only [smul_smul, mul_assoc]
    _ = ((p : ℤ) ^ (a - 2) * ((p : ℤ) * p)) • z := by rw [mul_comm]
    _ = ((p : ℤ) ^ ((a - 2) + 2)) • z := by rw [pow_add, pow_two]
    _ = ((p : ℤ) ^ a) • z := by rw [Nat.sub_add_cancel ha]

/-- In a torsion-free ambient module, a standard vector of content exactly
`p` fails primitivity in a neighbor exactly when its quotient lies there. -/
theorem divisibleIn_smul_iff_mem
    (N : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ)
    (hcancel : Function.Injective (fun x : V => (p : ℤ) • x)) (z : V) :
    DivisibleIn N p ((p : ℤ) • z) ↔ z ∈ N := by
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨w, hw, h⟩
    have hwz : w = z := hcancel h
    simpa only [hwz] using hw
  · intro hz
    exact ⟨z, hz, rfl⟩

end A354766Neighbors








-- END PrimitiveTransport.lean

-- BEGIN PrimitiveVectorModel.lean




/-!
The exact connection between primitive three-square counts in the recovered
integer-triple model and the coordinate-vector model used for neighbors.
-/

namespace A354766Neighbors

abbrev IntegerVector := Fin 3 → ℤ

def vectorContent (v : IntegerVector) : ℕ :=
  Nat.gcd (v 0).natAbs (Nat.gcd (v 1).natAbs (v 2).natAbs)

def vectorNorm (v : IntegerVector) : ℤ := (v 0) ^ 2 + (v 1) ^ 2 + (v 2) ^ 2

def tripleVectorEquiv : A354766.Triple ≃ IntegerVector where
  toFun u := ![u.1, u.2.1, u.2.2]
  invFun v := (v 0, v 1, v 2)
  left_inv := by rintro ⟨a, b, c⟩; rfl
  right_inv := by
    intro v
    funext i
    fin_cases i <;> rfl

theorem vectorContent_eq_one_iff (v : IntegerVector) :
    vectorContent v = 1 ↔
      ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → ¬ ((p : ℤ) ∣ v 0 ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ v 1 ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ v 2) := by
  rw [Nat.eq_one_iff_not_exists_prime_dvd]
  simp only [vectorContent, Nat.dvd_gcd_iff, Int.natCast_dvd]

theorem scalar_divisibility_iff_coordinates (p : ℕ) (v : IntegerVector) :
    (∃ w : IntegerVector, (p : ℤ) • w = v) ↔
      (p : ℤ) ∣ v 0 ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ v 1 ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ v 2 := by
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨w, hw⟩
    refine ⟨⟨w 0, ?_⟩, ⟨w 1, ?_⟩, ⟨w 2, ?_⟩⟩
    · simpa only [Pi.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul] using (congrFun hw 0).symm
    · simpa only [Pi.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul] using (congrFun hw 1).symm
    · simpa only [Pi.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul] using (congrFun hw 2).symm
  · rintro ⟨⟨a, ha⟩, ⟨b, hb⟩, ⟨c, hc⟩⟩
    refine ⟨![a, b, c], ?_⟩
    funext i
    fin_cases i <;> simp [Pi.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, ha, hb, hc]

theorem vectorContent_eq_one_iff_primitive (v : IntegerVector) :
    vectorContent v = 1 ↔
      ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → ¬ ∃ w : IntegerVector, (p : ℤ) • w = v := by
  rw [vectorContent_eq_one_iff]
  simp only [scalar_divisibility_iff_coordinates]

def PrimitiveVectorShell (N : ℕ) :=
  {v : IntegerVector // vectorNorm v = (N : ℤ) ∧ vectorContent v = 1}

def primitiveVectorShellEquiv (N : ℕ) :
    PrimitiveVectorShell N ≃ {u : A354766.Triple // u ∈ A354766.primitiveThreeSquareFinset N} where
  toFun v := ⟨tripleVectorEquiv.symm v.1, by
    apply A354766.mem_primitiveThreeSquareFinset.mpr
    exact v.property⟩
  invFun u := ⟨tripleVectorEquiv u.1, by
    have h := A354766.mem_primitiveThreeSquareFinset.mp u.property
    exact h⟩
  left_inv := by
    intro v
    apply Subtype.ext
    exact tripleVectorEquiv.apply_symm_apply v.1
  right_inv := by
    intro u
    apply Subtype.ext
    exact tripleVectorEquiv.symm_apply_apply u.1

instance (N : ℕ) : Finite (PrimitiveVectorShell N) :=
  Finite.of_equiv {u : A354766.Triple // u ∈ A354766.primitiveThreeSquareFinset N}
    (primitiveVectorShellEquiv N).symm

theorem primitiveVectorShell_card (N : ℕ) :
    Nat.card (PrimitiveVectorShell N) = A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite N := by
  rw [Nat.card_congr (primitiveVectorShellEquiv N)]
  simp only [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, Fintype.card_coe, A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite]

end A354766Neighbors



-- END PrimitiveVectorModel.lean

-- BEGIN ShellEquivalence.lean



/-!
Integral linear equivalences preserve primitivity and give genuine
equivalences of primitive shells whenever they preserve the indicated norm.
-/

namespace A354766Neighbors

variable {U W : Type*} [AddCommGroup U] [AddCommGroup W]

def PrimitiveElement (x : U) : Prop :=
  ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → ¬ ∃ y : U, (p : ℤ) • y = x

theorem linearEquiv_scalar_divisibility (e : U ≃ₗ[ℤ] W) (p : ℕ) (x : U) :
    (∃ y : U, (p : ℤ) • y = x) ↔ ∃ y : W, (p : ℤ) • y = e x := by
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨y, hy⟩
    refine ⟨e y, ?_⟩
    rw [← e.map_smul, hy]
  · rintro ⟨y, hy⟩
    refine ⟨e.symm y, ?_⟩
    apply e.injective
    rw [e.map_smul, e.apply_symm_apply, hy]

theorem linearEquiv_primitive (e : U ≃ₗ[ℤ] W) (x : U) :
    PrimitiveElement x ↔ PrimitiveElement (e x) := by
  constructor
  · intro h p hp hd
    exact h p hp ((linearEquiv_scalar_divisibility e p x).mpr hd)
  · intro h p hp hd
    exact h p hp ((linearEquiv_scalar_divisibility e p x).mp hd)

def primitiveShellEquiv {A : Type*} (e : U ≃ₗ[ℤ] W)
    (Q : U → A) (R : W → A) (hQ : ∀ x, R (e x) = Q x) (level : A) :
    {x : U // Q x = level ∧ PrimitiveElement x} ≃
      {y : W // R y = level ∧ PrimitiveElement y} where
  toFun x := ⟨e x.1, by
    rw [hQ]
    exact ⟨x.property.1, (linearEquiv_primitive e x.1).mp x.property.2⟩⟩
  invFun y := ⟨e.symm y.1, by
    refine ⟨?_, (linearEquiv_primitive e (e.symm y.1)).mpr ?_⟩
    · rw [← hQ, e.apply_symm_apply]
      exact y.property.1
    · simpa only [e.apply_symm_apply] using y.property.2⟩
  left_inv x := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    exact e.symm_apply_apply x.1
  right_inv y := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    exact e.apply_symm_apply y.1

theorem primitiveShell_card_eq {A : Type*} (e : U ≃ₗ[ℤ] W)
    (Q : U → A) (R : W → A) (hQ : ∀ x, R (e x) = Q x) (level : A) :
    Nat.card {x : U // Q x = level ∧ PrimitiveElement x} =
      Nat.card {y : W // R y = level ∧ PrimitiveElement y} :=
  Nat.card_congr (primitiveShellEquiv e Q R hQ level)

end A354766Neighbors



-- END ShellEquivalence.lean

-- BEGIN PrimitiveNeighborAlgebra.lean









/-!
# Primitive vectors in a prime neighbor

This file first proves the algebraic pieces of the double count from actual
submodule inclusions. No representation recurrence, multiplicativity, or
representation formula is assumed. The projective family is supplied by the
concrete construction, and the finite-shell coordinates by the geometric
classification; their integration is separate from the lemmas below.
-/

namespace A354766Neighbors

variable {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V]

/-- Primitivity in a specified submodule, expressed in the common ambient
group. Membership in the submodule is recorded separately in each shell. -/
def PrimitiveIn (L : Submodule ℤ V) (x : V) : Prop :=
  ∀ q : ℕ, q.Prime → ¬ DivisibleIn L q x

theorem primitiveIn_subtype_iff (L : Submodule ℤ V) (x : L) :
    PrimitiveIn L (x : V) ↔ PrimitiveElement x := by
  constructor
  · intro h q hq
    rintro ⟨y, hy⟩
    exact h q hq ⟨y, y.property, congrArg Subtype.val hy⟩
  · intro h q hq
    rintro ⟨y, hy, hxy⟩
    exact h q hq ⟨⟨y, hy⟩, Subtype.ext hxy⟩

theorem primitiveIn_iff_away_and_at (L : Submodule ℤ V)
    (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (x : V) :
    PrimitiveIn L x ↔ PrimitiveAway L p x ∧ ¬ DivisibleIn L p x := by
  constructor
  · intro h
    exact ⟨fun q hq _ => h q hq, h p hp⟩
  · rintro ⟨haway, hat⟩ q hq
    by_cases hqp : q = p
    · simpa only [hqp] using hat
    · exact haway q hq hqp

theorem primitiveAway_prime_smul_iff (L : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ)
    (hp : p.Prime) (z : V) (hz : z ∈ L) :
    PrimitiveAway L p ((p : ℤ) • z) ↔ PrimitiveAway L p z := by
  have hcop (q : ℕ) (hq : q.Prime) (hqp : q ≠ p) : p.Coprime q := by
    apply hp.coprime_iff_not_dvd.mpr
    intro hd
    exact hqp ((Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq hp hq).mp hd).symm
  constructor
  · intro h q hq hqp hd
    exact h q hq hqp ((divisibleIn_smul_iff L z hz p q (hcop q hq hqp)).mpr hd)
  · intro h q hq hqp hd
    exact h q hq hqp ((divisibleIn_smul_iff L z hz p q (hcop q hq hqp)).mp hd)

/-- Multiplication by p carries exterior primitive neighbor vectors exactly
to primitive standard vectors. All hypotheses are actual submodule facts. -/
theorem exterior_primitive_scaled_iff
    (L N M : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hML : M ≤ L) (hMN : M ≤ N)
    (hpL : ∀ z ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hpN : ∀ z ∈ N, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hcancel : Function.Injective (fun z : V => (p : ℤ) • z))
    (y : V) (hyN : y ∈ N) (hyL : y ∉ L) :
    PrimitiveIn L ((p : ℤ) • y) ↔ PrimitiveIn N y := by
  have hatL : ¬ DivisibleIn L p ((p : ℤ) • y) := by
    intro hd
    exact hyL ((divisibleIn_smul_iff_mem L p hcancel y).mp hd)
  have hatN : ¬ DivisibleIn N p y :=
    not_divisibleIn_of_outside L N p (fun z hz => hML (hpN z hz)) y hyL
  rw [primitiveIn_iff_away_and_at L p hp,
    primitiveIn_iff_away_and_at N p hp]
  simp only [hatL, hatN, not_false_eq_true, and_true]
  exact primitiveAway_scaled_iff L N M p hp hML hMN hpL hpN y hyN

/-- For an inside vector that is primitive in the standard lattice, the only
possible loss of neighbor primitivity is divisibility by p. -/
theorem inside_primitive_iff_not_divisible
    (L N M : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hML : M ≤ L) (hMN : M ≤ N)
    (hpL : ∀ z ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hpN : ∀ z ∈ N, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (z : V) (hzM : z ∈ M) (hz : PrimitiveIn L z) :
    PrimitiveIn N z ↔ ¬ DivisibleIn N p z := by
  have hawayL : PrimitiveAway L p z := ((primitiveIn_iff_away_and_at L p hp z).mp hz).1
  have hawayN : PrimitiveAway N p z :=
    (primitiveAway_iff_of_prime_multiple_inclusions
      L N M p hp hML hMN hpL hpN z hzM).mp hawayL
  rw [primitiveIn_iff_away_and_at N p hp]
  simp only [hawayN, true_and]

/-- The exact-content-p case: p*z is primitive in N exactly when the
primitive standard quotient z does not belong to N. -/
theorem inside_one_primitive_iff_not_mem
    (L N M : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hML : M ≤ L) (hMN : M ≤ N)
    (hpL : ∀ z ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hpN : ∀ z ∈ N, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hcancel : Function.Injective (fun z : V => (p : ℤ) • z))
    (z : V) (hzL : z ∈ L) (hz : PrimitiveIn L z) :
    PrimitiveIn N ((p : ℤ) • z) ↔ z ∉ N := by
  have hawayLz : PrimitiveAway L p z :=
    ((primitiveIn_iff_away_and_at L p hp z).mp hz).1
  have hawayLpz : PrimitiveAway L p ((p : ℤ) • z) :=
    (primitiveAway_prime_smul_iff L p hp z hzL).mpr hawayLz
  have hawayNpz : PrimitiveAway N p ((p : ℤ) • z) :=
    (primitiveAway_iff_of_prime_multiple_inclusions L N M p hp hML hMN hpL hpN
      ((p : ℤ) • z) (hpL z hzL)).mp hawayLpz
  rw [primitiveIn_iff_away_and_at N p hp]
  simp only [hawayNpz, true_and, divisibleIn_smul_iff_mem N p hcancel z]

/-- Content at least p² always destroys primitivity in a neighbor. -/
theorem inside_two_or_more_not_primitive
    (L N : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (a : ℕ) (ha : 2 ≤ a)
    (hpL : ∀ z ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ N) (z : V) (hz : z ∈ L) :
    ¬ PrimitiveIn N (((p : ℤ) ^ a) • z) := by
  intro hprim
  exact hprim p hp (divisibleIn_pow_of_two_le L N p a ha hpL z hz)

section ExteriorShell

variable {R : Type*} [CommRing R] [IsDomain R]

def OutsidePrimitiveShell (L N : Submodule ℤ V) (Q : V → R) (level : R) :=
  {y : V // y ∈ N ∧ Q y = level ∧ PrimitiveIn N y ∧ y ∉ L}

def ScaledPrimitiveShell (L N : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ)
    (Q : V → R) (level : R) :=
  {v : V // v ∈ L ∧ Q v = level ∧ PrimitiveIn L v ∧ DivisibleIn N p v}

def outsideScale
    (L N M : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hML : M ≤ L) (hMN : M ≤ N)
    (hpL : ∀ z ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hpN : ∀ z ∈ N, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hcancel : Function.Injective (fun z : V => (p : ℤ) • z))
    (Q : V → R) (scale level : R) (hQ : ∀ y, Q ((p : ℤ) • y) = scale * Q y) :
    OutsidePrimitiveShell L N Q level → ScaledPrimitiveShell L N p Q (scale * level) :=
  fun y => ⟨(p : ℤ) • y.val, hML (hpN y.val y.property.1), by
    rw [hQ, y.property.2.1],
    (exterior_primitive_scaled_iff L N M p hp hML hMN hpL hpN hcancel
      y.val y.property.1 y.property.2.2.2).mpr y.property.2.2.1,
    y.val, y.property.1, rfl⟩

theorem outsideScale_bijective
    (L N M : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hML : M ≤ L) (hMN : M ≤ N)
    (hpL : ∀ z ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hpN : ∀ z ∈ N, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hcancel : Function.Injective (fun z : V => (p : ℤ) • z))
    (Q : V → R) (scale level : R) (hscale : scale ≠ 0)
    (hQ : ∀ y, Q ((p : ℤ) • y) = scale * Q y) :
    Function.Bijective (outsideScale L N M p hp hML hMN hpL hpN hcancel Q scale level hQ) := by
  constructor
  · intro x y h
    exact Subtype.ext (hcancel (congrArg Subtype.val h))
  · rintro ⟨v, hvL, hvQ, hvprim, y, hyN, hpy⟩
    have hyL : y ∉ L := by
      intro h
      exact hvprim p hp ⟨y, h, hpy⟩
    have hyQ : Q y = level := by
      apply mul_left_cancel₀ hscale
      rw [← hQ, hpy]
      exact hvQ
    have hyprim : PrimitiveIn N y := by
      apply (exterior_primitive_scaled_iff
        L N M p hp hML hMN hpL hpN hcancel y hyN hyL).mp
      simpa only [hpy] using hvprim
    refine ⟨⟨y, hyN, hyQ, hyprim, hyL⟩, ?_⟩
    exact Subtype.ext hpy

/-- A proved bijection for the exterior part at one actual neighbor. The
remaining projective membership step identifies which neighbor occurs. -/
noncomputable def outsidePrimitiveShellEquiv
    (L N M : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hML : M ≤ L) (hMN : M ≤ N)
    (hpL : ∀ z ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hpN : ∀ z ∈ N, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hcancel : Function.Injective (fun z : V => (p : ℤ) • z))
    (Q : V → R) (scale level : R) (hscale : scale ≠ 0)
    (hQ : ∀ y, Q ((p : ℤ) • y) = scale * Q y) :
    OutsidePrimitiveShell L N Q level ≃ ScaledPrimitiveShell L N p Q (scale * level) :=
  Equiv.ofBijective (outsideScale L N M p hp hML hMN hpL hpN hcancel Q scale level hQ)
    (outsideScale_bijective L N M p hp hML hMN hpL hpN hcancel Q scale level hscale hQ)

end ExteriorShell

/-- This exact divisibility test includes the ramified prime three: a square
prime divisor of `3*m²` already divides `m`. -/
theorem prime_sq_dvd_three_mul_sq_iff (p m : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) :
    p ^ 2 ∣ 3 * m ^ 2 ↔ p ∣ m := by
  constructor
  · intro hd
    by_contra hpm
    have hpm2 : ¬ p ∣ m ^ 2 := fun h => hpm (hp.dvd_of_dvd_pow h)
    have hp3 : p ∣ 3 :=
      (hp.dvd_or_dvd ((dvd_pow_self p (by decide : (2 : ℕ) ≠ 0)).trans hd)).resolve_right hpm2
    have hep : p = 3 := (Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq hp Nat.prime_three).mp hp3
    subst p
    have hm2 : 3 ∣ m ^ 2 :=
      (mul_dvd_mul_iff_left (by decide : (3 : ℕ) ≠ 0)).mp (by simpa only [pow_two] using hd)
    exact hpm (Nat.prime_three.dvd_of_dvd_pow hm2)
  · rintro ⟨k, rfl⟩
    refine ⟨3 * k ^ 2, ?_⟩
    ring

/-- Integral neighbor norms rule out p-division at level `3*m²` when p does
not divide m. This proof remains valid at p=3, where reduction of the norm
itself is zero and a nonzero-residue-norm argument would be incorrect. -/
theorem not_divisibleIn_of_norm_three_mul_sq
    (N : Submodule ℤ V) (p m : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (hpm : ¬ p ∣ m)
    (Q : V → ℚ) (hQ : ∀ y, Q ((p : ℤ) • y) = (p : ℚ) ^ 2 * Q y)
    (hintegral : ∀ y ∈ N, ∃ k : ℤ, Q y = (k : ℚ))
    (x : V) (hx : Q x = ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)) :
    ¬ DivisibleIn N p x := by
  rintro ⟨y, hy, hpy⟩
  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := hintegral y hy
  have heqQ : ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ) = (p : ℚ) ^ 2 * (k : ℚ) := by
    calc
      ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ) = Q x := hx.symm
      _ = Q ((p : ℤ) • y) := congrArg Q hpy.symm
      _ = (p : ℚ) ^ 2 * (k : ℚ) := by rw [hQ, hk]
  have heqZ : ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℤ) = (p : ℤ) ^ 2 * k := by
    exact_mod_cast heqQ
  have hdZ : ((p ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℤ) ∣ ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℤ) :=
