    ⟨k, by simpa only [Nat.cast_pow] using heqZ⟩
  have hdN : p ^ 2 ∣ 3 * m ^ 2 := Int.natCast_dvd_natCast.mp hdZ
  exact hpm ((prime_sq_dvd_three_mul_sq_iff p m hp).mp hdN)

section InsideFibers

variable {I : Type*}

/-- In exact content p, the admissible neighbors are exactly the complement
of the neighbors containing the primitive quotient. -/
noncomputable def insideOneFiberEquiv
    (L : Submodule ℤ V) (N M : I → Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hML : ∀ i, M i ≤ L) (hMN : ∀ i, M i ≤ N i)
    (hpL : ∀ i z, z ∈ L → (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M i)
    (hpN : ∀ i z, z ∈ N i → (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M i)
    (hcancel : Function.Injective (fun z : V => (p : ℤ) • z))
    (z : V) (hzL : z ∈ L) (hz : PrimitiveIn L z) :
    {i : I // (p : ℤ) • z ∈ N i ∧ PrimitiveIn (N i) ((p : ℤ) • z)} ≃
      {i : I // z ∉ N i} :=
  Equiv.subtypeEquivRight (fun i => by
    have hm : (p : ℤ) • z ∈ N i := hMN i (hpL i z hzL)
    simp only [hm, true_and]
    exact inside_one_primitive_iff_not_mem
      L (N i) (M i) p hp (hML i) (hMN i) (hpL i) (hpN i) hcancel z hzL hz)

theorem card_inside_one_fiber [Finite I]
    (L : Submodule ℤ V) (N M : I → Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hML : ∀ i, M i ≤ L) (hMN : ∀ i, M i ≤ N i)
    (hpL : ∀ i z, z ∈ L → (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M i)
    (hpN : ∀ i z, z ∈ N i → (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M i)
    (hcancel : Function.Injective (fun z : V => (p : ℤ) • z))
    (z : V) (hzL : z ∈ L) (hz : PrimitiveIn L z) :
    Nat.card {i : I // (p : ℤ) • z ∈ N i ∧ PrimitiveIn (N i) ((p : ℤ) • z)} =
      Nat.card I - Nat.card {i : I // z ∈ N i} := by
  classical
  letI : Fintype I := Fintype.ofFinite I
  rw [Nat.card_congr (insideOneFiberEquiv L N M p hp hML hMN hpL hpN hcancel z hzL hz)]
  simp only [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, Fintype.card_subtype_compl]

/-- At exact content one and p∤m, every neighbor containing the standard
vector keeps it primitive. The integrality argument includes p=3. -/
noncomputable def insideZeroNondivFiberEquiv
    (L : Submodule ℤ V) (N M : I → Submodule ℤ V) (p m : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hpm : ¬ p ∣ m)
    (hML : ∀ i, M i ≤ L) (hMN : ∀ i, M i ≤ N i)
    (hcommon : ∀ i, L ⊓ N i ≤ M i)
    (hpL : ∀ i z, z ∈ L → (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M i)
    (hpN : ∀ i z, z ∈ N i → (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M i)
    (Q : V → ℚ) (hQ : ∀ y, Q ((p : ℤ) • y) = (p : ℚ) ^ 2 * Q y)
    (hintegral : ∀ i y, y ∈ N i → ∃ k : ℤ, Q y = (k : ℚ))
    (z : V) (hzL : z ∈ L) (hz : PrimitiveIn L z)
    (hzQ : Q z = ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)) :
    {i : I // z ∈ N i ∧ PrimitiveIn (N i) z} ≃ {i : I // z ∈ N i} :=
  Equiv.subtypeEquivRight (fun i => by
    constructor
    · exact And.left
    · intro hzi
      refine ⟨hzi, ?_⟩
      apply (inside_primitive_iff_not_divisible L (N i) (M i) p hp
        (hML i) (hMN i) (hpL i) (hpN i) z (hcommon i ⟨hzL, hzi⟩) hz).mpr
      exact not_divisibleIn_of_norm_three_mul_sq (N i) p m hp hpm Q hQ
        (hintegral i) z hzQ)

theorem card_inside_two_or_more_fiber_zero
    (L : Submodule ℤ V) (N : I → Submodule ℤ V)
    (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (a : ℕ) (ha : 2 ≤ a)
    (hpL : ∀ i z, z ∈ L → (p : ℤ) • z ∈ N i)
    (z : V) (hzL : z ∈ L) :
    Nat.card {i : I // ((p : ℤ) ^ a) • z ∈ N i ∧
      PrimitiveIn (N i) (((p : ℤ) ^ a) • z)} = 0 := by
  letI : IsEmpty {i : I // ((p : ℤ) ^ a) • z ∈ N i ∧
      PrimitiveIn (N i) (((p : ℤ) ^ a) • z)} :=
    ⟨fun i => inside_two_or_more_not_primitive
      L (N i.val) p hp a ha (hpL i.val) z hzL i.property.2⟩
  exact Nat.card_of_isEmpty

end InsideFibers

end A354766Neighbors









-- END PrimitiveNeighborAlgebra.lean

-- BEGIN NeighborShellCounts.lean





/-!
Actual normalized neighbor shells have the historical primitive three-square
cardinality. The lattice coordinate equivalence is constructed by the proved
small-lattice classification, and every shell identification is an equivalence.
No shell cardinality or representation recurrence is assumed.
-/

noncomputable section

namespace A354766Neighbors

open A354766Research.Neighbor

/-- Integer norm levels and the recovered primitive-content model agree. -/
def rationalPrimitiveVectorShellEquiv (n : ℕ) :
    {v : IntegerVector // (vectorNorm v : ℚ) = (n : ℚ) ∧ PrimitiveElement v} ≃
      PrimitiveVectorShell n :=
  Equiv.subtypeEquivRight (fun v => by
    have hnorm : (vectorNorm v : ℚ) = (n : ℚ) ↔ vectorNorm v = (n : ℤ) := by
      constructor <;> intro h <;> exact_mod_cast h
    have hprimitive : PrimitiveElement v ↔ vectorContent v = 1 :=
      (vectorContent_eq_one_iff_primitive v).symm
    exact and_congr hnorm hprimitive)

/-- Explicit integral norm coordinates identify primitive shells. -/
def coordinatePrimitiveShellEquiv {U : Type*} [AddCommGroup U]
    (e : U ≃ₗ[ℤ] IntegerVector) (Q : U → ℚ)
    (he : ∀ x, (vectorNorm (e x) : ℚ) = Q x) (n : ℕ) :
    {x : U // Q x = (n : ℚ) ∧ PrimitiveElement x} ≃ PrimitiveVectorShell n :=
  (primitiveShellEquiv e Q (fun v => (vectorNorm v : ℚ)) he (n : ℚ)).trans
    (rationalPrimitiveVectorShellEquiv n)

theorem coordinatePrimitiveShell_card {U : Type*} [AddCommGroup U]
    (e : U ≃ₗ[ℤ] IntegerVector) (Q : U → ℚ)
    (he : ∀ x, (vectorNorm (e x) : ℚ) = Q x) (n : ℕ) :
    Nat.card {x : U // Q x = (n : ℚ) ∧ PrimitiveElement x} =
      A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite n :=
  (Nat.card_congr (coordinatePrimitiveShellEquiv e Q he n)).trans
    (primitiveVectorShell_card n)

/-- Moving membership from an ambient shell into a subtype preserves the
exact scalar-divisibility definition of primitivity. -/
def ambientPrimitiveShellEquiv {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V]
    (L : Submodule ℤ V) (Q : V → ℚ) (level : ℚ) :
    {y : V // y ∈ L ∧ Q y = level ∧ PrimitiveIn L y} ≃
      {x : L // Q (x : V) = level ∧ PrimitiveElement x} where
  toFun y := ⟨⟨y.val, y.property.1⟩, y.property.2.1,
    (primitiveIn_subtype_iff L ⟨y.val, y.property.1⟩).mp y.property.2.2⟩
  invFun x := ⟨x.val.val, x.val.property, x.property.1,
    (primitiveIn_subtype_iff L x.val).mpr x.property.2⟩
  left_inv y := by apply Subtype.ext; rfl
  right_inv x := by apply Subtype.ext; rfl

/-- The actual normalized neighbor shell is equivalent to the finite
three-square model, at every natural norm level. -/
def actual_neighbor_shell_equiv
    (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (u : IntVec)
    (hu : ∃ i : Fin 3, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ u i)
    (hQ : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ dot3 u u) (level : ℕ) :
    {y : RatVec // y ∈ neighbor p u ∧ dot3 y y = (level : ℚ) ∧
      PrimitiveIn (neighbor p u) y} ≃ PrimitiveVectorShell level := by
  let e := Classical.choose (neighbor_integral_coordinates p hp u hu hQ)
  have he := Classical.choose_spec (neighbor_integral_coordinates p hp u hu hQ)
  have heQ (x : neighbor p u) :
      (vectorNorm (e x) : ℚ) = dot3 (x : RatVec) (x : RatVec) := by
    apply (Rat.cast_injective : Function.Injective (fun q : ℚ => (q : ℝ)))
    simpa [e, vectorNorm, Fin.sum_univ_succ, add_assoc] using he x
  exact (ambientPrimitiveShellEquiv (neighbor p u) (fun y => dot3 y y) (level : ℚ)).trans
    (coordinatePrimitiveShellEquiv e (fun x => dot3 (x : RatVec) (x : RatVec)) heQ level)

/-- This is the concrete cardinality needed by the projective-neighbor
double count, with every geometric hypothesis discharged above. -/
theorem actual_neighbor_shell_card
    (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (u : IntVec)
    (hu : ∃ i : Fin 3, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ u i)
    (hQ : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ dot3 u u) (level : ℕ) :
    Nat.card {y : RatVec // y ∈ neighbor p u ∧ dot3 y y = (level : ℚ) ∧
      PrimitiveIn (neighbor p u) y} = A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite level :=
  (Nat.card_congr (actual_neighbor_shell_equiv p hp u hu hQ level)).trans
    (primitiveVectorShell_card level)

theorem actual_neighbor_shell_finite
    (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (u : IntVec)
    (hu : ∃ i : Fin 3, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ u i)
    (hQ : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ dot3 u u) (level : ℕ) :
    Finite {y : RatVec // y ∈ neighbor p u ∧ dot3 y y = (level : ℚ) ∧
      PrimitiveIn (neighbor p u) y} :=
  Finite.of_equiv (PrimitiveVectorShell level)
    (actual_neighbor_shell_equiv p hp u hu hQ level).symm

end A354766Neighbors






-- END NeighborShellCounts.lean

-- BEGIN IsotropicLift.lean




/-!
# Lifting a nonzero isotropic integer triple modulo an odd prime

This is an elementary prerequisite for the integral p-neighbor construction.
It proves the lift itself, rather than assuming any neighbor-counting theorem
or recurrence for representation numbers.
-/

namespace A354766Research

/-- The standard positive ternary quadratic form on integer triples. -/
def tripleNormSq (u : ℤ × ℤ × ℤ) : ℤ :=
  u.1 ^ 2 + u.2.1 ^ 2 + u.2.2 ^ 2

/-- Coordinatewise congruence, stated with integer divisibility. -/
def tripleCongruent (p : ℕ) (u v : ℤ × ℤ × ℤ) : Prop :=
  (p : ℤ) ∣ u.1 - v.1 ∧
    (p : ℤ) ∣ u.2.1 - v.2.1 ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ u.2.2 - v.2.2

/-- A triple remains nonzero after reduction modulo p. -/
def tripleNonzeroMod (p : ℕ) (u : ℤ × ℤ × ℤ) : Prop :=
  ¬((p : ℤ) ∣ u.1 ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ u.2.1 ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ u.2.2)

private theorem not_dvd_coordinate_shift (p : ℕ) (a t : ℤ)
    (ha : ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ a) : ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ a + (p : ℤ) * t := by
  intro h
  apply ha
  simpa using dvd_sub h (dvd_mul_right (p : ℤ) t)

/-- Adjusting a coordinate that is nonzero modulo p lifts isotropy from p to p². -/
theorem exists_isotropic_coordinate_shift (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (a b c : ℤ) (ha : ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ a)
    (hQ : (p : ℤ) ∣ a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 + c ^ 2) :
    ∃ t : ℤ, (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ (a + (p : ℤ) * t) ^ 2 + b ^ 2 + c ^ 2 := by
  have hprime : Prime (p : ℤ) := Nat.prime_iff_prime_int.mp hp
  have hcoef : ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ 2 * a := by
    intro h
    rcases hprime.dvd_or_dvd h with htwo | ha'
    · have htwo' : p ∣ 2 := Int.natCast_dvd_natCast.mp htwo
      exact hp2 (Nat.le_antisymm (Nat.le_of_dvd (by decide) htwo') hp.two_le)
    · exact ha ha'
  have hcopNat : Nat.Coprime p (2 * a).natAbs :=
    hp.coprime_iff_not_dvd.mpr (by
      intro h
      exact hcoef (Int.natCast_dvd.mpr h))
  have hcop : IsCoprime (p : ℤ) (2 * a) :=
    Int.isCoprime_iff_gcd_eq_one.mpr (by
      simpa only [Int.gcd_def, Int.natAbs_natCast] using hcopNat)
  rcases hcop with ⟨s, t, hbez⟩
  rcases hQ with ⟨k, hk⟩
  refine ⟨-k * t, k * s + (-k * t) ^ 2, ?_⟩
  calc
    (a + (p : ℤ) * (-k * t)) ^ 2 + b ^ 2 + c ^ 2 =
        (a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 + c ^ 2) +
          (p : ℤ) * (-2 * a * k * t) + (p : ℤ) ^ 2 * (-k * t) ^ 2 := by ring
    _ = (p : ℤ) * k * (1 - t * (2 * a)) +
          (p : ℤ) ^ 2 * (-k * t) ^ 2 := by rw [hk]; ring
    _ = (p : ℤ) ^ 2 * (k * s + (-k * t) ^ 2) := by
      rw [← eq_sub_of_add_eq hbez]
      ring

/-- A nonzero isotropic class modulo an odd prime has an integer lift whose norm
is divisible by p². The lift stays in the original residue class and remains
nonzero modulo p. The proof changes just one coordinate. -/
theorem exists_normalized_isotropic_lift (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (u : ℤ × ℤ × ℤ) (hu : tripleNonzeroMod p u)
    (hQ : (p : ℤ) ∣ tripleNormSq u) :
    ∃ v : ℤ × ℤ × ℤ, tripleCongruent p v u ∧
      (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ tripleNormSq v ∧ tripleNonzeroMod p v := by
  rcases u with ⟨a, b, c⟩
  change ¬((p : ℤ) ∣ a ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ b ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ c) at hu
  change (p : ℤ) ∣ a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 + c ^ 2 at hQ
  by_cases ha : (p : ℤ) ∣ a
  · by_cases hb : (p : ℤ) ∣ b
    · have hc : ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ c := fun hc => hu ⟨ha, hb, hc⟩
      have hQ' : (p : ℤ) ∣ c ^ 2 + a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 := by
        rw [show c ^ 2 + a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 = a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 + c ^ 2 by ring]
        exact hQ
      obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := exists_isotropic_coordinate_shift p hp hp2 c a b hc hQ'
      refine ⟨(a, b, c + (p : ℤ) * t), ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
      · change (p : ℤ) ∣ a - a ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ b - b ∧
          (p : ℤ) ∣ (c + (p : ℤ) * t) - c
        refine ⟨by simp, by simp, t, ?_⟩
        ring
      · change (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 + (c + (p : ℤ) * t) ^ 2
        rw [show a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 + (c + (p : ℤ) * t) ^ 2 =
          (c + (p : ℤ) * t) ^ 2 + a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 by ring]
        exact ht
      · intro h
        exact not_dvd_coordinate_shift p c t hc h.2.2
    · obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := exists_isotropic_coordinate_shift p hp hp2 b a c hb (by
        rw [add_comm (b ^ 2) (a ^ 2)]
        exact hQ)
      refine ⟨(a, b + (p : ℤ) * t, c), ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
      · change (p : ℤ) ∣ a - a ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ (b + (p : ℤ) * t) - b ∧
          (p : ℤ) ∣ c - c
        refine ⟨by simp, ⟨t, ?_⟩, by simp⟩
        ring
      · change (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ a ^ 2 + (b + (p : ℤ) * t) ^ 2 + c ^ 2
        rw [add_comm (a ^ 2) ((b + (p : ℤ) * t) ^ 2)]
        exact ht
      · intro h
        exact not_dvd_coordinate_shift p b t hb h.2.1
  · obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := exists_isotropic_coordinate_shift p hp hp2 a b c ha hQ
    refine ⟨(a + (p : ℤ) * t, b, c), ?_, ht, ?_⟩
    · change (p : ℤ) ∣ (a + (p : ℤ) * t) - a ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ b - b ∧
        (p : ℤ) ∣ c - c
      refine ⟨⟨t, ?_⟩, by simp, by simp⟩
      ring
    · intro h
      exact not_dvd_coordinate_shift p a t ha h.1

end A354766Research



-- END IsotropicLift.lean

-- BEGIN IsotropicLines.lean










/-!
# Explicit points of a split ternary conic

The parametrization below proves the projective conic count by an actual
equivalence, rather than by division of a nonzero-vector count.  It works over
every field, including fields of characteristic two.  The standard
three-squares bridge below requires characteristic different from two.
-/

namespace A354766Research.IsotropicLines

variable (F : Type*) [Field F]

abbrev Ternary := Fin 3 → F

/-- A representative of an isotropic projective line for `a*b = c^2`.
The existential formulation makes independence of the representative explicit. -/
def SplitIsotropic (P : Projectivization F (Ternary F)) : Prop :=
  ∃ (v : Ternary F) (hv : v ≠ 0),
    Projectivization.mk F v hv = P ∧ v 0 * v 1 = (v 2) ^ 2

abbrev SplitConic := {P : Projectivization F (Ternary F) // SplitIsotropic F P}

/-- The affine chart and the single point at infinity. -/
def chartVector : Option F → Ternary F
  | none => ![0, 1, 0]
  | some t => ![1, t ^ 2, t]

theorem chartVector_ne_zero (t : Option F) : chartVector F t ≠ 0 := by
  cases t with
  | none =>
      intro h
      have := congrFun h 1
      simpa [chartVector] using this
  | some t =>
      intro h
      have := congrFun h 0
      simpa [chartVector] using this

theorem chartVector_isotropic (t : Option F) :
    chartVector F t 0 * chartVector F t 1 = (chartVector F t 2) ^ 2 := by
  cases t <;> simp [chartVector]

def conicParam (t : Option F) : SplitConic F :=
  ⟨Projectivization.mk F (chartVector F t) (chartVector_ne_zero F t),
    chartVector F t, chartVector_ne_zero F t, rfl, chartVector_isotropic F t⟩

theorem conicParam_injective : Function.Injective (conicParam F) := by
  intro s t h
  have hp := congrArg Subtype.val h
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ :=
    (Projectivization.mk_eq_mk_iff' F (chartVector F s) (chartVector F t)
      (chartVector_ne_zero F s) (chartVector_ne_zero F t)).mp hp
  have h0 := congrFun ha 0
  have h1 := congrFun ha 1
  have h2 := congrFun ha 2
  cases s with
  | none =>
      cases t with
      | none => rfl
      | some t =>
          simp [chartVector] at h0 h1
          simp [h0] at h1
  | some s =>
      cases t with
      | none => simpa [chartVector] using h0
      | some t =>
          simp [chartVector] at h0 h2
          have hts : t = s := by simpa [h0] using h2
          exact congrArg some hts.symm

theorem conicParam_surjective : Function.Surjective (conicParam F) := by
  rintro ⟨P, v, hv, rfl, hq⟩
  by_cases h0 : v 0 = 0
  · have h2 : v 2 = 0 := by
      have hs : (v 2) ^ 2 = 0 := by simpa [h0] using hq.symm
      exact eq_zero_of_pow_eq_zero hs
    have h1 : v 1 ≠ 0 := by
      intro hh
      apply hv
      ext i
      fin_cases i <;> simp [h0, h2, hh]
    refine ⟨none, Subtype.ext ?_⟩
    apply (Projectivization.mk_eq_mk_iff' F _ _ _ _).mpr
    refine ⟨(v 1)⁻¹, ?_⟩
    ext i
    fin_cases i <;> simp [chartVector, h0, h2, h1]
  · refine ⟨some (v 2 / v 0), Subtype.ext ?_⟩
    apply (Projectivization.mk_eq_mk_iff' F _ _ _ _).mpr
    refine ⟨(v 0)⁻¹, ?_⟩
    ext i
    fin_cases i
    · simp [chartVector, h0]
    · change (v 0)⁻¹ * v 1 = (v 2 / v 0) ^ 2
      field_simp [h0]
      simpa [mul_comm] using hq
    · simp [chartVector, div_eq_mul_inv, mul_comm]

/-- Every split isotropic projective line has exactly one chart parameter. -/
noncomputable def splitConicEquiv : Option F ≃ SplitConic F :=
  Equiv.ofBijective (conicParam F)
    ⟨conicParam_injective F, conicParam_surjective F⟩

/-- A split ternary conic has `|F| + 1` projective isotropic lines. -/
theorem card_splitConic [Finite F] : Nat.card (SplitConic F) = Nat.card F + 1 := by
  classical
  letI : Fintype F := Fintype.ofFinite F
  rw [← Nat.card_congr (splitConicEquiv F)]
  simp only [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, Fintype.card_option]

/-- The standard quadratic form on three coordinates. -/
def standardNorm (v : Ternary F) : F := v 0 ^ 2 + v 1 ^ 2 + v 2 ^ 2

def StandardIsotropic (P : Projectivization F (Ternary F)) : Prop :=
  ∃ (v : Ternary F) (hv : v ≠ 0),
    Projectivization.mk F v hv = P ∧ standardNorm F v = 0

abbrev StandardConic := {P : Projectivization F (Ternary F) // StandardIsotropic F P}

/-- Coordinates adapted to the isotropic vector `(a,b,1)`. -/
def toSplit (a b : F) : Ternary F →ₗ[F] Ternary F where
  toFun v := ![v 2 - a * v 0 - b * v 1, v 2 + a * v 0 + b * v 1,
    a * v 1 - b * v 0]
  map_add' v w := by ext i; fin_cases i <;> simp <;> ring
  map_smul' s v := by ext i; fin_cases i <;> simp <;> ring

def fromSplit (a b : F) : Ternary F →ₗ[F] Ternary F where
  toFun w := ![-a * (w 1 - w 0) / 2 + b * w 2,
    -b * (w 1 - w 0) / 2 - a * w 2, (w 0 + w 1) / 2]
  map_add' v w := by ext i; fin_cases i <;> simp <;> ring
  map_smul' s v := by ext i; fin_cases i <;> simp <;> ring

theorem fromSplit_toSplit (a b : F) (hab : a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 = -1)
    (h2 : (2 : F) ≠ 0) (v : Ternary F) :
    fromSplit F a b (toSplit F a b v) = v := by
  ext i
  fin_cases i
  · change -a * ((v 2 + a * v 0 + b * v 1) -
        (v 2 - a * v 0 - b * v 1)) / 2 + b * (a * v 1 - b * v 0) = v 0
    field_simp [h2]
    linear_combination -2 * v 0 * hab
  · change -b * ((v 2 + a * v 0 + b * v 1) -
        (v 2 - a * v 0 - b * v 1)) / 2 - a * (a * v 1 - b * v 0) = v 1
    field_simp [h2]
    linear_combination -2 * v 1 * hab
  · change ((v 2 - a * v 0 - b * v 1) +
        (v 2 + a * v 0 + b * v 1)) / 2 = v 2
    field_simp [h2] <;> ring

theorem toSplit_fromSplit (a b : F) (hab : a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 = -1)
    (h2 : (2 : F) ≠ 0) (w : Ternary F) :
    toSplit F a b (fromSplit F a b w) = w := by
  ext i
  fin_cases i
  · change (w 0 + w 1) / 2 -
        a * (-a * (w 1 - w 0) / 2 + b * w 2) -
        b * (-b * (w 1 - w 0) / 2 - a * w 2) = w 0
    field_simp [h2]
    linear_combination (w 1 - w 0) * hab
  · change (w 0 + w 1) / 2 +
        a * (-a * (w 1 - w 0) / 2 + b * w 2) +
        b * (-b * (w 1 - w 0) / 2 - a * w 2) = w 1
    field_simp [h2]
    linear_combination (w 0 - w 1) * hab
  · change a * (-b * (w 1 - w 0) / 2 - a * w 2) -
        b * (-a * (w 1 - w 0) / 2 + b * w 2) = w 2
    field_simp [h2]
    linear_combination -2 * w 2 * hab

/-- The explicit invertible change to split coordinates. -/
def splitLinearEquiv (a b : F) (hab : a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 = -1)
    (h2 : (2 : F) ≠ 0) : Ternary F ≃ₗ[F] Ternary F where
  toLinearMap := toSplit F a b
  invFun := fromSplit F a b
  left_inv := fromSplit_toSplit F a b hab h2
  right_inv := toSplit_fromSplit F a b hab h2

theorem toSplit_norm (a b : F) (hab : a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 = -1)
    (v : Ternary F) :
    toSplit F a b v 0 * toSplit F a b v 1 - (toSplit F a b v 2) ^ 2 =
      standardNorm F v := by
  simp only [toSplit, standardNorm, LinearMap.coe_mk, AddHom.coe_mk,
    Matrix.cons_val_zero, Matrix.cons_val_one, Matrix.cons_val_fin_one,
      Matrix.cons_val_two, Matrix.head_cons, Matrix.tail_cons]
  linear_combination -(v 0 ^ 2 + v 1 ^ 2) * hab

def projectiveEquiv (e : Ternary F ≃ₗ[F] Ternary F) :
    Projectivization F (Ternary F) ≃ Projectivization F (Ternary F) where
  toFun := Projectivization.map e.toLinearMap e.injective
  invFun := Projectivization.map e.symm.toLinearMap e.symm.injective
  left_inv P := by
    induction P using Projectivization.ind with
    | h v hv => simp only [Projectivization.map_mk, LinearEquiv.coe_coe,
        LinearEquiv.symm_apply_apply]
  right_inv P := by
    induction P using Projectivization.ind with
    | h v hv => simp only [Projectivization.map_mk, LinearEquiv.coe_coe,
        LinearEquiv.apply_symm_apply]

theorem standard_iff_split (a b : F) (hab : a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 = -1)
    (h2 : (2 : F) ≠ 0) (P : Projectivization F (Ternary F)) :
    StandardIsotropic F P ↔
      SplitIsotropic F (projectiveEquiv F (splitLinearEquiv F a b hab h2) P) := by
  let e := splitLinearEquiv F a b hab h2
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨v, hv, rfl, hq⟩
    refine ⟨e v, e.map_ne_zero_iff.mpr hv, rfl, ?_⟩
    apply sub_eq_zero.mp
    exact (toSplit_norm F a b hab v).trans hq
  · rintro ⟨w, hw, hwP, hq⟩
    refine ⟨e.symm w, e.symm.map_ne_zero_iff.mpr hw, ?_, ?_⟩
    · apply (projectiveEquiv F e).injective
      change Projectivization.map e.toLinearMap e.injective
        (Projectivization.mk F (e.symm w) _) = _
      simpa only [Projectivization.map_mk, LinearEquiv.coe_coe,
        LinearEquiv.apply_symm_apply] using hwP
    · have hn := toSplit_norm F a b hab (e.symm w)
      change e (e.symm w) 0 * e (e.symm w) 1 - (e (e.symm w) 2) ^ 2 = _ at hn
      rw [e.apply_symm_apply] at hn
      exact hn.symm.trans (sub_eq_zero.mpr hq)

/-- A standard conic count given an isotropic vector `(a,b,1)`. -/
theorem card_standardConic_of_sq_add_sq [Finite F] (a b : F)
    (hab : a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 = -1) (h2 : (2 : F) ≠ 0) :
    Nat.card (StandardConic F) = Nat.card F + 1 := by
  let e := Equiv.subtypeEquiv
    (projectiveEquiv F (splitLinearEquiv F a b hab h2))
    (standard_iff_split F a b hab h2)
  exact (Nat.card_congr e).trans (card_splitConic F)

/-- The standard ternary form has exactly `p+1` isotropic projective lines over
`ZMod p`, for every odd prime `p`. -/
theorem card_standardConic_zmod (p : ℕ) [hp : Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) :
    Nat.card (StandardConic (ZMod p)) = p + 1 := by
  obtain ⟨a, b, hab⟩ := ZMod.sq_add_sq p (-1)
  have h2 : (2 : ZMod p) ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    have hd : p ∣ 2 := (ZMod.natCast_eq_zero_iff 2 p).mp h
    exact hp2 (Nat.le_antisymm (Nat.le_of_dvd (by decide) hd) hp.out.two_le)
  simpa only [Nat.card_zmod] using
    card_standardConic_of_sq_add_sq (ZMod p) a b hab h2

/-- The quadratic equation for a conic point perpendicular to `(A,B,C)`.
The infinity chart occurs precisely when `A=0`. -/
def SplitPerpParameter (A B C : F) : Option F → Prop
  | none => A = 0
  | some t => A * t ^ 2 - 2 * C * t + B = 0

theorem completing_square (A B C t : F) :
    (A * t - C) ^ 2 - (C ^ 2 - A * B) =
      A * (A * t ^ 2 - 2 * C * t + B) := by ring

def completingSquareEquiv (A B C : F) (hA : A ≠ 0) :
    {t : F // A * t ^ 2 - 2 * C * t + B = 0} ≃
      {s : F // s ^ 2 = C ^ 2 - A * B} where
  toFun t := ⟨A * t - C, by
    apply sub_eq_zero.mp
    rw [completing_square, t.property, mul_zero]⟩
  invFun s := ⟨(s + C) / A, by
    have hlin : A * ((s + C) / A) - C = s := by field_simp [hA] <;> ring
    have hh : A * (A * ((s + C) / A) ^ 2 - 2 * C * ((s + C) / A) + B) = 0 := by
      rw [← completing_square, hlin, s.property, sub_self]
    exact (mul_eq_zero.mp hh).resolve_left hA⟩
  left_inv t := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    change ((A * t - C + C) / A) = t
    field_simp [hA] <;> ring
  right_inv s := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    change A * ((s + C) / A) - C = s
    field_simp [hA] <;> ring

/-- Partition an `Option` subtype into its infinity and affine pieces. -/
def optionSubtypeSumEquiv {α : Type*} (P : Option α → Prop) :
    {t : Option α // P t} ≃ ({_u : Unit // P none} ⊕ {t : α // P (some t)}) where
  toFun := by
    rintro ⟨t, ht⟩
    cases t with
    | none => exact Sum.inl ⟨(), ht⟩
    | some t => exact Sum.inr ⟨t, ht⟩
  invFun := by
    rintro (⟨_, ht⟩ | ⟨t, ht⟩)
    · exact ⟨none, ht⟩
    · exact ⟨some t, ht⟩
  left_inv := by rintro ⟨(_ | t), ht⟩ <;> rfl
  right_inv := by
    rintro (⟨u, ht⟩ | ⟨t, ht⟩)
    · cases u; rfl
    · rfl

/-- The chart equation has `1+χ(C²-A*B)` parameters for a nonzero coefficient
triple. This includes the infinity point and both degenerate affine cases. -/
theorem card_splitPerpParameter [Fintype F] [DecidableEq F]
    (hchar : ringChar F ≠ 2) (A B C : F)
    (hne : ¬(A = 0 ∧ B = 0 ∧ C = 0)) :
    (Nat.card {t : Option F // SplitPerpParameter F A B C t} : ℤ) =
      quadraticChar F (C ^ 2 - A * B) + 1 := by
  classical
  have hcard : Nat.card {t : Option F // SplitPerpParameter F A B C t} =
      Nat.card {_u : Unit // A = 0} +
        Nat.card {t : F // A * t ^ 2 - 2 * C * t + B = 0} := by
    exact (Nat.card_congr (optionSubtypeSumEquiv (SplitPerpParameter F A B C))).trans
      (Nat.card_sum)
  by_cases hA : A = 0
  · have hinf : Nat.card {_u : Unit // A = 0} = 1 := by simp [hA]
    by_cases hC : C = 0
    · have hB : B ≠ 0 := fun hB => hne ⟨hA, hB, hC⟩
      have hfinite : Nat.card {t : F // A * t ^ 2 - 2 * C * t + B = 0} = 0 := by
        letI : IsEmpty {t : F // A * t ^ 2 - 2 * C * t + B = 0} :=
          ⟨by rintro ⟨t, ht⟩; exact hB (by simpa [hA, hC] using ht)⟩
        exact Nat.card_of_isEmpty
      rw [hcard, hinf, hfinite]
      simp [hA, hC]
    · have h2C : 2 * C ≠ 0 := mul_ne_zero (Ring.two_ne_zero hchar) hC
      have hroot (t : F) :
          A * t ^ 2 - 2 * C * t + B = 0 ↔ t = B / (2 * C) := by
        rw [eq_div_iff h2C]
        constructor
        · intro ht
          rw [hA] at ht
          linear_combination -ht
        · intro ht
          rw [hA]
          linear_combination -ht
      have hfinite : Nat.card {t : F // A * t ^ 2 - 2 * C * t + B = 0} = 1 := by
        apply Nat.card_eq_one_iff_exists.mpr
        refine ⟨⟨B / (2 * C), (hroot _).mpr rfl⟩, ?_⟩
        intro y
        exact Subtype.ext ((hroot y).mp y.property)
      rw [hcard, hinf, hfinite]
      simp [hA, quadraticChar_sq_one' (F := F) hC]
  · have hinf : Nat.card {_u : Unit // A = 0} = 0 := by
      letI : IsEmpty {_u : Unit // A = 0} := ⟨fun u => hA u.property⟩
      exact Nat.card_of_isEmpty
    have hsquare :
        (Nat.card {s : F // s ^ 2 = C ^ 2 - A * B} : ℤ) =
          quadraticChar F (C ^ 2 - A * B) + 1 := by
      have hn := Nat.card_eq_card_toFinset {s : F | s ^ 2 = C ^ 2 - A * B}
      exact (congrArg (fun n : ℕ => (n : ℤ)) hn).trans
        (quadraticChar_card_sqrts hchar _)
    have hfinite := Nat.card_congr (completingSquareEquiv F A B C hA)
    simpa only [hcard, hinf, zero_add, hfinite] using hsquare

/-- Twice the standard dot product in split coordinates. -/
def splitPolar (x : Ternary F) : Ternary F →ₗ[F] F where
  toFun y := x 0 * y 1 + x 1 * y 0 - 2 * x 2 * y 2
  map_add' v w := by simp; ring
  map_smul' s v := by simp; ring

abbrev SplitPerpConic (x : Ternary F) :=
  {P : SplitConic F // P.val.submodule ≤ (splitPolar F x).ker}

theorem splitPolar_chart_zero_iff (x : Ternary F) (t : Option F) :
    splitPolar F x (chartVector F t) = 0 ↔
      SplitPerpParameter F (x 0) (x 1) (x 2) t := by
  cases t with
  | none => simp [splitPolar, chartVector, SplitPerpParameter]
  | some t =>
      change x 0 * t ^ 2 + x 1 * 1 - 2 * x 2 * t = 0 ↔
        x 0 * t ^ 2 - 2 * x 2 * t + x 1 = 0
      rw [show x 0 * t ^ 2 + x 1 * 1 - 2 * x 2 * t =
        x 0 * t ^ 2 - 2 * x 2 * t + x 1 by ring]

noncomputable def splitPerpConicEquiv (x : Ternary F) :
    {t : Option F // SplitPerpParameter F (x 0) (x 1) (x 2) t} ≃
      SplitPerpConic F x :=
  Equiv.subtypeEquiv (splitConicEquiv F) (by
    intro t
    change SplitPerpParameter F (x 0) (x 1) (x 2) t ↔
      (Projectivization.mk F (chartVector F t) _).submodule ≤ (splitPolar F x).ker
    rw [Projectivization.submodule_mk, Submodule.span_singleton_le_iff_mem,
      LinearMap.mem_ker]
    exact (splitPolar_chart_zero_iff F x t).symm)

theorem card_splitPerpConic [Fintype F] [DecidableEq F]
    (hchar : ringChar F ≠ 2) (x : Ternary F) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
    (Nat.card (SplitPerpConic F x) : ℤ) =
      quadraticChar F ((x 2) ^ 2 - x 0 * x 1) + 1 := by
  have hne : ¬(x 0 = 0 ∧ x 1 = 0 ∧ x 2 = 0) := by
    rintro ⟨h0, h1, h2⟩
    apply hx
    ext i
    fin_cases i <;> simp [h0, h1, h2]
  rw [← Nat.card_congr (splitPerpConicEquiv F x)]
  exact card_splitPerpParameter F hchar (x 0) (x 1) (x 2) hne

/-- The standard dot product as a linear functional in its second variable. -/
def standardDot (x : Ternary F) : Ternary F →ₗ[F] F where
  toFun y := x 0 * y 0 + x 1 * y 1 + x 2 * y 2
  map_add' v w := by simp; ring
  map_smul' s v := by simp; ring

abbrev StandardPerpConic (x : Ternary F) :=
  {P : StandardConic F // P.val.submodule ≤ (standardDot F x).ker}

theorem card_standardPerpConic_zero :
    Nat.card (StandardPerpConic F 0) = Nat.card (StandardConic F) := by
  apply Nat.card_congr
  apply Equiv.subtypeUnivEquiv
  intro P v hv
  change standardDot F 0 v = 0
  simp [standardDot]

theorem toSplit_polar (a b : F) (hab : a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 = -1)
    (x y : Ternary F) :
    splitPolar F (toSplit F a b x) (toSplit F a b y) = 2 * standardDot F x y := by
  simp only [splitPolar, standardDot, toSplit, LinearMap.coe_mk, AddHom.coe_mk,
    Matrix.cons_val_zero, Matrix.cons_val_one, Matrix.cons_val_fin_one,
    Matrix.cons_val_two, Matrix.head_cons, Matrix.tail_cons]
  linear_combination -2 * (x 0 * y 0 + x 1 * y 1) * hab

def standardConicEquiv (a b : F) (hab : a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 = -1)
    (h2 : (2 : F) ≠ 0) : StandardConic F ≃ SplitConic F :=
  Equiv.subtypeEquiv (projectiveEquiv F (splitLinearEquiv F a b hab h2))
    (standard_iff_split F a b hab h2)

theorem standard_perp_iff_split (a b : F) (hab : a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 = -1)
    (h2 : (2 : F) ≠ 0) (x : Ternary F) (P : Projectivization F (Ternary F)) :
    P.submodule ≤ (standardDot F x).ker ↔
      (projectiveEquiv F (splitLinearEquiv F a b hab h2) P).submodule ≤
        (splitPolar F (toSplit F a b x)).ker := by
  induction P using Projectivization.ind with
  | h v hv =>
      change (Projectivization.mk F v hv).submodule ≤ (standardDot F x).ker ↔
        (Projectivization.mk F (toSplit F a b v) _).submodule ≤
          (splitPolar F (toSplit F a b x)).ker
      rw [Projectivization.submodule_mk, Projectivization.submodule_mk,
        Submodule.span_singleton_le_iff_mem, Submodule.span_singleton_le_iff_mem,
        LinearMap.mem_ker, LinearMap.mem_ker, toSplit_polar F a b hab]
      simp only [mul_eq_zero, h2, false_or]

def standardPerpConicEquiv (a b : F) (hab : a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 = -1)
    (h2 : (2 : F) ≠ 0) (x : Ternary F) :
    StandardPerpConic F x ≃ SplitPerpConic F (toSplit F a b x) :=
  Equiv.subtypeEquiv (standardConicEquiv F a b hab h2) (by
    intro P
    exact standard_perp_iff_split F a b hab h2 x P.val)

/-- The orthogonal-line count, with the exceptional zero vector excluded. -/
theorem card_standardPerpConic_of_sq_add_sq [Fintype F] [DecidableEq F]
    (hchar : ringChar F ≠ 2) (a b : F) (hab : a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 = -1)
    (x : Ternary F) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
    (Nat.card (StandardPerpConic F x) : ℤ) =
      quadraticChar F (-standardNorm F x) + 1 := by
  let h2 := Ring.two_ne_zero hchar
  let e := splitLinearEquiv F a b hab h2
  rw [Nat.card_congr (standardPerpConicEquiv F a b hab h2 x)]
  have hcount := card_splitPerpConic F hchar (e x) (e.map_ne_zero_iff.mpr hx)
  have hdisc : (e x 2) ^ 2 - e x 0 * e x 1 = -standardNorm F x := by
    change (toSplit F a b x 2) ^ 2 - toSplit F a b x 0 * toSplit F a b x 1 = _
    linear_combination -(toSplit_norm F a b hab x)
  simpa only [hdisc] using hcount

/-- The companion of `card_standardConic_zmod`: for a nonzero vector `x`,
exactly `1+χ(-Q(x))` isotropic projective lines lie in its perpendicular plane. -/
theorem card_standardPerpConic_zmod (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (x : Ternary (ZMod p)) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
    (Nat.card (StandardPerpConic (ZMod p) x) : ℤ) =
      quadraticChar (ZMod p) (-standardNorm (ZMod p) x) + 1 := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨a, b, hab⟩ := ZMod.sq_add_sq p (-1)
  have hchar : ringChar (ZMod p) ≠ 2 := by
    simpa only [ZMod.ringChar_zmod_n] using hp2
  exact card_standardPerpConic_of_sq_add_sq (ZMod p) hchar a b hab x hx

theorem card_standardPerpConic_zmod_zero (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) :
    Nat.card (StandardPerpConic (ZMod p) 0) = p + 1 := by
  rw [card_standardPerpConic_zero, card_standardConic_zmod p hp2]

end A354766Research.IsotropicLines




-- END IsotropicLines.lean

-- BEGIN NormalizedNeighborFamily.lean




/-!
# A chosen normalized neighbor for each isotropic projective line

This file is proof text prepared against the checked prerequisite modules.
Its own compilation and axiom audit are separate obligations until recorded.
-/

namespace A354766Research.NeighborFamily

open Neighbor IsotropicLines

def reduceVec (p : ℕ) (u : IntVec) : Ternary (ZMod p) := fun i => (u i : ZMod p)

theorem reduceVec_ne_zero_iff (p : ℕ) (u : IntVec) :
    reduceVec p u ≠ 0 ↔ ∃ i : Fin 3, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ u i := by
  classical
  constructor
  · intro h
    by_contra hn
    apply h
    funext i
    apply (ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd (u i) p).mpr
    by_contra hi
    exact hn ⟨i, hi⟩
  · rintro ⟨i, hi⟩ h
    apply hi
    apply (ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd (u i) p).mp
    exact congrFun h i

theorem reduceVec_norm (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (u : IntVec) :
    standardNorm (ZMod p) (reduceVec p u) = ((dot3 u u : ℤ) : ZMod p) := by
  simp [standardNorm, reduceVec, dot3, pow_two]

theorem reduceVec_dot (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (u v : IntVec) :
    standardDot (ZMod p) (reduceVec p u) (reduceVec p v) =
      ((dot3 u v : ℤ) : ZMod p) := by
  simp [standardDot, reduceVec, dot3]

theorem reduceVec_eq_of_congr (p : ℕ) (u v : IntVec)
    (h : ∀ i : Fin 3, (p : ℤ) ∣ u i - v i) : reduceVec p u = reduceVec p v := by
  funext i
  apply sub_eq_zero.mp
  have hz := (ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd (u i - v i) p).mpr (h i)
  simpa only [Int.cast_sub] using hz

theorem exists_normalized_vector_lift (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (u : IntVec) (hu : ∃ i : Fin 3, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ u i) (hQ : (p : ℤ) ∣ dot3 u u) :
    ∃ v : IntVec, (∀ i : Fin 3, (p : ℤ) ∣ v i - u i) ∧
      (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ dot3 v v ∧ (∃ i : Fin 3, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ v i) := by
  have ht : tripleNonzeroMod p (u 0, u 1, u 2) := by
    rintro ⟨h0, h1, h2⟩
    obtain ⟨i, hi⟩ := hu
    fin_cases i
    · exact hi h0
    · exact hi h1
    · exact hi h2
  have hQt : (p : ℤ) ∣ tripleNormSq (u 0, u 1, u 2) := by
    simpa only [tripleNormSq, dot3, pow_two] using hQ
  obtain ⟨t, hc, hQt', hnt⟩ := exists_normalized_isotropic_lift p hp hp2 (u 0, u 1, u 2) ht hQt
  let v : IntVec := ![t.1, t.2.1, t.2.2]
  have hnv : ¬((p : ℤ) ∣ v 0 ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ v 1 ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ v 2) := by
    simpa [v, tripleNonzeroMod] using hnt
  refine ⟨v, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rcases hc with ⟨h0, h1, h2⟩
    intro i
    fin_cases i
    · simpa [v] using h0
    · simpa [v] using h1
    · simpa [v] using h2
  · simpa [v, dot3, tripleNormSq, pow_two] using hQt'
  · by_cases h0 : (p : ℤ) ∣ v 0
    · by_cases h1 : (p : ℤ) ∣ v 1
      · exact ⟨2, fun h2 => hnv ⟨h0, h1, h2⟩⟩
      · exact ⟨1, h1⟩
    · exact ⟨0, h0⟩

theorem exists_normalized_representative (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) :
    ∃ u : IntVec, (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ dot3 u u ∧
      ∃ hu : reduceVec p u ≠ 0, Projectivization.mk (ZMod p) (reduceVec p u) hu = P.val := by
  letI : NeZero p := ⟨(Fact.out : p.Prime).ne_zero⟩
  rcases P.property with ⟨x, hx, hP, hQx⟩
  let z : IntVec := fun i => ((x i).val : ℤ)
  have hRz : reduceVec p z = x := by
    funext i
    simp [reduceVec, z, ZMod.natCast_zmod_val]
  have hz : ∃ i : Fin 3, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ z i :=
    (reduceVec_ne_zero_iff p z).mp (by rw [hRz]; exact hx)
  have hQz : (p : ℤ) ∣ dot3 z z := by
    apply (ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd (dot3 z z) p).mp
    rw [← reduceVec_norm, hRz]
    exact hQx
  obtain ⟨u, hcong, hQu, hnu⟩ := exists_normalized_vector_lift p Fact.out hp2 z hz hQz
  have hRu : reduceVec p u = x := (reduceVec_eq_of_congr p u z hcong).trans hRz
  have hnu' : reduceVec p u ≠ 0 := (reduceVec_ne_zero_iff p u).mpr hnu
  refine ⟨u, hQu, hnu', ?_⟩
  simpa only [hRu] using hP

noncomputable def normalizedRepresentative (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) : IntVec :=
  (exists_normalized_representative p hp2 P).choose

theorem normalizedRepresentative_norm (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) :
    (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ dot3 (normalizedRepresentative p hp2 P) (normalizedRepresentative p hp2 P) :=
  (exists_normalized_representative p hp2 P).choose_spec.1

theorem normalizedRepresentative_ne_zero (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) : reduceVec p (normalizedRepresentative p hp2 P) ≠ 0 :=
  (exists_normalized_representative p hp2 P).choose_spec.2.choose

theorem normalizedRepresentative_projective (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) :
    Projectivization.mk (ZMod p) (reduceVec p (normalizedRepresentative p hp2 P))
      (normalizedRepresentative_ne_zero p hp2 P) = P.val :=
  (exists_normalized_representative p hp2 P).choose_spec.2.choose_spec

noncomputable def family (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) : Submodule ℤ RatVec :=
  neighbor p (normalizedRepresentative p hp2 P)

theorem projective_eq_iff_integer_scalar (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime]
    (u v : IntVec) (hu : reduceVec p u ≠ 0) (hv : reduceVec p v ≠ 0) :
    Projectivization.mk (ZMod p) (reduceVec p v) hv =
        Projectivization.mk (ZMod p) (reduceVec p u) hu ↔
      ∃ a : ℤ, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ a ∧ ∀ i : Fin 3, (p : ℤ) ∣ v i - a * u i := by
  letI : NeZero p := ⟨(Fact.out : p.Prime).ne_zero⟩
  rw [Projectivization.mk_eq_mk_iff']
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨a, hae⟩
    have ha0 : a ≠ 0 := by
      intro h
      apply hv
      rw [← hae, h, zero_smul]
    let az : ℤ := (a.val : ℤ)
    have hcast : (az : ZMod p) = a := by simp [az, ZMod.natCast_zmod_val]
    refine ⟨az, ?_, ?_⟩
    · intro hd
      apply ha0
      rw [← hcast]
      exact (ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd az p).mpr hd
    · intro i
      apply (ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd (v i - az * u i) p).mp
      simp only [Int.cast_sub, Int.cast_mul, hcast]
      have hi := congrFun hae i
      change a * (u i : ZMod p) = (v i : ZMod p) at hi
      exact sub_eq_zero.mpr hi.symm
  · rintro ⟨a, ha, hcong⟩
    refine ⟨(a : ZMod p), ?_⟩
    funext i
    change (a : ZMod p) * (u i : ZMod p) = (v i : ZMod p)
    have hz := (ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd (v i - a * u i) p).mpr (hcong i)
    have hz' : (v i : ZMod p) - (a : ZMod p) * (u i : ZMod p) = 0 := by
      simpa only [Int.cast_sub, Int.cast_mul] using hz
    exact (sub_eq_zero.mp hz').symm

/-- The exact projective membership interface needed for exterior vectors. -/
theorem exterior_mem_family_iff (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) (v : IntVec) (hv : reduceVec p v ≠ 0)
    (hQv : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ dot3 v v) :
    liftVec p v ∈ family p hp2 P ↔
      Projectivization.mk (ZMod p) (reduceVec p v) hv = P.val := by
  rw [family, exterior_neighbor_membership_iff p Fact.out hp2
    (normalizedRepresentative p hp2 P) v ((reduceVec_ne_zero_iff p v).mp hv)
    (normalizedRepresentative_norm p hp2 P) hQv]
  rw [← normalizedRepresentative_projective p hp2 P]
  exact (projective_eq_iff_integer_scalar p (normalizedRepresentative p hp2 P) v
    (normalizedRepresentative_ne_zero p hp2 P) hv).symm

/-- The exact perpendicular-line interface needed for integral vectors. -/
theorem inside_mem_family_iff (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) (z : IntVec) :
    castVec z ∈ family p hp2 P ↔
      P.val.submodule ≤ (standardDot (ZMod p) (reduceVec p z)).ker := by
  let u := normalizedRepresentative p hp2 P
  have hu : ∃ i : Fin 3, ¬(p : ℤ) ∣ u i :=
    (reduceVec_ne_zero_iff p u).mp (normalizedRepresentative_ne_zero p hp2 P)
  have hQu : (p : ℤ) ∣ dot3 u u := by
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := normalizedRepresentative_norm p hp2 P
    refine ⟨(p : ℤ) * k, ?_⟩
    change dot3 (normalizedRepresentative p hp2 P) (normalizedRepresentative p hp2 P) = _
    rw [hk]
    ring
  have hmid : castVec z ∈ family p hp2 P ↔ castVec z ∈ midpoint p u := by
    constructor
    · intro hz
      have hi : castVec z ∈ standard ⊓ neighbor p u := ⟨⟨z, rfl⟩, hz⟩
      rw [standard_inf_neighbor p Fact.out u hu hQu] at hi
      exact hi
    · intro hz
      change castVec z ∈ neighbor p u
      exact midpoint_le_neighbor p u hz
  rw [hmid, castVec_mem_midpoint_iff]
  rw [← normalizedRepresentative_projective p hp2 P, Projectivization.submodule_mk,
    Submodule.span_singleton_le_iff_mem, LinearMap.mem_ker, reduceVec_dot]
  rw [ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd]
  have hc : dot3 z u = dot3 u z := by simp only [dot3]; ring
  rw [hc]

end A354766Research.NeighborFamily




-- END NormalizedNeighborFamily.lean

-- BEGIN InsideContentClassification.lean



/-!
Every primitive neighbor vector that lies in the standard lattice has
standard content either one or exactly p. The proof extracts an actual
prime divisor and excludes a second p division; it does not assume a
content decomposition or a counting recurrence.
-/

namespace A354766Neighbors

variable {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V]

theorem inside_primitive_cases
    (L N M : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hML : M ≤ L) (hMN : M ≤ N) (hcommon : L ⊓ N ≤ M)
    (hpL : ∀ z ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hpN : ∀ z ∈ N, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (x : V) (hxL : x ∈ L) (hxN : x ∈ N) (hx : PrimitiveIn N x) :
    PrimitiveIn L x ∨
      ∃ z ∈ L, PrimitiveIn L z ∧ x = (p : ℤ) • z ∧ z ∉ N := by
  classical
  by_cases hxprim : PrimitiveIn L x
  · exact Or.inl hxprim
  · right
    have hxM : x ∈ M := hcommon ⟨hxL, hxN⟩
    have hawayN : PrimitiveAway N p x :=
      ((primitiveIn_iff_away_and_at N p hp x).mp hx).1
    have hawayL : PrimitiveAway L p x :=
      (primitiveAway_iff_of_prime_multiple_inclusions
        L N M p hp hML hMN hpL hpN x hxM).mpr hawayN
    change ¬ (∀ q : ℕ, q.Prime → ¬ DivisibleIn L q x) at hxprim
    push_neg at hxprim
    obtain ⟨q, hq, hqdiv⟩ := hxprim
    have hqp : q = p := by
      by_contra hqp
      exact hawayL q hq hqp hqdiv
    subst q
    obtain ⟨z, hzL, hpz⟩ := hqdiv
    have hawayZ : PrimitiveAway L p z :=
      (primitiveAway_prime_smul_iff L p hp z hzL).mp (by simpa only [hpz] using hawayL)
    have hatZ : ¬ DivisibleIn L p z := by
      rintro ⟨w, hwL, hpw⟩
      apply hx p hp
      refine ⟨(p : ℤ) • w, hMN (hpL w hwL), ?_⟩
      calc
        (p : ℤ) • ((p : ℤ) • w) = (p : ℤ) • z := congrArg ((p : ℤ) • ·) hpw
        _ = x := hpz
    have hzprim : PrimitiveIn L z :=
      (primitiveIn_iff_away_and_at L p hp z).mpr ⟨hawayZ, hatZ⟩
    have hzN : z ∉ N := fun h => hx p hp ⟨z, h, hpz⟩
    exact ⟨z, hzL, hzprim, hpz.symm, hzN⟩

theorem inside_primitive_iff_content_cases
    (L N M : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hML : M ≤ L) (hMN : M ≤ N) (hcommon : L ⊓ N ≤ M)
    (hpL : ∀ z ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hpN : ∀ z ∈ N, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hcancel : Function.Injective (fun z : V => (p : ℤ) • z))
    (x : V) (hxL : x ∈ L) (hxN : x ∈ N) :
    PrimitiveIn N x ↔
      (PrimitiveIn L x ∧ ¬ DivisibleIn N p x) ∨
        ∃ z ∈ L, PrimitiveIn L z ∧ x = (p : ℤ) • z ∧ z ∉ N := by
  constructor
  · intro hx
    rcases inside_primitive_cases L N M p hp hML hMN hcommon hpL hpN x hxL hxN hx with h | h
    · exact Or.inl ⟨h, hx p hp⟩
    · exact Or.inr h
  · rintro (⟨hx, hdiv⟩ | ⟨z, hzL, hz, rfl, hzN⟩)
    · exact (inside_primitive_iff_not_divisible L N M p hp hML hMN hpL hpN
        x (hcommon ⟨hxL, hxN⟩) hx).mpr hdiv
    · exact (inside_one_primitive_iff_not_mem L N M p hp hML hMN hpL hpN
        hcancel z hzL hz).mpr hzN

end A354766Neighbors



-- END InsideContentClassification.lean

-- BEGIN InsideShellPartition.lean



namespace A354766Neighbors

variable {V R : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [CommRing R] [IsDomain R]

def InsidePrimitiveShell (L N : Submodule ℤ V) (Q : V → R) (level : R) :=
  {x : V // x ∈ L ∧ x ∈ N ∧ Q x = level ∧ PrimitiveIn N x}

def InsidePrimitiveZeroShell (L N : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ)
    (Q : V → R) (level : R) :=
  {x : V // x ∈ L ∧ x ∈ N ∧ Q x = level ∧
    PrimitiveIn L x ∧ ¬ DivisibleIn N p x}

def insidePartsMap
    (L N M : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hML : M ≤ L) (hMN : M ≤ N) (hcommon : L ⊓ N ≤ M)
    (hpL : ∀ z ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hpN : ∀ z ∈ N, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hcancel : Function.Injective (fun z : V => (p : ℤ) • z))
    (Q : V → R) (scale level : R)
    (hQ : ∀ z, Q ((p : ℤ) • z) = scale * Q z) :
    (InsidePrimitiveZeroShell L N p Q (scale * level) ⊕
      OutsidePrimitiveShell N L Q level) →
      InsidePrimitiveShell L N Q (scale * level)
  | Sum.inl x => ⟨x.val, x.property.1, x.property.2.1,
      x.property.2.2.1,
      (inside_primitive_iff_not_divisible L N M p hp hML hMN hpL hpN
        x.val (hcommon ⟨x.property.1, x.property.2.1⟩)
        x.property.2.2.2.1).mpr x.property.2.2.2.2⟩
  | Sum.inr z => ⟨(p : ℤ) • z.val, hML (hpL z.val z.property.1),
      hMN (hpL z.val z.property.1), by rw [hQ, z.property.2.1],
      (inside_one_primitive_iff_not_mem L N M p hp hML hMN hpL hpN
        hcancel z.val z.property.1 z.property.2.2.1).mpr z.property.2.2.2⟩

theorem insidePartsMap_bijective
    (L N M : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hML : M ≤ L) (hMN : M ≤ N) (hcommon : L ⊓ N ≤ M)
    (hpL : ∀ z ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hpN : ∀ z ∈ N, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hcancel : Function.Injective (fun z : V => (p : ℤ) • z))
    (Q : V → R) (scale level : R) (hscale : scale ≠ 0)
    (hQ : ∀ z, Q ((p : ℤ) • z) = scale * Q z) :
    Function.Bijective (insidePartsMap
      L N M p hp hML hMN hcommon hpL hpN hcancel Q scale level hQ) := by
  constructor
  · intro x y h
    have hval := congrArg Subtype.val h
    cases x with
    | inl x =>
      cases y with
      | inl y => exact congrArg Sum.inl (Subtype.ext hval)
      | inr z =>
        have hdiv : DivisibleIn L p x.val := ⟨z.val, z.property.1, hval.symm⟩
        exact False.elim (x.property.2.2.2.1 p hp hdiv)
    | inr z =>
      cases y with
      | inl y =>
        have hdiv : DivisibleIn L p y.val := ⟨z.val, z.property.1, hval⟩
        exact False.elim (y.property.2.2.2.1 p hp hdiv)
      | inr w => exact congrArg Sum.inr (Subtype.ext (hcancel hval))
  · intro x
    rcases inside_primitive_cases L N M p hp hML hMN hcommon hpL hpN
      x.val x.property.1 x.property.2.1 x.property.2.2.2 with hx | hz
    · refine ⟨Sum.inl ⟨x.val, x.property.1, x.property.2.1,
        x.property.2.2.1, hx, x.property.2.2.2 p hp⟩, ?_⟩
      rfl
    · obtain ⟨z, hzL, hzprim, hxz, hzN⟩ := hz
      have hzQ : Q z = level := by
        apply mul_left_cancel₀ hscale
        rw [← hQ, ← hxz]
        exact x.property.2.2.1
      refine ⟨Sum.inr ⟨z, hzL, hzQ, hzprim, hzN⟩, ?_⟩
      exact Subtype.ext hxz.symm

/-- The actual content-zero/content-one partition of an inside primitive
shell. The second part consists of primitive standard vectors outside N,
at the unscaled norm. -/
noncomputable def insidePrimitiveShellEquiv
    (L N M : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hML : M ≤ L) (hMN : M ≤ N) (hcommon : L ⊓ N ≤ M)
    (hpL : ∀ z ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hpN : ∀ z ∈ N, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hcancel : Function.Injective (fun z : V => (p : ℤ) • z))
    (Q : V → R) (scale level : R) (hscale : scale ≠ 0)
    (hQ : ∀ z, Q ((p : ℤ) • z) = scale * Q z) :
    (InsidePrimitiveZeroShell L N p Q (scale * level) ⊕
      OutsidePrimitiveShell N L Q level) ≃
      InsidePrimitiveShell L N Q (scale * level) :=
  Equiv.ofBijective
    (insidePartsMap L N M p hp hML hMN hcommon hpL hpN hcancel Q scale level hQ)
    (insidePartsMap_bijective
      L N M p hp hML hMN hcommon hpL hpN hcancel Q scale level hscale hQ)

end A354766Neighbors


-- END InsideShellPartition.lean

-- BEGIN ShellPartitions.lean



namespace A354766Neighbors

variable {V R : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [CommRing R] [IsDomain R]

abbrev AmbientPrimitiveShell (N : Submodule ℤ V) (Q : V → R) (level : R) :=
  {x : V // x ∈ N ∧ Q x = level ∧ PrimitiveIn N x}

def insideOutsideMap (L N : Submodule ℤ V) (Q : V → R) (level : R) :
    (InsidePrimitiveShell L N Q level ⊕ OutsidePrimitiveShell L N Q level) →
      AmbientPrimitiveShell N Q level
  | Sum.inl x => ⟨x.val, x.property.2.1, x.property.2.2.1, x.property.2.2.2⟩
  | Sum.inr x => ⟨x.val, x.property.1, x.property.2.1, x.property.2.2.1⟩

theorem insideOutsideMap_bijective (L N : Submodule ℤ V) (Q : V → R) (level : R) :
    Function.Bijective (insideOutsideMap L N Q level) := by
  constructor
  · intro x y h
    have hval := congrArg Subtype.val h
    cases x with
    | inl x =>
      cases y with
      | inl y => exact congrArg Sum.inl (Subtype.ext hval)
      | inr y =>
        change x.val = y.val at hval
        have hxL : y.val ∈ L := by rw [← hval]; exact x.property.1
        exact False.elim (y.property.2.2.2 hxL)
    | inr x =>
      cases y with
      | inl y =>
        change x.val = y.val at hval
        have hyL : x.val ∈ L := by rw [hval]; exact y.property.1
        exact False.elim (x.property.2.2.2 hyL)
      | inr y => exact congrArg Sum.inr (Subtype.ext hval)
  · intro x
    by_cases hxL : x.val ∈ L
    · exact ⟨Sum.inl ⟨x.val, hxL, x.property.1, x.property.2.1, x.property.2.2⟩, rfl⟩
    · exact ⟨Sum.inr ⟨x.val, x.property.1, x.property.2.1, x.property.2.2, hxL⟩, rfl⟩

noncomputable def insideOutsideShellEquiv
    (L N : Submodule ℤ V) (Q : V → R) (level : R) :
    (InsidePrimitiveShell L N Q level ⊕ OutsidePrimitiveShell L N Q level) ≃
      AmbientPrimitiveShell N Q level :=
  Equiv.ofBijective (insideOutsideMap L N Q level)
    (insideOutsideMap_bijective L N Q level)

instance finite_insidePrimitiveShell
    (L N : Submodule ℤ V) (Q : V → R) (level : R)
    [Finite (AmbientPrimitiveShell N Q level)] :
    Finite (InsidePrimitiveShell L N Q level) :=
  Finite.of_injective
    (fun x : InsidePrimitiveShell L N Q level =>
      (⟨x.val, x.property.2.1, x.property.2.2.1, x.property.2.2.2⟩ :
        AmbientPrimitiveShell N Q level))
    (fun _ _ h => Subtype.ext
      (congrArg (fun x : AmbientPrimitiveShell N Q level => x.val) h))

instance finite_outsidePrimitiveShell
    (L N : Submodule ℤ V) (Q : V → R) (level : R)
    [Finite (AmbientPrimitiveShell N Q level)] :
    Finite (OutsidePrimitiveShell L N Q level) :=
  Finite.of_injective
    (fun x : OutsidePrimitiveShell L N Q level =>
      (⟨x.val, x.property.1, x.property.2.1, x.property.2.2.1⟩ :
        AmbientPrimitiveShell N Q level))
    (fun _ _ h => Subtype.ext
      (congrArg (fun x : AmbientPrimitiveShell N Q level => x.val) h))

instance finite_insidePrimitiveZeroShell
    (L N : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (Q : V → R) (level : R)
    [Finite (AmbientPrimitiveShell L Q level)] :
    Finite (InsidePrimitiveZeroShell L N p Q level) :=
  Finite.of_injective
    (fun x : InsidePrimitiveZeroShell L N p Q level =>
      (⟨x.val, x.property.1, x.property.2.2.1, x.property.2.2.2.1⟩ :
        AmbientPrimitiveShell L Q level))
    (fun _ _ h => Subtype.ext
      (congrArg (fun x : AmbientPrimitiveShell L Q level => x.val) h))

theorem ambientPrimitiveShell_card_partition
    (L N : Submodule ℤ V) (Q : V → R) (level : R)
    [Finite (AmbientPrimitiveShell N Q level)] :
    Nat.card (AmbientPrimitiveShell N Q level) =
      Nat.card (InsidePrimitiveShell L N Q level) +
        Nat.card (OutsidePrimitiveShell L N Q level) := by
  rw [← Nat.card_congr (insideOutsideShellEquiv L N Q level), Nat.card_sum]

theorem insidePrimitiveShell_card_partition
    (L N M : Submodule ℤ V) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hML : M ≤ L) (hMN : M ≤ N) (hcommon : L ⊓ N ≤ M)
    (hpL : ∀ z ∈ L, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hpN : ∀ z ∈ N, (p : ℤ) • z ∈ M)
    (hcancel : Function.Injective (fun z : V => (p : ℤ) • z))
    (Q : V → R) (scale level : R) (hscale : scale ≠ 0)
    (hQ : ∀ z, Q ((p : ℤ) • z) = scale * Q z)
    [Finite (AmbientPrimitiveShell L Q (scale * level))]
    [Finite (AmbientPrimitiveShell L Q level)] :
    Nat.card (InsidePrimitiveShell L N Q (scale * level)) =
      Nat.card (InsidePrimitiveZeroShell L N p Q (scale * level)) +
        Nat.card (OutsidePrimitiveShell N L Q level) := by
  rw [← Nat.card_congr (insidePrimitiveShellEquiv
    L N M p hp hML hMN hcommon hpL hpN hcancel Q scale level hscale hQ), Nat.card_sum]

end A354766Neighbors




-- END ShellPartitions.lean

-- BEGIN MinusThreeCharacter.lean



/-!
Local arithmetic supporting the Cooper--Hirschhorn specialization.
This file does not assert the missing all-m representation-count theorem.
-/

namespace A354766Research

def residueCharacter (p : ℕ) : ℤ :=
  if p % 6 = 1 then 1 else if p % 6 = 5 then -1 else 0

theorem legendre_neg_three_eq_at_three (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) :
    legendreSym p (-3) = legendreSym 3 (p : ℤ) := by
  haveI : Fact (Nat.Prime 3) := ⟨by decide⟩
  have hodd : p % 2 = 1 :=
    (Nat.Prime.mod_two_eq_one_iff_ne_two Fact.out).mpr hp2
  have hqr : legendreSym p (3 : ℤ) =
      (-1 : ℤ) ^ (p / 2) * legendreSym 3 (p : ℤ) := by
    simpa using
      (legendreSym.quadratic_reciprocity' (p := 3) (q := p) (by decide) hp2)
  calc
    legendreSym p (-3) = legendreSym p (-1) * legendreSym p 3 := by
      rw [← legendreSym.mul]
      norm_num
    _ = (-1 : ℤ) ^ (p / 2) *
        ((-1 : ℤ) ^ (p / 2) * legendreSym 3 (p : ℤ)) := by
      rw [legendreSym.at_neg_one hp2, ZMod.χ₄_eq_neg_one_pow hodd, hqr]
    _ = legendreSym 3 (p : ℤ) := by
      rcases neg_one_pow_eq_or ℤ (p / 2) with h | h <;> simp [h]

theorem legendre_neg_three_eq_residueCharacter (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime]
    (hp2 : p ≠ 2) :
    legendreSym p (-3) = residueCharacter p := by
  haveI : Fact (Nat.Prime 3) := ⟨by decide⟩
  rw [legendre_neg_three_eq_at_three p hp2, legendreSym.mod 3 (p : ℤ),
    ← Int.natCast_mod]
  have hodd : p % 2 = 1 :=
    (Nat.Prime.mod_two_eq_one_iff_ne_two Fact.out).mpr hp2
  have h6 : p % 6 = 1 ∨ p % 6 = 3 ∨ p % 6 = 5 := by omega
  rcases h6 with h | h | h
  · have h3 : p % 3 = 1 := by omega
    norm_num [h3, residueCharacter, h]
  · have h3 : p % 3 = 0 := by omega
    norm_num [h3, residueCharacter, h]
  · have h3 : p % 3 = 2 := by omega
    norm_num [h3, residueCharacter, h]

end A354766Research



-- END MinusThreeCharacter.lean

-- BEGIN CharacterScaling.lean


/-! Character coefficients for the primitive neighbor recurrence. -/

namespace A354766Research

theorem legendre_neg_three_mul_square (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (k : ℕ) :
    legendreSym p (-3 * (k : ℤ) ^ 2) = if p ∣ k then 0 else residueCharacter p := by
  by_cases hk : p ∣ k
  · rw [if_pos hk]
    apply (legendreSym.eq_zero_iff p (-3 * (k : ℤ) ^ 2)).mpr
    have hk0 : ((k : ℤ) : ZMod p) = 0 :=
      (ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd (k : ℤ) p).mpr
        (Int.natCast_dvd_natCast.mpr hk)
    have hk0' : (k : ZMod p) = 0 := by simpa only [Int.cast_natCast] using hk0
    simp [hk0']
  · rw [if_neg hk]
    have hk0 : ((k : ℤ) : ZMod p) ≠ 0 := by
      intro h
      exact hk (Int.natCast_dvd_natCast.mp
        ((ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd (k : ℤ) p).mp h))
    rw [legendreSym.mul, legendreSym.sq_one' p hk0, mul_one,
      legendre_neg_three_eq_residueCharacter p hp2]

theorem residueCharacter_range (p : ℕ) :
    residueCharacter p = 0 ∨ residueCharacter p = 1 ∨ residueCharacter p = -1 := by
  by_cases h1 : p % 6 = 1
  · simp [residueCharacter, h1]
  · by_cases h5 : p % 6 = 5 <;> simp [residueCharacter, h1, h5]

@[simp] theorem residueCharacter_three : residueCharacter 3 = 0 := by
  norm_num [residueCharacter]

theorem legendre_neg_three_mul_square_at_three (k : ℕ) :
    legendreSym 3 (-3 * (k : ℤ) ^ 2) = 0 := by
  haveI : Fact (Nat.Prime 3) := ⟨by decide⟩
  rw [legendre_neg_three_mul_square 3 (by decide)]
  split <;> simp

end A354766Research




-- END CharacterScaling.lean

-- BEGIN PrimitiveResidue.lean




/-! Primitive standard vectors and their nonzero projective residues. -/

namespace A354766Neighbors

open A354766Research.Neighbor A354766Research.NeighborFamily
open A354766Research.IsotropicLines

theorem divisibleIn_standard_castVec_iff (q : ℕ) (v : IntVec) :
    DivisibleIn standard q (castVec v) ↔ ∃ w : IntVec, (q : ℤ) • w = v := by
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨y, ⟨w, rfl⟩, hwy⟩
    refine ⟨w, castVec_injective ?_⟩
    rw [castVec_smul]
    exact hwy
  · rintro ⟨w, hwy⟩
    refine ⟨castVec w, ⟨w, rfl⟩, ?_⟩
    rw [← castVec_smul, hwy]

theorem divisibleIn_standard_castVec_iff_coordinates (q : ℕ) (v : IntVec) :
    DivisibleIn standard q (castVec v) ↔
      (q : ℤ) ∣ v 0 ∧ (q : ℤ) ∣ v 1 ∧ (q : ℤ) ∣ v 2 := by
  rw [divisibleIn_standard_castVec_iff, scalar_divisibility_iff_coordinates]

theorem primitiveIn_standard_castVec_iff (v : IntVec) :
    PrimitiveIn standard (castVec v) ↔ vectorContent v = 1 := by
  rw [vectorContent_eq_one_iff_primitive]
  simp only [PrimitiveIn, divisibleIn_standard_castVec_iff]

theorem primitive_reduction_ne_zero (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (v : IntVec) (hv : vectorContent v = 1) : reduceVec p v ≠ 0 := by
  apply (reduceVec_ne_zero_iff p v).mpr
  by_contra hn
  have hd (i : Fin 3) : (p : ℤ) ∣ v i := by
    by_contra hi
    exact hn ⟨i, hi⟩
  exact ((vectorContent_eq_one_iff v).mp hv p hp) ⟨hd 0, hd 1, hd 2⟩

theorem primitiveIn_standard_reduction_ne_zero (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (v : IntVec) (hv : PrimitiveIn standard (castVec v)) : reduceVec p v ≠ 0 :=
  primitive_reduction_ne_zero p hp v ((primitiveIn_standard_castVec_iff v).mp hv)

/-- A vector of norm 3*(p*m)^2 is isotropic modulo p; primitivity makes its
residue a genuine projective point rather than the zero vector. -/
theorem primitive_square_residue_isotropic (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (m : ℕ)
    (v : IntVec) (hv : vectorContent v = 1)
    (hQ : vectorNorm v = ((3 * (p * m) ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℤ)) :
    reduceVec p v ≠ 0 ∧ standardNorm (ZMod p) (reduceVec p v) = 0 := by
  refine ⟨primitive_reduction_ne_zero p Fact.out v hv, ?_⟩
  have hdot : dot3 v v = ((3 * (p * m) ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℤ) := by
    simpa only [dot3, vectorNorm, pow_two] using hQ
  rw [reduceVec_norm, hdot]
  simp

theorem primitiveIn_standard_square_residue_isotropic (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (m : ℕ)
    (v : IntVec) (hv : PrimitiveIn standard (castVec v))
    (hQ : vectorNorm v = ((3 * (p * m) ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℤ)) :
    reduceVec p v ≠ 0 ∧ standardNorm (ZMod p) (reduceVec p v) = 0 :=
  primitive_square_residue_isotropic p m v ((primitiveIn_standard_castVec_iff v).mp hv) hQ

def rationalNorm (x : RatVec) : ℚ := dot3 x x

theorem rationalNorm_prime_smul (p : ℕ) (x : RatVec) :
    rationalNorm ((p : ℤ) • x) = (p : ℚ) ^ 2 * rationalNorm x := by
  simp only [rationalNorm, dot3, Pi.smul_apply, zsmul_eq_mul, Int.cast_natCast, Pi.mul_apply, Pi.intCast_def, Pi.natCast_def]
  ring

theorem rationalNorm_castVec (v : IntVec) :
    rationalNorm (castVec v) = (vectorNorm v : ℚ) := by
  simp [rationalNorm, dot3, castVec, vectorNorm, pow_two]

theorem prime_smul_ratVec_injective (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) :
    Function.Injective (fun z : RatVec => (p : ℤ) • z) := by
  intro x y h
  funext i
  have hi := congrFun h i
  change (p : ℚ) * x i = (p : ℚ) * y i at hi
  exact mul_left_cancel₀ (Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr hp.ne_zero) hi

noncomputable def familyMidpoint (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) : Submodule ℤ RatVec :=
  midpoint p (normalizedRepresentative p hp2 P)

theorem familyMidpoint_le_standard (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) : familyMidpoint p hp2 P ≤ standard :=
  midpoint_le_standard p _

theorem familyMidpoint_le_family (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) : familyMidpoint p hp2 P ≤ family p hp2 P :=
  midpoint_le_neighbor p _

theorem family_standard_inf (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) :
    standard ⊓ family p hp2 P = familyMidpoint p hp2 P := by
  apply standard_inf_neighbor p Fact.out
  · exact (reduceVec_ne_zero_iff p _).mp (normalizedRepresentative_ne_zero p hp2 P)
  · exact (dvd_pow_self (p : ℤ) (by decide : (2 : ℕ) ≠ 0)).trans
      (normalizedRepresentative_norm p hp2 P)

theorem family_p_standard (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) (z : RatVec) (hz : z ∈ standard) :
    (p : ℤ) • z ∈ familyMidpoint p hp2 P :=
  p_smul_standard_mem_midpoint p _ z hz

theorem family_p_neighbor (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) (z : RatVec) (hz : z ∈ family p hp2 P) :
    (p : ℤ) • z ∈ familyMidpoint p hp2 P := by
  apply p_smul_neighbor_mem_midpoint p (Fact.out : p.Prime).ne_zero _ _ z hz
  exact (dvd_pow_self (p : ℤ) (by decide : (2 : ℕ) ≠ 0)).trans
    (normalizedRepresentative_norm p hp2 P)

theorem family_norm_integral (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) (z : RatVec) (hz : z ∈ family p hp2 P) :
    ∃ k : ℤ, rationalNorm z = (k : ℚ) :=
  neighbor_integral p (Fact.out : p.Prime).ne_zero _
    (normalizedRepresentative_norm p hp2 P) z z hz hz


end A354766Neighbors









-- END PrimitiveResidue.lean

-- BEGIN ExteriorNeighborCount.lean


/-! The actual exterior primitive-neighbor bijection and its global count. -/

noncomputable section

open scoped BigOperators

namespace A354766Neighbors

open A354766Research.Neighbor A354766Research.NeighborFamily
open A354766Research.IsotropicLines

abbrev DivisibleVectorFiber (N : Submodule ℤ RatVec) (p n : ℕ) :=
  {v : PrimitiveVectorShell n // DivisibleIn N p (castVec v.val)}

def scaledVectorCast (N : Submodule ℤ RatVec) (p n : ℕ) :
    DivisibleVectorFiber N p n → ScaledPrimitiveShell standard N p rationalNorm (n : ℚ) :=
  fun v => ⟨castVec v.val.val, ⟨v.val.val, rfl⟩, by
    rw [rationalNorm_castVec, v.val.property.1]
    simp,
    (primitiveIn_standard_castVec_iff v.val.val).mpr v.val.property.2,
    v.property⟩

theorem scaledVectorCast_bijective (N : Submodule ℤ RatVec) (p n : ℕ) :
    Function.Bijective (scaledVectorCast N p n) := by
  constructor
  · intro v w h
    apply Subtype.ext
    apply Subtype.ext
    exact castVec_injective (congrArg Subtype.val h)
  · intro y
    obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := y.property.1
    have hQ : vectorNorm v = (n : ℤ) := by
      have hQ' : (vectorNorm v : ℚ) = (n : ℚ) := by
        rw [← rationalNorm_castVec, hv]
        exact y.property.2.1
      exact_mod_cast hQ'
    have hprim : vectorContent v = 1 :=
      (primitiveIn_standard_castVec_iff v).mp (by rw [hv]; exact y.property.2.2.1)
    refine ⟨⟨⟨v, hQ, hprim⟩, ?_⟩, ?_⟩
    · change DivisibleIn N p (castVec v)
      rw [hv]
      exact y.property.2.2.2
    · apply Subtype.ext
      exact hv

def standardScaledVectorEquiv (N : Submodule ℤ RatVec) (p n : ℕ) :
    ScaledPrimitiveShell standard N p rationalNorm (n : ℚ) ≃ DivisibleVectorFiber N p n :=
  (Equiv.ofBijective (scaledVectorCast N p n) (scaledVectorCast_bijective N p n)).symm

theorem divided_family_mem_iff (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) (v : IntVec) :
    DivisibleIn (family p hp2 P) p (castVec v) ↔ liftVec p v ∈ family p hp2 P := by
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨y, hy, hpy⟩
    have heq : y = liftVec p v :=
      prime_smul_ratVec_injective p Fact.out
        (hpy.trans (p_smul_liftVec p (Fact.out : p.Prime).ne_zero v).symm)
    rwa [← heq]
  · intro hv
    exact ⟨liftVec p v, hv, p_smul_liftVec p (Fact.out : p.Prime).ne_zero v⟩

def primitiveResidueLine (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (m : ℕ)
    (v : PrimitiveVectorShell (3 * (p * m) ^ 2)) : StandardConic (ZMod p) := by
  have h := primitive_square_residue_isotropic p m v.val v.property.2 v.property.1
  exact ⟨Projectivization.mk (ZMod p) (reduceVec p v.val) h.1,
    reduceVec p v.val, h.1, rfl, h.2⟩

theorem divisible_family_iff_residue_label (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (m : ℕ) (P : StandardConic (ZMod p))
    (v : PrimitiveVectorShell (3 * (p * m) ^ 2)) :
    DivisibleIn (family p hp2 P) p (castVec v.val) ↔ primitiveResidueLine p m v = P := by
  have h := primitive_square_residue_isotropic p m v.val v.property.2 v.property.1
  have hQ : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ dot3 v.val v.val := by
    have hd : dot3 v.val v.val = vectorNorm v.val := by
      simp only [dot3, vectorNorm, pow_two]
    rw [hd, v.property.1]
    refine ⟨3 * (m : ℤ) ^ 2, ?_⟩
    push_cast
    ring
  rw [divided_family_mem_iff, exterior_mem_family_iff p hp2 P v.val h.1 hQ]
  change Projectivization.mk (ZMod p) (reduceVec p v.val) h.1 = P.val ↔
    primitiveResidueLine p m v = P
  constructor
  · intro heq
    apply Subtype.ext
    exact heq
  · intro heq
    exact congrArg Subtype.val heq

/-- Forgetting the unique projective label is a genuine bijection. -/
def projectiveLabelFiberEquiv (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (m : ℕ) :
    (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p), DivisibleVectorFiber (family p hp2 P) p
      (3 * (p * m) ^ 2)) ≃ PrimitiveVectorShell (3 * (p * m) ^ 2) := by
  apply Equiv.ofBijective (fun x => x.2.val)
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨P, v, hv⟩ ⟨Q, w, hw⟩ h
    change v = w at h
    cases h
    have hPQ : P = Q :=
      ((divisible_family_iff_residue_label p hp2 m P v).mp hv).symm.trans
        ((divisible_family_iff_residue_label p hp2 m Q v).mp hw)
    subst Q
    rfl
  · intro v
    exact ⟨⟨primitiveResidueLine p m v,
      ⟨v, (divisible_family_iff_residue_label p hp2 m (primitiveResidueLine p m v) v).mpr rfl⟩⟩,
      rfl⟩

theorem prime_square_level (p m : ℕ) :
    (p : ℚ) ^ 2 * (3 * (m : ℚ) ^ 2) = ((3 * (p * m) ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ) := by
  push_cast
  ring

def outsideFamilyScaleEquiv (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (m : ℕ)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) :
    OutsidePrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm (3 * (m : ℚ) ^ 2) ≃
      ScaledPrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P) p rationalNorm
        ((3 * (p * m) ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ) := by
  have hscale : (p : ℚ) ^ 2 ≠ 0 :=
    pow_ne_zero 2 (Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr (Fact.out : p.Prime).ne_zero)
  have e := outsidePrimitiveShellEquiv standard (family p hp2 P) (familyMidpoint p hp2 P)
    p Fact.out (familyMidpoint_le_standard p hp2 P) (familyMidpoint_le_family p hp2 P)
    (family_p_standard p hp2 P) (family_p_neighbor p hp2 P)
    (prime_smul_ratVec_injective p Fact.out) rationalNorm ((p : ℚ) ^ 2)
    (3 * (m : ℚ) ^ 2) hscale (rationalNorm_prime_smul p)
  simpa only [prime_square_level p m] using e

/-- Every primitive vector at the scaled norm appears at exactly one actual
neighbor, with inverse obtained by division by p. -/
def outside_family_equiv (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (m : ℕ) :
    (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p),
      OutsidePrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm (3 * (m : ℚ) ^ 2)) ≃
        PrimitiveVectorShell (3 * (p * m) ^ 2) :=
  (Equiv.sigmaCongrRight fun P =>
    (outsideFamilyScaleEquiv p hp2 m P).trans
      (standardScaledVectorEquiv (family p hp2 P) p (3 * (p * m) ^ 2))).trans
    (projectiveLabelFiberEquiv p hp2 m)

theorem outside_family_card (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (m : ℕ) :
    Nat.card (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p),
      OutsidePrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm (3 * (m : ℚ) ^ 2)) =
        A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * (p * m) ^ 2) :=
  (Nat.card_congr (outside_family_equiv p hp2 m)).trans
    (primitiveVectorShell_card (3 * (p * m) ^ 2))

theorem outside_family_sum_card (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (m : ℕ)
    [Fintype (StandardConic (ZMod p))] :
    (∑ P : StandardConic (ZMod p),
      Nat.card (OutsidePrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm
        (3 * (m : ℚ) ^ 2))) = A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * (p * m) ^ 2) := by
  let S := Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p),
    OutsidePrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm (3 * (m : ℚ) ^ 2)
  letI : Finite S := Finite.of_equiv (PrimitiveVectorShell (3 * (p * m) ^ 2))
    (outside_family_equiv p hp2 m).symm
  haveI (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) :
      Finite (OutsidePrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm
        (3 * (m : ℚ) ^ 2)) :=
    Finite.of_injective (fun x => (⟨P, x⟩ : S)) (by intro x y h; cases h; rfl)
  rw [← Nat.card_sigma]
  exact outside_family_card p hp2 m

end A354766Neighbors






-- END ExteriorNeighborCount.lean

-- BEGIN EulerProductFromRecurrence.lean






/-!
# Euler product from the explicit odd-prime recurrences

The hypotheses describe only recurrences and the normalization at one.
No Euler product or multiplicativity assumption is made.  In fact the proof
works for an arbitrary integer-valued `χ`, without restrictions on its values.
-/

namespace A354766Research

/-- The structural input to be supplied by the lattice/counting argument. -/
structure OddPrimeRecurrences (F χ : ℕ → ℤ) : Prop where
  at_one : F 1 = 8
  first_step : ∀ (p b : ℕ), p.Prime → Odd p → 0 < b → Odd b → ¬ p ∣ b →
    F (p * b) = ((p : ℤ) - χ p) * F b
  repeated_step : ∀ (p m : ℕ), p.Prime → Odd p → 0 < m → Odd m → p ∣ m →
    F (p * m) = ((p : ℤ) + 1) * F m -
      (if p ∣ m / p then (p : ℤ) else (p : ℤ) - χ p) * F (m / p)

/-- Pure algebra: the exceptional second term determines every later term. -/
theorem sequence_from_recurrence (A : ℕ → ℤ) (q c : ℤ)
    (h₁ : A 1 = (q - c) * A 0)
    (h₂ : A 2 = (q + 1) * A 1 - (q - c) * A 0)
    (hrec : ∀ n : ℕ, A (n + 3) = (q + 1) * A (n + 2) - q * A (n + 1))
    (n : ℕ) : A (n + 1) = q ^ n * (q - c) * A 0 := by
  induction n using Nat.twoStepInduction with
  | zero => simpa using h₁
  | one => rw [h₂, h₁]; ring
  | more n hn hn₁ =>
    rw [show n + 2 + 1 = n + 3 by omega, hrec, hn₁, hn]
    ring

/-- A prime-power multiple, with the exponent written as `n+1`. -/
theorem prime_power_mul_succ {F χ : ℕ → ℤ} (h : OddPrimeRecurrences F χ)
    {p b : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime) (hpo : Odd p) (hb : 0 < b) (hbo : Odd b)
    (hpb : ¬ p ∣ b) (n : ℕ) :
    F (p ^ (n + 1) * b) = (p : ℤ) ^ n * ((p : ℤ) - χ p) * F b := by
  have hdiv (k : ℕ) : (p ^ (k + 1) * b) / p = p ^ k * b := by
    rw [pow_succ', mul_assoc, Nat.mul_div_cancel_left _ hp.pos]
  have hpos (k : ℕ) : 0 < p ^ k * b := mul_pos (pow_pos hp.pos _) hb
  have hodd (k : ℕ) : Odd (p ^ k * b) := (hpo.pow).mul hbo
  have hdvd (k : ℕ) : p ∣ p ^ (k + 1) * b := by
    rw [pow_succ', mul_assoc]
    exact dvd_mul_right _ _
  have hmul (k : ℕ) : p * (p ^ k * b) = p ^ (k + 1) * b := by
    rw [pow_succ', mul_assoc]
  have h₁ : F (p ^ 1 * b) = ((p : ℤ) - χ p) * F (p ^ 0 * b) := by
    simpa using h.first_step p b hp hpo hb hbo hpb
  have h₂ : F (p ^ 2 * b) = ((p : ℤ) + 1) * F (p ^ 1 * b) -
      ((p : ℤ) - χ p) * F (p ^ 0 * b) := by
    have hr := h.repeated_step p (p ^ 1 * b) hp hpo (hpos 1) (hodd 1) (hdvd 0)
    simpa only [hmul, hdiv, pow_zero, one_mul, if_neg hpb] using hr
  have hr (k : ℕ) : F (p ^ (k + 3) * b) =
      ((p : ℤ) + 1) * F (p ^ (k + 2) * b) - (p : ℤ) * F (p ^ (k + 1) * b) := by
    have hr := h.repeated_step p (p ^ (k + 2) * b) hp hpo
      (hpos (k + 2)) (hodd (k + 2)) (hdvd (k + 1))
    simpa only [hmul, hdiv, if_pos (hdvd k)] using hr
  simpa using sequence_from_recurrence (fun k => F (p ^ k * b)) (p : ℤ) (χ p)
    h₁ h₂ hr n

/-- Closed form on a prime-power multiple, for any positive exponent. -/
theorem prime_power_mul {F χ : ℕ → ℤ} (h : OddPrimeRecurrences F χ)
    {p b a : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime) (hpo : Odd p) (hb : 0 < b) (hbo : Odd b)
    (hpb : ¬ p ∣ b) (ha : 1 ≤ a) :
    F (p ^ a * b) = (p : ℤ) ^ (a - 1) * ((p : ℤ) - χ p) * F b := by
  obtain ⟨n, rfl⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_succ_of_ne_zero (by omega : a ≠ 0)
  simpa using prime_power_mul_succ h hp hpo hb hbo hpb n

/-- The Euler product, directly from the recurrence hypotheses. -/
theorem euler_product {F χ : ℕ → ℤ} (h : OddPrimeRecurrences F χ)
    {m : ℕ} (hm : 0 < m) (hmo : Odd m) :
    F m = 8 * ∏ p ∈ m.primeFactors,
      ((p : ℤ) ^ (m.factorization p - 1) * ((p : ℤ) - χ p)) := by
  classical
  suffices hprod : ∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → Odd n →
      F n = 8 * n.factorization.prod
        (fun p a => (p : ℤ) ^ (a - 1) * ((p : ℤ) - χ p)) by
    exact hprod m hm hmo
  apply Nat.recOnPrimePow
  · intro hzero _
    omega
  · intro _ _
    simpa using h.at_one
  · intro b p a hp hpb ha ih hpos hodd
    have hb : 0 < b := Nat.pos_of_mul_pos_left hpos
    have hbo : Odd b := (Nat.odd_mul.mp hodd).2
    have hpo : Odd p := (Nat.odd_pow_iff (by omega : a ≠ 0)).mp
      (Nat.odd_mul.mp hodd).1
    have hcop : (p ^ a).Coprime b :=
      ((hp.coprime_iff_not_dvd).mpr hpb).pow_left a
    rw [prime_power_mul h hp hpo hb hbo hpb ha, ih hb hbo,
      Nat.factorization_mul (pow_ne_zero _ hp.ne_zero) hb.ne',
      Finsupp.prod_add_index_of_disjoint hcop.disjoint_primeFactors,
      hp.factorization_pow]
    have hsingle : (Finsupp.single p a).prod
        (fun q k => (q : ℤ) ^ (k - 1) * ((q : ℤ) - χ q)) =
        (p : ℤ) ^ (a - 1) * ((p : ℤ) - χ p) := by
      simp [Finsupp.prod, Finsupp.support_single_ne_zero p (by omega : a ≠ 0)]
    rw [hsingle]
    ring

/-- The equivalent rational Euler product, with nonzero denominators proved
from membership in `m.primeFactors`. -/
theorem euler_product_rat {F χ : ℕ → ℤ} (h : OddPrimeRecurrences F χ)
    {m : ℕ} (hm : 0 < m) (hmo : Odd m) :
    (F m : ℚ) = 8 * (m : ℚ) *
      ∏ p ∈ m.primeFactors, (1 - (χ p : ℚ) / (p : ℚ)) := by
  classical
  have hcast : (F m : ℚ) = 8 * ∏ p ∈ m.primeFactors,
      ((p : ℚ) ^ (m.factorization p - 1) * ((p : ℚ) - (χ p : ℚ))) := by
    exact_mod_cast euler_product h hm hmo
  have hpow : (∏ p ∈ m.primeFactors, (p : ℚ) ^ m.factorization p) = (m : ℚ) := by
    have hnat := Nat.factorization_prod_pow_eq_self hm.ne'
    change (∏ p ∈ m.primeFactors, p ^ m.factorization p) = m at hnat
    exact_mod_cast hnat
  have hfactor (p : ℕ) (hpm : p ∈ m.primeFactors) :
      (p : ℚ) ^ (m.factorization p - 1) * ((p : ℚ) - (χ p : ℚ)) =
      (p : ℚ) ^ m.factorization p * (1 - (χ p : ℚ) / (p : ℚ)) := by
    have hp : p.Prime := Nat.prime_of_mem_primeFactors hpm
    have hp0 : (p : ℚ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hp.ne_zero
    have ha : 1 ≤ m.factorization p :=
      hp.factorization_pos_of_dvd hm.ne' (Nat.dvd_of_mem_primeFactors hpm)
    have hc : (p : ℚ) * (1 - (χ p : ℚ) / (p : ℚ)) = (p : ℚ) - (χ p : ℚ) := by
      rw [mul_sub, mul_one, mul_div_cancel₀ _ hp0]
    calc
      _ = (p : ℚ) ^ (m.factorization p - 1) *
          ((p : ℚ) * (1 - (χ p : ℚ) / (p : ℚ))) := by rw [hc]
      _ = _ := by rw [← mul_assoc, ← pow_succ, Nat.sub_add_cancel ha]
  calc
    (F m : ℚ) = 8 * ∏ p ∈ m.primeFactors,
        ((p : ℚ) ^ m.factorization p * (1 - (χ p : ℚ) / (p : ℚ))) := by
      rw [hcast]
      congr 1
      exact Finset.prod_congr rfl hfactor
    _ = 8 * (m : ℚ) * ∏ p ∈ m.primeFactors, (1 - (χ p : ℚ) / (p : ℚ)) := by
      rw [Finset.prod_mul_distrib, hpow, mul_assoc]

end A354766Research




-- END EulerProductFromRecurrence.lean

namespace A354766Research

set_option maxRecDepth 8000 in
set_option maxHeartbeats 2000000 in
theorem primitive_three_base_kernel : A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite 3 = 8 := by
  decide
