-- BEGIN NeighborFiberCounts.lean






/-!
# The concrete primitive-neighbor double count

The algebraic primitive-vector lemmas are in `PrimitiveNeighborAlgebra`.
This file connects the proved normalized projective neighbor family with the
finite conic counts and the norm-preserving integer shell model. No recurrence
or representation formula is assumed as an input.
-/

namespace A354766Neighbors

open A354766Research.Neighbor A354766Research.NeighborFamily
open A354766Research.IsotropicLines

noncomputable def familyMembershipEquiv (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (z : IntVec) :
    {P : StandardConic (ZMod p) // castVec z ∈ family p hp2 P} ≃
      StandardPerpConic (ZMod p) (reduceVec p z) :=
  Equiv.subtypeEquivRight (fun P => inside_mem_family_iff p hp2 P z)

theorem family_membership_card (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (z : IntVec) (hz : reduceVec p z ≠ 0) :
    (Nat.card {P : StandardConic (ZMod p) // castVec z ∈ family p hp2 P} : ℤ) =
      quadraticChar (ZMod p) (-standardNorm (ZMod p) (reduceVec p z)) + 1 := by
  rw [Nat.card_congr (familyMembershipEquiv p hp2 z)]
  exact card_standardPerpConic_zmod p hp2 (reduceVec p z) hz

/-- The exact content-p fiber, using the actual constructed projective family. -/
theorem family_inside_one_card (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (z : IntVec) (hz : vectorContent z = 1) :
    (Nat.card {P : StandardConic (ZMod p) //
      castVec ((p : ℤ) • z) ∈ family p hp2 P ∧
        PrimitiveIn (family p hp2 P) (castVec ((p : ℤ) • z))} : ℤ) =
      (p : ℤ) - quadraticChar (ZMod p) (-standardNorm (ZMod p) (reduceVec p z)) := by
  classical
  let N := family p hp2
  let M := familyMidpoint p hp2
  have hg := card_inside_one_fiber standard N M p (Fact.out : p.Prime)
    (familyMidpoint_le_standard p hp2) (familyMidpoint_le_family p hp2)
    (family_p_standard p hp2) (family_p_neighbor p hp2)
    (prime_smul_ratVec_injective p Fact.out) (castVec z) ⟨z, rfl⟩
    ((primitiveIn_standard_castVec_iff z).mpr hz)
  rw [← castVec_smul] at hg
  have hle : Nat.card {P : StandardConic (ZMod p) // castVec z ∈ N P} ≤
      Nat.card (StandardConic (ZMod p)) :=
    Nat.card_le_card_of_injective Subtype.val Subtype.val_injective
  have hsum : Nat.card {P : StandardConic (ZMod p) //
      castVec ((p : ℤ) • z) ∈ N P ∧ PrimitiveIn (N P) (castVec ((p : ℤ) • z))} +
      Nat.card {P : StandardConic (ZMod p) // castVec z ∈ N P} = p + 1 := by
    rw [hg, Nat.sub_add_cancel hle, card_standardConic_zmod p hp2]
  have hsumZ : (Nat.card {P : StandardConic (ZMod p) //
      castVec ((p : ℤ) • z) ∈ N P ∧ PrimitiveIn (N P) (castVec ((p : ℤ) • z))} : ℤ) +
      (Nat.card {P : StandardConic (ZMod p) // castVec z ∈ N P} : ℤ) = (p : ℤ) + 1 := by
    exact_mod_cast hsum
  have hm := family_membership_card p hp2 z (primitive_reduction_ne_zero p Fact.out z hz)
  change (Nat.card {P : StandardConic (ZMod p) //
      castVec ((p : ℤ) • z) ∈ N P ∧ PrimitiveIn (N P) (castVec ((p : ℤ) • z))} : ℤ) = _
  linear_combination hsumZ - hm

def isotropicPoint (F : Type*) [Field F] (x : Ternary F) (hx : x ≠ 0)
    (hQ : standardNorm F x = 0) : StandardConic F :=
  ⟨Projectivization.mk F x hx, x, hx, rfl, hQ⟩

theorem isotropicPoint_perp_self (F : Type*) [Field F] (x : Ternary F) (hx : x ≠ 0)
    (hQ : standardNorm F x = 0) :
    (isotropicPoint F x hx hQ).val.submodule ≤ (standardDot F x).ker := by
  change (Projectivization.mk F x hx).submodule ≤ (standardDot F x).ker
  rw [Projectivization.submodule_mk, Submodule.span_singleton_le_iff_mem, LinearMap.mem_ker]
  simpa only [standardDot, standardNorm, LinearMap.coe_mk, AddHom.coe_mk, pow_two] using hQ

theorem unique_isotropic_perp (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (x : Ternary (ZMod p)) (hx : x ≠ 0) (hQ : standardNorm (ZMod p) x = 0)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p))
    (hP : P.val.submodule ≤ (standardDot (ZMod p) x).ker) :
    P.val = Projectivization.mk (ZMod p) x hx := by
  classical
  have hcZ := card_standardPerpConic_zmod p hp2 x hx
  rw [hQ, neg_zero, quadraticChar_zero, zero_add] at hcZ
  have hc : Nat.card (StandardPerpConic (ZMod p) x) = 1 := by exact_mod_cast hcZ
  have hs : Subsingleton (StandardPerpConic (ZMod p) x) :=
    (Nat.card_eq_one_iff_unique.mp hc).1
  have he := hs.elim (⟨P, hP⟩ : StandardPerpConic (ZMod p) x)
    ⟨isotropicPoint (ZMod p) x hx hQ, isotropicPoint_perp_self (ZMod p) x hx hQ⟩
  exact congrArg (fun q : StandardPerpConic (ZMod p) x => q.val.val) he

theorem dot3_int_eq_vectorNorm (z : IntVec) : dot3 z z = vectorNorm z := by
  simp only [dot3, vectorNorm, pow_two]

/-- At level 3*m² with p|m, an inside standard-primitive vector is never
primitive in a neighbor. Its unique perpendicular isotropic line forces
actual p-division, using the proved exterior membership theorem at norm p². -/
theorem family_inside_zero_div_not_primitive
    (p m : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (hpm : p ∣ m)
    (z : IntVec) (hz : vectorContent z = 1)
    (hzQ : vectorNorm z = ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℤ))
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) (hzN : castVec z ∈ family p hp2 P) :
    ¬ PrimitiveIn (family p hp2 P) (castVec z) := by
  have hred := primitive_reduction_ne_zero p Fact.out z hz
  have hQp2 : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ dot3 z z := by
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := hpm
    rw [dot3_int_eq_vectorNorm, hzQ, hk]
    refine ⟨3 * (k : ℤ) ^ 2, ?_⟩
    push_cast
    ring
  have hQp : (p : ℤ) ∣ dot3 z z :=
    (dvd_pow_self (p : ℤ) (by decide : (2 : ℕ) ≠ 0)).trans hQp2
  have hQred : standardNorm (ZMod p) (reduceVec p z) = 0 := by
    rw [reduceVec_norm]
    exact (ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd (dot3 z z) p).mpr hQp
  have hproj : P.val = Projectivization.mk (ZMod p) (reduceVec p z) hred :=
    unique_isotropic_perp p hp2 (reduceVec p z) hred hQred P
      ((inside_mem_family_iff p hp2 P z).mp hzN)
  have hyN : liftVec p z ∈ family p hp2 P :=
    (exterior_mem_family_iff p hp2 P z hred hQp2).mpr hproj.symm
  intro hprim
  exact hprim p Fact.out ⟨liftVec p z, hyN, p_smul_liftVec p (Fact.out : p.Prime).ne_zero z⟩

theorem family_inside_zero_div_card
    (p m : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (hpm : p ∣ m)
    (z : IntVec) (hz : vectorContent z = 1)
    (hzQ : vectorNorm z = ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℤ)) :
    Nat.card {P : StandardConic (ZMod p) // castVec z ∈ family p hp2 P ∧
      PrimitiveIn (family p hp2 P) (castVec z)} = 0 := by
  letI : IsEmpty {P : StandardConic (ZMod p) // castVec z ∈ family p hp2 P ∧
      PrimitiveIn (family p hp2 P) (castVec z)} :=
    ⟨fun P => family_inside_zero_div_not_primitive p m hp2 hpm z hz hzQ P.val
      P.property.1 P.property.2⟩
  exact Nat.card_of_isEmpty

/-- The content-one fiber for p∤m, including p=3. -/
theorem family_inside_zero_nondiv_card
    (p m : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (hpm : ¬ p ∣ m)
    (z : IntVec) (hz : vectorContent z = 1)
    (hzQ : vectorNorm z = ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℤ)) :
    (Nat.card {P : StandardConic (ZMod p) // castVec z ∈ family p hp2 P ∧
      PrimitiveIn (family p hp2 P) (castVec z)} : ℤ) =
      quadraticChar (ZMod p) (-standardNorm (ZMod p) (reduceVec p z)) + 1 := by
  have hzQrat : rationalNorm (castVec z) = ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ) := by
    rw [rationalNorm_castVec, hzQ]
    simp only [Int.cast_natCast]
  let e := insideZeroNondivFiberEquiv standard (family p hp2) (familyMidpoint p hp2)
    p m (Fact.out : p.Prime) hpm (familyMidpoint_le_standard p hp2)
    (familyMidpoint_le_family p hp2) (fun P => (family_standard_inf p hp2 P).le)
    (family_p_standard p hp2) (family_p_neighbor p hp2)
    rationalNorm (rationalNorm_prime_smul p) (family_norm_integral p hp2)
    (castVec z) ⟨z, rfl⟩ ((primitiveIn_standard_castVec_iff z).mpr hz) hzQrat
  rw [Nat.card_congr e]
  exact family_membership_card p hp2 z (primitive_reduction_ne_zero p Fact.out z hz)

theorem character_of_vector_norm (p k : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime]
    (z : IntVec) (hzQ : vectorNorm z = ((3 * k ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℤ)) :
    quadraticChar (ZMod p) (-standardNorm (ZMod p) (reduceVec p z)) =
      legendreSym p (-3 * (k : ℤ) ^ 2) := by
  have harg : -standardNorm (ZMod p) (reduceVec p z) =
      ((-3 * (k : ℤ) ^ 2 : ℤ) : ZMod p) := by
    rw [reduceVec_norm, dot3_int_eq_vectorNorm, hzQ]
    push_cast
    ring
  exact congrArg (quadraticChar (ZMod p)) harg

theorem family_inside_one_card_at_level (p k : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (z : IntVec) (hz : vectorContent z = 1)
    (hzQ : vectorNorm z = ((3 * k ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℤ)) :
    (Nat.card {P : StandardConic (ZMod p) //
      castVec ((p : ℤ) • z) ∈ family p hp2 P ∧
        PrimitiveIn (family p hp2 P) (castVec ((p : ℤ) • z))} : ℤ) =
      (p : ℤ) - (if p ∣ k then 0 else A354766Research.residueCharacter p) := by
  rw [family_inside_one_card p hp2 z hz, character_of_vector_norm p k z hzQ,
    A354766Research.legendre_neg_three_mul_square p hp2 k]

theorem family_inside_zero_nondiv_card_at_level
    (p m : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (hpm : ¬ p ∣ m)
    (z : IntVec) (hz : vectorContent z = 1)
    (hzQ : vectorNorm z = ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℤ)) :
    (Nat.card {P : StandardConic (ZMod p) // castVec z ∈ family p hp2 P ∧
      PrimitiveIn (family p hp2 P) (castVec z)} : ℤ) =
      A354766Research.residueCharacter p + 1 := by
  rw [family_inside_zero_nondiv_card p m hp2 hpm z hz hzQ,
    character_of_vector_norm p m z hzQ,
    A354766Research.legendre_neg_three_mul_square p hp2 m, if_neg hpm]

end A354766Neighbors

#print axioms A354766Neighbors.family_inside_one_card
#print axioms A354766Neighbors.family_inside_zero_div_card
#print axioms A354766Neighbors.family_inside_one_card_at_level
#print axioms A354766Neighbors.family_inside_zero_nondiv_card_at_level

-- BEGIN InsideNeighborSums.lean




/-!
Global inside counts, obtained by an explicit reordering of the finite
neighbor/vector incidence set. Every fiber coefficient is supplied by the
proved concrete family lemmas; no representation recurrence is assumed.
-/

noncomputable section

open scoped BigOperators

namespace A354766Neighbors

open A354766Research.Neighbor A354766Research.NeighborFamily
open A354766Research.IsotropicLines

/-- Reorder an incidence set without changing any membership condition. -/
def sigmaFiberSwap {A B : Type*} (R : A → B → Prop) :
    (Σ a : A, {b : B // R a b}) ≃ Σ b : B, {a : A // R a b} where
  toFun x := ⟨x.2.val, x.1, x.2.property⟩
  invFun x := ⟨x.2.val, x.1, x.2.property⟩
  left_inv _ := rfl
  right_inv _ := rfl

/-- Count a family from its actual constant vector fibers. -/
theorem sigma_card_from_constant_fibers
    {A B : Type*} [Finite A] [Finite B]
    (T : A → Type*) [∀ a, Finite (T a)] (R : A → B → Prop) (k : ℤ)
    (hT : ∀ a, Nat.card (T a) = Nat.card {b : B // R a b})
    (hR : ∀ b, (Nat.card {a : A // R a b} : ℤ) = k) :
    (Nat.card (Σ a, T a) : ℤ) = k * (Nat.card B : ℤ) := by
  classical
  letI : Fintype A := Fintype.ofFinite A
  letI : Fintype B := Fintype.ofFinite B
  have hcards : Nat.card (Σ a, T a) = Nat.card (Σ a : A, {b : B // R a b}) := by
    rw [Nat.card_sigma, Nat.card_sigma]
    exact Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun a _ => hT a)
  rw [hcards, Nat.card_congr (sigmaFiberSwap R), Nat.card_sigma, Nat.cast_sum]
  calc
    (∑ b : B, (Nat.card {a : A // R a b} : ℤ)) = ∑ _ : B, k :=
      Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun b _ => hR b)
    _ = k * (Nat.card B : ℤ) := by
      simp [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, mul_comm]

def sumsStandardShellCast (n : ℕ) : PrimitiveVectorShell n →
    AmbientPrimitiveShell standard rationalNorm (n : ℚ) := fun v =>
  ⟨castVec v.val, ⟨v.val, rfl⟩, by
    rw [rationalNorm_castVec, v.property.1]
    simp only [Int.cast_natCast],
    (primitiveIn_standard_castVec_iff v.val).mpr v.property.2⟩

theorem sumsStandardShellCast_bijective (n : ℕ) :
    Function.Bijective (sumsStandardShellCast n) := by
  constructor
  · intro x y h
    exact Subtype.ext (castVec_injective (congrArg Subtype.val h))
  · rintro ⟨x, ⟨v, rfl⟩, hQ, hprim⟩
    have hvQ : vectorNorm v = (n : ℤ) := by
      rw [rationalNorm_castVec] at hQ
      exact_mod_cast hQ
    exact ⟨⟨v, hvQ, (primitiveIn_standard_castVec_iff v).mp hprim⟩, rfl⟩

def sumsStandardShellCastEquiv (n : ℕ) : PrimitiveVectorShell n ≃
    AmbientPrimitiveShell standard rationalNorm (n : ℚ) :=
  Equiv.ofBijective (sumsStandardShellCast n) (sumsStandardShellCast_bijective n)

theorem sums_standard_shell_finite (n : ℕ) :
    Finite (AmbientPrimitiveShell standard rationalNorm (n : ℚ)) :=
  Finite.of_equiv (PrimitiveVectorShell n) (sumsStandardShellCastEquiv n)

abbrev SumsStandardInside (N : Submodule ℤ RatVec) (n : ℕ) :=
  {x : RatVec // x ∈ standard ∧ x ∈ N ∧ rationalNorm x = (n : ℚ) ∧
    PrimitiveIn standard x ∧ PrimitiveIn N x}

def sumsInsideCast (N : Submodule ℤ RatVec) (n : ℕ) :
    {z : PrimitiveVectorShell n // castVec z.val ∈ N ∧ PrimitiveIn N (castVec z.val)} →
      SumsStandardInside N n := fun z =>
  ⟨castVec z.val.val, ⟨z.val.val, rfl⟩, z.property.1, by
    rw [rationalNorm_castVec, z.val.property.1]
    simp only [Int.cast_natCast],
    (primitiveIn_standard_castVec_iff z.val.val).mpr z.val.property.2, z.property.2⟩

theorem sumsInsideCast_bijective (N : Submodule ℤ RatVec) (n : ℕ) :
    Function.Bijective (sumsInsideCast N n) := by
  constructor
  · intro x y h
    apply Subtype.ext
    apply Subtype.ext
    exact castVec_injective (congrArg Subtype.val h)
  · rintro ⟨x, ⟨v, rfl⟩, hvN, hQ, hprim, hprimN⟩
    have hvQ : vectorNorm v = (n : ℤ) := by
      rw [rationalNorm_castVec] at hQ
      exact_mod_cast hQ
    exact ⟨⟨⟨v, hvQ, (primitiveIn_standard_castVec_iff v).mp hprim⟩, hvN, hprimN⟩, rfl⟩

def sumsInsideCastEquiv (N : Submodule ℤ RatVec) (n : ℕ) :
    {z : PrimitiveVectorShell n // castVec z.val ∈ N ∧ PrimitiveIn N (castVec z.val)} ≃
      SumsStandardInside N n :=
  Equiv.ofBijective (sumsInsideCast N n) (sumsInsideCast_bijective N n)

def sumsOutsideCast (N : Submodule ℤ RatVec) (n : ℕ) :
    {z : PrimitiveVectorShell n // castVec z.val ∉ N} →
      OutsidePrimitiveShell N standard rationalNorm (n : ℚ) := fun z =>
  ⟨castVec z.val.val, ⟨z.val.val, rfl⟩, by
    rw [rationalNorm_castVec, z.val.property.1]
    simp only [Int.cast_natCast],
    (primitiveIn_standard_castVec_iff z.val.val).mpr z.val.property.2, z.property⟩

theorem sumsOutsideCast_bijective (N : Submodule ℤ RatVec) (n : ℕ) :
    Function.Bijective (sumsOutsideCast N n) := by
  constructor
  · intro x y h
    apply Subtype.ext
    apply Subtype.ext
    exact castVec_injective (congrArg Subtype.val h)
  · rintro ⟨x, ⟨v, rfl⟩, hQ, hprim, hnot⟩
    have hvQ : vectorNorm v = (n : ℤ) := by
      rw [rationalNorm_castVec] at hQ
      exact_mod_cast hQ
    exact ⟨⟨⟨v, hvQ, (primitiveIn_standard_castVec_iff v).mp hprim⟩, hnot⟩, rfl⟩

def sumsOutsideCastEquiv (N : Submodule ℤ RatVec) (n : ℕ) :
    {z : PrimitiveVectorShell n // castVec z.val ∉ N} ≃
      OutsidePrimitiveShell N standard rationalNorm (n : ℚ) :=
  Equiv.ofBijective (sumsOutsideCast N n) (sumsOutsideCast_bijective N n)

def sumsZeroInsideEquiv (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) (n : ℕ) :
    InsidePrimitiveZeroShell standard (family p hp2 P) p rationalNorm (n : ℚ) ≃
      SumsStandardInside (family p hp2 P) n :=
  Equiv.subtypeEquivRight (fun x => by
    constructor
    · rintro ⟨hxL, hxN, hxQ, hprimL, hnot⟩
      refine ⟨hxL, hxN, hxQ, hprimL, ?_⟩
      exact (inside_primitive_iff_not_divisible standard (family p hp2 P)
        (familyMidpoint p hp2 P) p Fact.out (familyMidpoint_le_standard p hp2 P)
        (familyMidpoint_le_family p hp2 P) (family_p_standard p hp2 P)
        (family_p_neighbor p hp2 P) x
        ((family_standard_inf p hp2 P).le ⟨hxL, hxN⟩) hprimL).mpr hnot
    · rintro ⟨hxL, hxN, hxQ, hprimL, hprimN⟩
      exact ⟨hxL, hxN, hxQ, hprimL, hprimN p Fact.out⟩)

theorem sums_inside_nondiv_standard_primitive
    (p m : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (hpm : ¬ p ∣ m)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) (x : RatVec)
    (hxL : x ∈ standard) (hxN : x ∈ family p hp2 P)
    (hxQ : rationalNorm x = ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ))
    (hx : PrimitiveIn (family p hp2 P) x) : PrimitiveIn standard x := by
  have hnot : ¬ DivisibleIn standard p x :=
    not_divisibleIn_of_norm_three_mul_sq standard p m Fact.out hpm rationalNorm
      (rationalNorm_prime_smul p) (by
        rintro y ⟨z, rfl⟩
        exact ⟨vectorNorm z, rationalNorm_castVec z⟩) x hxQ
  rcases inside_primitive_cases standard (family p hp2 P) (familyMidpoint p hp2 P)
    p Fact.out (familyMidpoint_le_standard p hp2 P) (familyMidpoint_le_family p hp2 P)
    (family_standard_inf p hp2 P).le (family_p_standard p hp2 P)
    (family_p_neighbor p hp2 P) x hxL hxN hx with h | ⟨z, hz, _, heq, _⟩
  · exact h
  · exact False.elim (hnot ⟨z, hz, heq.symm⟩)

def sumsNondivInsideEquiv
    (p m : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (hpm : ¬ p ∣ m)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) :
    InsidePrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ) ≃
      SumsStandardInside (family p hp2 P) (3 * m ^ 2) :=
  Equiv.subtypeEquivRight (fun x => by
    constructor
    · rintro ⟨hxL, hxN, hxQ, hx⟩
      exact ⟨hxL, hxN, hxQ,
        sums_inside_nondiv_standard_primitive p m hp2 hpm P x hxL hxN hxQ hx, hx⟩
    · rintro ⟨hxL, hxN, hxQ, _, hx⟩
      exact ⟨hxL, hxN, hxQ, hx⟩)

theorem sums_family_shell_finite (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) (n : ℕ) :
    Finite (AmbientPrimitiveShell (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm (n : ℚ)) :=
  actual_neighbor_shell_finite p Fact.out (normalizedRepresentative p hp2 P)
    ((reduceVec_ne_zero_iff p _).mp (normalizedRepresentative_ne_zero p hp2 P))
    (normalizedRepresentative_norm p hp2 P) n

theorem inside_family_card_of_not_dvd
    (p m : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (hpm : ¬ p ∣ m) :
    (Nat.card (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p),
      InsidePrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm
        ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)) : ℤ) =
      (A354766Research.residueCharacter p + 1) *
        (A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * m ^ 2) : ℤ) := by
  classical
  letI (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) :
      Finite (AmbientPrimitiveShell (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm
        ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)) := sums_family_shell_finite p hp2 P _
  have h := sigma_card_from_constant_fibers
    (fun P : StandardConic (ZMod p) =>
      InsidePrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ))
    (fun P (z : PrimitiveVectorShell (3 * m ^ 2)) =>
      castVec z.val ∈ family p hp2 P ∧ PrimitiveIn (family p hp2 P) (castVec z.val))
    (A354766Research.residueCharacter p + 1)
    (fun P => Nat.card_congr ((sumsNondivInsideEquiv p m hp2 hpm P).trans
      (sumsInsideCastEquiv (family p hp2 P) _).symm))
    (fun z => family_inside_zero_nondiv_card_at_level p m hp2 hpm z.val
      z.property.2 z.property.1)
  simpa only [primitiveVectorShell_card] using h

theorem sums_zero_family_card_of_dvd
    (p m : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (hpm : p ∣ m) :
    (Nat.card (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p),
      InsidePrimitiveZeroShell standard (family p hp2 P) p rationalNorm
        ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)) : ℤ) = 0 := by
  classical
  letI : Finite (AmbientPrimitiveShell standard rationalNorm ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)) :=
    sums_standard_shell_finite _
  have h := sigma_card_from_constant_fibers
    (fun P : StandardConic (ZMod p) =>
      InsidePrimitiveZeroShell standard (family p hp2 P) p rationalNorm
        ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ))
    (fun P (z : PrimitiveVectorShell (3 * m ^ 2)) =>
      castVec z.val ∈ family p hp2 P ∧ PrimitiveIn (family p hp2 P) (castVec z.val))
    0
    (fun P => Nat.card_congr ((sumsZeroInsideEquiv p hp2 P _).trans
      (sumsInsideCastEquiv (family p hp2 P) _).symm))
    (fun z => by exact_mod_cast
      family_inside_zero_div_card p m hp2 hpm z.val z.property.2 z.property.1)
  simpa only [zero_mul] using h

theorem sums_outside_reverse_family_card
    (p k : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) :
    (Nat.card (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p),
      OutsidePrimitiveShell (family p hp2 P) standard rationalNorm
        ((3 * k ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)) : ℤ) =
      ((p : ℤ) - (if p ∣ k then 0 else A354766Research.residueCharacter p)) *
        (A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * k ^ 2) : ℤ) := by
  classical
  letI : Finite (AmbientPrimitiveShell standard rationalNorm ((3 * k ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)) :=
    sums_standard_shell_finite _
  have h := sigma_card_from_constant_fibers
    (fun P : StandardConic (ZMod p) =>
      OutsidePrimitiveShell (family p hp2 P) standard rationalNorm ((3 * k ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ))
    (fun P (z : PrimitiveVectorShell (3 * k ^ 2)) => castVec z.val ∉ family p hp2 P)
    ((p : ℤ) - (if p ∣ k then 0 else A354766Research.residueCharacter p))
    (fun P => Nat.card_congr (sumsOutsideCastEquiv (family p hp2 P) _).symm)
    (fun z => by
      let e := insideOneFiberEquiv standard (family p hp2) (familyMidpoint p hp2)
        p Fact.out (familyMidpoint_le_standard p hp2) (familyMidpoint_le_family p hp2)
        (family_p_standard p hp2) (family_p_neighbor p hp2)
        (prime_smul_ratVec_injective p Fact.out) (castVec z.val) ⟨z.val, rfl⟩
        ((primitiveIn_standard_castVec_iff z.val).mpr z.property.2)
      rw [← Nat.card_congr e]
      simpa only [castVec_smul] using
        family_inside_one_card_at_level p k hp2 z.val z.property.2 z.property.1)
  simpa only [primitiveVectorShell_card] using h

theorem inside_family_card_of_dvd
    (p m : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (hpm : p ∣ m) :
    (Nat.card (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p),
      InsidePrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm
        ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)) : ℤ) =
      ((p : ℤ) - (if p ∣ m / p then 0 else A354766Research.residueCharacter p)) *
        (A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * (m / p) ^ 2) : ℤ) := by
  classical
  letI : Fintype (StandardConic (ZMod p)) := Fintype.ofFinite _
  have hm : p * (m / p) = m := by
    simpa only [Nat.mul_comm] using Nat.div_mul_cancel hpm
  have hmQ : (m : ℚ) = (p : ℚ) * ((m / p : ℕ) : ℚ) := by exact_mod_cast hm.symm
  have hlevel : ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ) =
      (p : ℚ) ^ 2 * ((3 * (m / p) ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ) := by
    push_cast
    rw [hmQ]
    ring
  letI : Finite (AmbientPrimitiveShell standard rationalNorm ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)) :=
    sums_standard_shell_finite _
  letI : Finite (AmbientPrimitiveShell standard rationalNorm
      ((3 * (m / p) ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)) := sums_standard_shell_finite _
  letI : Finite (AmbientPrimitiveShell standard rationalNorm
      ((p : ℚ) ^ 2 * ((3 * (m / p) ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ))) := by
    rw [← hlevel]
    infer_instance
  letI (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) :
      Finite (AmbientPrimitiveShell (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm
        ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)) := sums_family_shell_finite p hp2 P _
  have hpart (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) :
      Nat.card (InsidePrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm
        ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)) =
      Nat.card (InsidePrimitiveZeroShell standard (family p hp2 P) p rationalNorm
        ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)) +
      Nat.card (OutsidePrimitiveShell (family p hp2 P) standard rationalNorm
        ((3 * (m / p) ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)) := by
    rw [hlevel]
    exact insidePrimitiveShell_card_partition
      standard (family p hp2 P) (familyMidpoint p hp2 P) p Fact.out
      (familyMidpoint_le_standard p hp2 P) (familyMidpoint_le_family p hp2 P)
      (family_standard_inf p hp2 P).le (family_p_standard p hp2 P)
      (family_p_neighbor p hp2 P) (prime_smul_ratVec_injective p Fact.out)
      rationalNorm ((p : ℚ) ^ 2) ((3 * (m / p) ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)
      (pow_ne_zero _ (Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr (Fact.out : p.Prime).ne_zero))
      (rationalNorm_prime_smul p)
  have htotal :
      (Nat.card (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p), InsidePrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P)
        rationalNorm ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)) : ℤ) =
      (Nat.card (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p), InsidePrimitiveZeroShell standard (family p hp2 P)
        p rationalNorm ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)) : ℤ) +
      (Nat.card (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p), OutsidePrimitiveShell (family p hp2 P) standard
        rationalNorm ((3 * (m / p) ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ)) : ℤ) := by
    rw [Nat.card_sigma, Nat.card_sigma, Nat.card_sigma, Nat.cast_sum, Nat.cast_sum,
      Nat.cast_sum, ← Finset.sum_add_distrib]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro P _
    exact_mod_cast hpart P
  rw [htotal, sums_zero_family_card_of_dvd p m hp2 hpm, zero_add,
    sums_outside_reverse_family_card]

end A354766Neighbors

#print axioms A354766Neighbors.sigma_card_from_constant_fibers
#print axioms A354766Neighbors.inside_family_card_of_not_dvd
#print axioms A354766Neighbors.inside_family_card_of_dvd
-- END InsideNeighborSums.lean

-- BEGIN PrimitiveNeighborCount.lean









/-! The actual primitive-neighbor recurrences, with no recurrence hypothesis. -/

open scoped BigOperators

namespace A354766Neighbors

open A354766Research.Neighbor A354766Research.NeighborFamily
open A354766Research.IsotropicLines

instance family_ambient_shell_finite (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) (level : ℕ) :
    Finite (AmbientPrimitiveShell (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm (level : ℚ)) :=
  actual_neighbor_shell_finite p Fact.out (normalizedRepresentative p hp2 P)
    ((reduceVec_ne_zero_iff p _).mp (normalizedRepresentative_ne_zero p hp2 P))
    (normalizedRepresentative_norm p hp2 P) level

theorem family_ambient_shell_card (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (P : StandardConic (ZMod p)) (level : ℕ) :
    Nat.card (AmbientPrimitiveShell (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm (level : ℚ)) =
      A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite level :=
  actual_neighbor_shell_card p Fact.out (normalizedRepresentative p hp2 P)
    ((reduceVec_ne_zero_iff p _).mp (normalizedRepresentative_ne_zero p hp2 P))
    (normalizedRepresentative_norm p hp2 P) level

/-- Summing actual primitive neighbor shells gives `(p+1)*r3Primitive`.
Finiteness comes from the proved lattice-coordinate equivalences. -/
theorem total_family_shell_card (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (level : ℕ) :
    Nat.card (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p),
      AmbientPrimitiveShell (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm (level : ℚ)) =
        (p + 1) * A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite level := by
  classical
  letI : Fintype (StandardConic (ZMod p)) := Fintype.ofFinite _
  rw [Nat.card_sigma]
  simp only [family_ambient_shell_card, Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, nsmul_eq_mul]
  rw [← Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, card_standardConic_zmod p hp2]
  simp only [Nat.cast_id]

/-- The global inside/outside partition, obtained by summing the actual
per-neighbor bijections; no counting identity is a structural input. -/
theorem total_family_shell_partition (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (level : ℕ) :
    Nat.card (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p),
      AmbientPrimitiveShell (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm (level : ℚ)) =
      Nat.card (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p),
        InsidePrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm (level : ℚ)) +
      Nat.card (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p),
        OutsidePrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm (level : ℚ)) := by
  classical
  letI : Fintype (StandardConic (ZMod p)) := Fintype.ofFinite _
  rw [Nat.card_sigma, Nat.card_sigma, Nat.card_sigma, ← Finset.sum_add_distrib]
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro P _
  exact ambientPrimitiveShell_card_partition standard (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm (level : ℚ)

/-- The primitive-vector double-count balance, before evaluating its inside
term. The exterior term is the proved unique-projective-line bijection. -/
theorem family_count_balance (p m : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2) :
    ((p : ℤ) + 1) * (A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * m ^ 2) : ℤ) =
      (Nat.card (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p),
        InsidePrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm (3 * (m : ℚ) ^ 2)) : ℤ) +
      (A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * (p * m) ^ 2) : ℤ) := by
  have hlevel : ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ) = 3 * (m : ℚ) ^ 2 := by
    push_cast <;> ring
  have ht : Nat.card (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p),
      AmbientPrimitiveShell (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm (3 * (m : ℚ) ^ 2)) =
        (p + 1) * A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * m ^ 2) := by
    rw [← hlevel]
    exact total_family_shell_card p hp2 (3 * m ^ 2)
  have hpart : Nat.card (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p),
      AmbientPrimitiveShell (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm (3 * (m : ℚ) ^ 2)) =
      Nat.card (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p),
        InsidePrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm (3 * (m : ℚ) ^ 2)) +
      Nat.card (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p),
        OutsidePrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm (3 * (m : ℚ) ^ 2)) := by
    rw [← hlevel]
    exact total_family_shell_partition p hp2 (3 * m ^ 2)
  have hnat : (p + 1) * A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * m ^ 2) =
      Nat.card (Σ P : StandardConic (ZMod p),
        InsidePrimitiveShell standard (family p hp2 P) rationalNorm (3 * (m : ℚ) ^ 2)) +
      A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * (p * m) ^ 2) := by
    rw [← ht, hpart, outside_family_card p hp2 m]
  exact_mod_cast hnat

/-- The first prime step, proved by the actual inside/outside double count. -/
theorem primitive_neighbor_first_step (p m : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (hpm : ¬ p ∣ m) :
    (A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * (p * m) ^ 2) : ℤ) =
      ((p : ℤ) - A354766Research.residueCharacter p) *
        (A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * m ^ 2) : ℤ) := by
  have hlevel : ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ) = 3 * (m : ℚ) ^ 2 := by
    push_cast <;> ring
  have hb := family_count_balance p m hp2
  have hi := inside_family_card_of_not_dvd p m hp2 hpm
  rw [hlevel] at hi
  linear_combination -hb - hi

/-- The repeated prime step, including the ramified prime three. -/
theorem primitive_neighbor_repeated_step (p m : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime] (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (hpm : p ∣ m) :
    (A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * (p * m) ^ 2) : ℤ) =
      ((p : ℤ) + 1) * (A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * m ^ 2) : ℤ) -
        (if p ∣ m / p then (p : ℤ) else (p : ℤ) - A354766Research.residueCharacter p) *
          (A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * (m / p) ^ 2) : ℤ) := by
  have hlevel : ((3 * m ^ 2 : ℕ) : ℚ) = 3 * (m : ℚ) ^ 2 := by
    push_cast <;> ring
  have hb := family_count_balance p m hp2
  have hi := inside_family_card_of_dvd p m hp2 hpm
  rw [hlevel] at hi
  have hc : (p : ℤ) - (if p ∣ m / p then 0 else A354766Research.residueCharacter p) =
      (if p ∣ m / p then (p : ℤ) else (p : ℤ) - A354766Research.residueCharacter p) := by
    by_cases h : p ∣ m / p <;> simp only [h, if_true, if_false, sub_zero]
  rw [hc] at hi
  linear_combination -hb - hi

def primitiveOddCount (m : ℕ) : ℤ := (A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * m ^ 2) : ℤ)

/-- Every recurrence input to the independently proved Euler-product theorem
is instantiated by the actual three-square count. -/
theorem actual_odd_prime_recurrences :
    A354766Research.OddPrimeRecurrences primitiveOddCount A354766Research.residueCharacter where
  at_one := by
    simp only [primitiveOddCount, one_pow, mul_one]
    exact_mod_cast A354766Research.primitive_three_base_kernel
  first_step := by
    intro p m hp hpo _ _ hpm
    letI : Fact p.Prime := ⟨hp⟩
    have hp2 : p ≠ 2 := by
      intro h
      subst p
      rcases hpo with ⟨k, hk⟩
      omega
    exact primitive_neighbor_first_step p m hp2 hpm
  repeated_step := by
    intro p m hp hpo _ _ hpm
    letI : Fact p.Prime := ⟨hp⟩
    have hp2 : p ≠ 2 := by
      intro h
      subst p
      rcases hpo with ⟨k, hk⟩
      omega
    exact primitive_neighbor_repeated_step p m hp2 hpm

/-- The primitive three-squares Euler product, with the recurrences supplied
by the proved projective-neighbor double count. -/
theorem primitive_three_squares_euler_product (m : ℕ) (hm : 0 < m) (hmo : Odd m) :
    (A354766.r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * m ^ 2) : ℚ) =
      8 * (m : ℚ) * ∏ p ∈ m.primeFactors,
        (1 - (A354766Research.residueCharacter p : ℚ) / (p : ℚ)) := by
  have h := A354766Research.euler_product_rat actual_odd_prime_recurrences hm hmo
  simpa only [primitiveOddCount, Int.cast_natCast] using h

end A354766Neighbors

#print axioms A354766Neighbors.total_family_shell_card
#print axioms A354766Neighbors.total_family_shell_partition
#print axioms A354766Neighbors.family_count_balance
#print axioms A354766Neighbors.primitive_neighbor_first_step
#print axioms A354766Neighbors.primitive_neighbor_repeated_step
#print axioms A354766Neighbors.actual_odd_prime_recurrences
#print axioms A354766Neighbors.primitive_three_squares_euler_product
-- END PrimitiveNeighborCount.lean

-- BEGIN Bridge.lean




/-!
# Parity and content lemmas for the Hadamard bridge

The key local obstruction is modulo eight: if four integer squares sum to the
square of a multiple of four, then all four integers are even.  The finite
`ZMod 8` lemma below checks exactly the 4096 possible residue quadruples; the
remaining lemmas lift that closed modular fact back to integers and to the
quad content.
-/

namespace A354766

inductive SquareResidue8
  | zero
  | one
  | four
  deriving DecidableEq, Fintype

def SquareResidue8.value : SquareResidue8 → ZMod 8
  | .zero => 0
  | .one => 1
  | .four => 4

def SquareResidue8.parity : SquareResidue8 → ZMod 2
  | .zero => 0
  | .one => 1
  | .four => 0

/-- Every square modulo eight has one of three residues, which also records
the parity of its square root. -/
theorem zmod8_square_class :
    ∀ a : ZMod 8, ∃ r : SquareResidue8,
      a ^ 2 = r.value ∧
      ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 8) (ZMod 2) a = r.parity := by
  decide

/-- Among four square residues, a zero sum modulo eight contains no odd
square.  This is the 81-case finite core of the congruence argument. -/
theorem squareResidue8_sum_zero :
    ∀ a b c d : SquareResidue8,
      a.value + b.value + c.value + d.value = 0 →
        a.parity = 0 ∧ b.parity = 0 ∧ c.parity = 0 ∧ d.parity = 0 := by
  decide

/-- A sum of four squares that vanishes modulo eight has even coordinates. -/
theorem zmod8_sum_four_squares_even
    (a b c d : ZMod 8)
    (hsum : a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 + c ^ 2 + d ^ 2 = 0) :
    ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 8) (ZMod 2) a = 0 ∧
    ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 8) (ZMod 2) b = 0 ∧
    ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 8) (ZMod 2) c = 0 ∧
    ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 8) (ZMod 2) d = 0 := by
  obtain ⟨ra, haSq, haParity⟩ := zmod8_square_class a
  obtain ⟨rb, hbSq, hbParity⟩ := zmod8_square_class b
  obtain ⟨rc, hcSq, hcParity⟩ := zmod8_square_class c
  obtain ⟨rd, hdSq, hdParity⟩ := zmod8_square_class d
  have hr :
      ra.value + rb.value + rc.value + rd.value = 0 := by
    simpa [haSq, hbSq, hcSq, hdSq] using hsum
  have hp := squareResidue8_sum_zero ra rb rc rd hr
  simpa [haParity, hbParity, hcParity, hdParity] using hp

theorem zmod8_four_mul_sq_zero :
    ∀ a : ZMod 8, (4 * a) ^ 2 = 0 := by
  decide

/-- If the norm of four integers is the square of a multiple of four, every
coordinate is even. -/
theorem even_coordinates_of_norm_eq_sq_of_four_dvd
    {n h i j k : ℤ} (hn : (4 : ℤ) ∣ n)
    (hnorm : h ^ 2 + i ^ 2 + j ^ 2 + k ^ 2 = n ^ 2) :
    Even h ∧ Even i ∧ Even j ∧ Even k := by
  obtain ⟨m, rfl⟩ := hn
  have hmod8 :
      (h : ZMod 8) ^ 2 + (i : ZMod 8) ^ 2 +
          (j : ZMod 8) ^ 2 + (k : ZMod 8) ^ 2 = 0 := by
    have hc := congrArg (Int.castRingHom (ZMod 8)) hnorm
    norm_num at hc
    calc
      (h : ZMod 8) ^ 2 + (i : ZMod 8) ^ 2 +
          (j : ZMod 8) ^ 2 + (k : ZMod 8) ^ 2 =
          (4 * (m : ZMod 8)) ^ 2 := by simpa using hc
      _ = 0 := zmod8_four_mul_sq_zero (m : ZMod 8)
  have he := zmod8_sum_four_squares_even
    (h : ZMod 8) (i : ZMod 8) (j : ZMod 8) (k : ZMod 8) hmod8
  simpa [ZMod.intCast_eq_zero_iff_even] using he

/-- A quad at a level divisible by four cannot have content one. -/
theorem quadContent_ne_one_of_four_dvd
    {n : ℕ} {q : QuadCoords} (hn : 4 ∣ n) (hq : IsQuad (n : ℤ) q) :
    quadContent q ≠ 1 := by
  have hnInt : (4 : ℤ) ∣ (n : ℤ) := by
    exact_mod_cast hn
  have he := even_coordinates_of_norm_eq_sq_of_four_dvd hnInt hq.2
  have hh : 2 ∣ q.h.natAbs :=
    even_iff_two_dvd.mp (Int.natAbs_even.mpr he.1)
  have hi : 2 ∣ q.i.natAbs :=
    even_iff_two_dvd.mp (Int.natAbs_even.mpr he.2.1)
  have hj : 2 ∣ q.j.natAbs :=
    even_iff_two_dvd.mp (Int.natAbs_even.mpr he.2.2.1)
  have hk : 2 ∣ q.k.natAbs :=
    even_iff_two_dvd.mp (Int.natAbs_even.mpr he.2.2.2)
  have hdiv : 2 ∣ quadContent q := by
    exact Nat.dvd_gcd hh (Nat.dvd_gcd hi (Nat.dvd_gcd hj hk))
  intro hcontent
  rw [hcontent] at hdiv
  norm_num at hdiv

/-- The primitive quad search is empty at every level divisible by four. -/
theorem primitiveQuadFinset_eq_empty_of_four_dvd
    {n : ℕ} (hn : 4 ∣ n) :
    primitiveQuadFinset n = ∅ := by
  apply Finset.eq_empty_iff_forall_notMem.mpr
  intro q hq
  rw [mem_primitiveQuadFinset] at hq
  exact quadContent_ne_one_of_four_dvd hn hq.1 hq.2

/-- Consequently the exact primitive quad count vanishes when `4 ∣ n`. -/
theorem pqFinite_eq_zero_of_four_dvd {n : ℕ} (hn : 4 ∣ n) :
    pqFinite n = 0 := by
  simp [pqFinite, primitiveQuadFinset_eq_empty_of_four_dvd hn]

end A354766
-- END Bridge.lean

-- BEGIN HadamardBridge.lean


/-!
# Forward and inverse Hadamard maps

This file turns the identities in `Elementary` into genuine maps and inverse
lemmas.  It does not yet perform the parity or primitive-content count.
-/

namespace A354766

@[ext] theorem quadCoords_ext {q₁ q₂ : QuadCoords}
    (hh : q₁.h = q₂.h) (hi : q₁.i = q₂.i)
    (hj : q₁.j = q₂.j) (hk : q₁.k = q₂.k) :
    q₁ = q₂ := by
  cases q₁
  cases q₂
  simp_all

def forwardTriple (q : QuadCoords) : Triple :=
  (x q, y q, z q)

def negateTriple (u : Triple) : Triple :=
  (-u.1, -u.2.1, -u.2.2)

@[simp] theorem negateTriple_fst (u : Triple) :
    (negateTriple u).1 = -u.1 := rfl

@[simp] theorem negateTriple_snd_fst (u : Triple) :
    (negateTriple u).2.1 = -u.2.1 := rfl

@[simp] theorem negateTriple_snd_snd (u : Triple) :
    (negateTriple u).2.2 = -u.2.2 := rfl

@[simp] theorem negateTriple_involutive (u : Triple) :
    negateTriple (negateTriple u) = u := by
  rcases u with ⟨x, y, z⟩
  simp [negateTriple]

theorem forwardTriple_isThreeSquare {n : ℕ} {q : QuadCoords}
    (hq : IsQuad (n : ℤ) q) :
    IsThreeSquare (3 * n ^ 2) (forwardTriple q) := by
  unfold IsThreeSquare forwardTriple
  simpa [Nat.cast_mul, Nat.cast_pow] using three_square_of_quad hq

theorem forwardTriple_injective_at_level {n : ℤ} {q₁ q₂ : QuadCoords}
    (hq₁ : IsQuad n q₁) (hq₂ : IsQuad n q₂)
    (hforward : forwardTriple q₁ = forwardTriple q₂) :
    q₁ = q₂ := by
  have hx : x q₁ = x q₂ := congrArg Prod.fst hforward
  have hy : y q₁ = y q₂ := congrArg (fun u : Triple => u.2.1) hforward
  have hz : z q₁ = z q₂ := congrArg (fun u : Triple => u.2.2) hforward
  apply quadCoords_ext
  · have h₁ := inverse_h q₁
    have h₂ := inverse_h q₂
    rw [hq₁.1, hx, hy, hz] at h₁
    rw [hq₂.1] at h₂
    linarith
  · have h₁ := inverse_i q₁
    have h₂ := inverse_i q₂
    rw [hq₁.1, hx, hy, hz] at h₁
    rw [hq₂.1] at h₂
    linarith
  · have h₁ := inverse_j q₁
    have h₂ := inverse_j q₂
    rw [hq₁.1, hx, hy, hz] at h₁
    rw [hq₂.1] at h₂
    linarith
  · have h₁ := inverse_k q₁
    have h₂ := inverse_k q₂
    rw [hq₁.1, hx, hy, hz] at h₁
    rw [hq₂.1] at h₂
    linarith

def InverseNumeratorsDivisible (n : ℤ) (u : Triple) : Prop :=
  (4 : ℤ) ∣ hNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2 ∧
  (4 : ℤ) ∣ iNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2 ∧
  (4 : ℤ) ∣ jNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2 ∧
  (4 : ℤ) ∣ kNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2

def inverseQuad (n : ℤ) (u : Triple) : QuadCoords where
  h := hNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2 / 4
  i := iNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2 / 4
  j := jNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2 / 4
  k := kNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2 / 4

theorem four_mul_inverseQuad_h {n : ℤ} {u : Triple}
    (hdiv : InverseNumeratorsDivisible n u) :
    4 * (inverseQuad n u).h = hNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2 := by
  simpa [inverseQuad, mul_comm] using
    Int.ediv_mul_cancel hdiv.1

theorem four_mul_inverseQuad_i {n : ℤ} {u : Triple}
    (hdiv : InverseNumeratorsDivisible n u) :
    4 * (inverseQuad n u).i = iNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2 := by
  simpa [inverseQuad, mul_comm] using
    Int.ediv_mul_cancel hdiv.2.1

theorem four_mul_inverseQuad_j {n : ℤ} {u : Triple}
    (hdiv : InverseNumeratorsDivisible n u) :
    4 * (inverseQuad n u).j = jNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2 := by
  simpa [inverseQuad, mul_comm] using
    Int.ediv_mul_cancel hdiv.2.2.1

theorem four_mul_inverseQuad_k {n : ℤ} {u : Triple}
    (hdiv : InverseNumeratorsDivisible n u) :
    4 * (inverseQuad n u).k = kNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2 := by
  simpa [inverseQuad, mul_comm] using
    Int.ediv_mul_cancel hdiv.2.2.2

theorem inverseQuad_sum {n : ℤ} {u : Triple}
    (hdiv : InverseNumeratorsDivisible n u) :
    (inverseQuad n u).h + (inverseQuad n u).i +
        (inverseQuad n u).j + (inverseQuad n u).k = n := by
  have hh := four_mul_inverseQuad_h hdiv
  have hi := four_mul_inverseQuad_i hdiv
  have hj := four_mul_inverseQuad_j hdiv
  have hk := four_mul_inverseQuad_k hdiv
  have hsum := numerator_sum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2
  linarith

theorem inverseQuad_isQuad {n : ℕ} {u : Triple}
    (hu : IsThreeSquare (3 * n ^ 2) u)
    (hdiv : InverseNumeratorsDivisible (n : ℤ) u) :
    IsQuad (n : ℤ) (inverseQuad (n : ℤ) u) := by
  refine ⟨inverseQuad_sum hdiv, ?_⟩
  have hh := four_mul_inverseQuad_h hdiv
  have hi := four_mul_inverseQuad_i hdiv
  have hj := four_mul_inverseQuad_j hdiv
  have hk := four_mul_inverseQuad_k hdiv
  have hnorm := numerator_norm (n : ℤ) u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2
  unfold IsThreeSquare at hu
  norm_num [Nat.cast_mul, Nat.cast_pow] at hu
  rw [← hh, ← hi, ← hj, ← hk] at hnorm
  ring_nf at hnorm
  nlinarith

theorem forwardTriple_inverseQuad {n : ℤ} {u : Triple}
    (hdiv : InverseNumeratorsDivisible n u) :
    forwardTriple (inverseQuad n u) = u := by
  rcases u with ⟨x₀, y₀, z₀⟩
  apply Prod.ext
  · have hh := four_mul_inverseQuad_h hdiv
    have hi := four_mul_inverseQuad_i hdiv
    have hj := four_mul_inverseQuad_j hdiv
    have hk := four_mul_inverseQuad_k hdiv
    have hx := numerator_hadamard_x n x₀ y₀ z₀
    simp only [forwardTriple, x]
    dsimp only at hh hi hj hk
    linarith
  · apply Prod.ext
    · have hh := four_mul_inverseQuad_h hdiv
      have hi := four_mul_inverseQuad_i hdiv
      have hj := four_mul_inverseQuad_j hdiv
      have hk := four_mul_inverseQuad_k hdiv
      have hy := numerator_hadamard_y n x₀ y₀ z₀
      simp only [forwardTriple, y]
      dsimp only at hh hi hj hk
      linarith
    · have hh := four_mul_inverseQuad_h hdiv
      have hi := four_mul_inverseQuad_i hdiv
      have hj := four_mul_inverseQuad_j hdiv
      have hk := four_mul_inverseQuad_k hdiv
      have hz := numerator_hadamard_z n x₀ y₀ z₀
      simp only [forwardTriple, z]
      dsimp only at hh hi hj hk
      linarith

theorem inverseNumeratorsDivisible_forwardTriple {n : ℤ} {q : QuadCoords}
    (hq : IsQuad n q) :
    InverseNumeratorsDivisible n (forwardTriple q) := by
  have hh := inverse_h q
  have hi := inverse_i q
  have hj := inverse_j q
  have hk := inverse_k q
  rw [hq.1] at hh hi hj hk
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · exact ⟨q.h, by simpa [forwardTriple, hNum, mul_comm] using hh⟩
  · exact ⟨q.i, by simpa [forwardTriple, iNum, mul_comm] using hi⟩
  · exact ⟨q.j, by simpa [forwardTriple, jNum, mul_comm] using hj⟩
  · exact ⟨q.k, by simpa [forwardTriple, kNum, mul_comm] using hk⟩

theorem inverseQuad_forwardTriple {n : ℤ} {q : QuadCoords}
    (hq : IsQuad n q) :
    inverseQuad n (forwardTriple q) = q := by
  have hdiv := inverseNumeratorsDivisible_forwardTriple hq
  apply quadCoords_ext
  · have hh := four_mul_inverseQuad_h hdiv
    have hh' := inverse_h q
    rw [hq.1] at hh'
    dsimp [forwardTriple, hNum] at hh
    apply mul_left_cancel₀ (by norm_num : (4 : ℤ) ≠ 0)
    exact hh.trans hh'
  · have hi := four_mul_inverseQuad_i hdiv
    have hi' := inverse_i q
    rw [hq.1] at hi'
    dsimp [forwardTriple, iNum] at hi
    apply mul_left_cancel₀ (by norm_num : (4 : ℤ) ≠ 0)
    exact hi.trans hi'
  · have hj := four_mul_inverseQuad_j hdiv
    have hj' := inverse_j q
    rw [hq.1] at hj'
    dsimp [forwardTriple, jNum] at hj
    apply mul_left_cancel₀ (by norm_num : (4 : ℤ) ≠ 0)
    exact hj.trans hj'
  · have hk := four_mul_inverseQuad_k hdiv
    have hk' := inverse_k q
    rw [hq.1] at hk'
    dsimp [forwardTriple, kNum] at hk
    apply mul_left_cancel₀ (by norm_num : (4 : ℤ) ≠ 0)
    exact hk.trans hk'

end A354766
-- END HadamardBridge.lean

-- BEGIN ParityBridge.lean


/-!
# Parity and admissibility for the Hadamard counting bridge

The statements in this file are closed congruence arguments plus their lifts
to integer triples.  They isolate the parity part of the odd-level bijection.
-/

namespace A354766

/-- The square-residue form of the fact that three squares equal to three
times an odd square modulo eight must all be odd squares. -/
theorem squareResidue8_three_eq_three_odd :
    ∀ a b c n : SquareResidue8,
      n.parity = 1 →
      a.value + b.value + c.value = 3 * n.value →
      a.parity = 1 ∧ b.parity = 1 ∧ c.parity = 1 := by
  decide

/-- At odd level, every coordinate in a three-square representation of
`3 * n^2` is odd. -/
theorem odd_threeSquare_coordinates {n : ℕ} {u : Triple}
    (hn : Odd n) (hu : IsThreeSquare (3 * n ^ 2) u) :
    Odd u.1 ∧ Odd u.2.1 ∧ Odd u.2.2 := by
  have hnInt : Odd (n : ℤ) := by exact_mod_cast hn
  have hmod8 :
      (u.1 : ZMod 8) ^ 2 + (u.2.1 : ZMod 8) ^ 2 +
          (u.2.2 : ZMod 8) ^ 2 =
        3 * (n : ZMod 8) ^ 2 := by
    have hc := congrArg (Int.castRingHom (ZMod 8)) hu
    norm_num [IsThreeSquare, Nat.cast_mul, Nat.cast_pow] at hc ⊢
    simpa using hc
  obtain ⟨rx, hxSq, hxParity⟩ := zmod8_square_class (u.1 : ZMod 8)
  obtain ⟨ry, hySq, hyParity⟩ := zmod8_square_class (u.2.1 : ZMod 8)
  obtain ⟨rz, hzSq, hzParity⟩ := zmod8_square_class (u.2.2 : ZMod 8)
  obtain ⟨rn, hnSq, hnParity⟩ := zmod8_square_class (n : ZMod 8)
  have hnClass : rn.parity = 1 := by
    rw [← hnParity]
    simpa using hnInt.intCast_zmod_two
  have hclasses :
      rx.value + ry.value + rz.value = 3 * rn.value := by
    simpa [hxSq, hySq, hzSq, hnSq] using hmod8
  have hp := squareResidue8_three_eq_three_odd rx ry rz rn hnClass hclasses
  have hxMod2 : (u.1 : ZMod 2) = 1 := by
    simpa using hxParity.trans hp.1
  have hyMod2 : (u.2.1 : ZMod 2) = 1 := by
    simpa using hyParity.trans hp.2.1
  have hzMod2 : (u.2.2 : ZMod 2) = 1 := by
    simpa using hzParity.trans hp.2.2
  exact ⟨ZMod.intCast_eq_one_iff_odd.mp hxMod2,
    ZMod.intCast_eq_one_iff_odd.mp hyMod2,
    ZMod.intCast_eq_one_iff_odd.mp hzMod2⟩

/-- For four odd residues modulo four, divisibility of the first inverse
numerator forces divisibility of all four. -/
theorem zmod4_all_inverse_numerators_zero
    (n x y z : ZMod 4)
    (hn : ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) n = 1)
    (hx : ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) x = 1)
    (hy : ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) y = 1)
    (hz : ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) z = 1)
    (hh : n + x + y + z = 0) :
    n + x - y - z = 0 ∧
    n - x + y - z = 0 ∧
    n - x - y + z = 0 := by
  revert n x y z
  decide

/-- For four odd residues modulo four, exactly one of a triple and its
simultaneous negative satisfies the first inverse congruence. -/
theorem zmod4_exactly_one_sign_admissible
    (n x y z : ZMod 4)
    (hn : ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) n = 1)
    (hx : ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) x = 1)
    (hy : ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) y = 1)
    (hz : ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) z = 1) :
    ((n + x + y + z = 0) ∧ ¬(n - x - y - z = 0)) ∨
    (¬(n + x + y + z = 0) ∧ (n - x - y - z = 0)) := by
  revert n x y z
  decide

def OddTripleAdmissible (n : ℤ) (u : Triple) : Prop :=
  (4 : ℤ) ∣ hNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2

instance (n : ℤ) (u : Triple) : Decidable (OddTripleAdmissible n u) := by
  unfold OddTripleAdmissible
  infer_instance

/-- For odd data, the single admissibility congruence implies divisibility of
all four inverse numerators. -/
theorem inverseNumeratorsDivisible_of_odd_admissible
    {n : ℤ} {u : Triple}
    (hn : Odd n) (hu : Odd u.1 ∧ Odd u.2.1 ∧ Odd u.2.2)
    (hadm : OddTripleAdmissible n u) :
    InverseNumeratorsDivisible n u := by
  have hn2 : ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) (n : ZMod 4) = 1 := by
    simpa using hn.intCast_zmod_two
  have hx2 : ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) (u.1 : ZMod 4) = 1 := by
    simpa using hu.1.intCast_zmod_two
  have hy2 : ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) (u.2.1 : ZMod 4) = 1 := by
    simpa using hu.2.1.intCast_zmod_two
  have hz2 : ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) (u.2.2 : ZMod 4) = 1 := by
    simpa using hu.2.2.intCast_zmod_two
  have hh4 :
      (n : ZMod 4) + (u.1 : ZMod 4) +
          (u.2.1 : ZMod 4) + (u.2.2 : ZMod 4) = 0 := by
    simpa [OddTripleAdmissible, hNum,
      ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd] using
        (ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd
          (hNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2) 4).2 hadm
  have hall := zmod4_all_inverse_numerators_zero
    (n : ZMod 4) (u.1 : ZMod 4) (u.2.1 : ZMod 4) (u.2.2 : ZMod 4)
    hn2 hx2 hy2 hz2 hh4
  refine ⟨hadm, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · exact (ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd
      (iNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2) 4).1 (by
        simpa [iNum] using hall.1)
  · exact (ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd
      (jNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2) 4).1 (by
        simpa [jNum] using hall.2.1)
  · exact (ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd
      (kNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2) 4).1 (by
        simpa [kNum] using hall.2.2)

/-- At odd level, simultaneous negation toggles admissibility. -/
theorem exactly_one_sign_oddTripleAdmissible
    {n : ℤ} {u : Triple}
    (hn : Odd n) (hu : Odd u.1 ∧ Odd u.2.1 ∧ Odd u.2.2) :
    (OddTripleAdmissible n u ∧
        ¬OddTripleAdmissible n (negateTriple u)) ∨
      (¬OddTripleAdmissible n u ∧
        OddTripleAdmissible n (negateTriple u)) := by
  have hn2 : ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) (n : ZMod 4) = 1 := by
    simpa using hn.intCast_zmod_two
  have hx2 : ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) (u.1 : ZMod 4) = 1 := by
    simpa using hu.1.intCast_zmod_two
  have hy2 : ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) (u.2.1 : ZMod 4) = 1 := by
    simpa using hu.2.1.intCast_zmod_two
  have hz2 : ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) (u.2.2 : ZMod 4) = 1 := by
    simpa using hu.2.2.intCast_zmod_two
  have hx := zmod4_exactly_one_sign_admissible
    (n : ZMod 4) (u.1 : ZMod 4) (u.2.1 : ZMod 4) (u.2.2 : ZMod 4)
    hn2 hx2 hy2 hz2
  have hx' :
      (((hNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2 : ℤ) : ZMod 4) = 0 ∧
          ¬(((hNum n (negateTriple u).1 (negateTriple u).2.1
            (negateTriple u).2.2 : ℤ) : ZMod 4) = 0)) ∨
        (¬(((hNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2 : ℤ) : ZMod 4) = 0) ∧
          ((hNum n (negateTriple u).1 (negateTriple u).2.1
            (negateTriple u).2.2 : ℤ) : ZMod 4) = 0) := by
    simpa [negateTriple, hNum, sub_eq_add_neg] using hx
  have hpos :
      OddTripleAdmissible n u ↔
        ((hNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2 : ℤ) : ZMod 4) = 0 := by
    exact (ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd
      (hNum n u.1 u.2.1 u.2.2) 4).symm
  have hneg :
      OddTripleAdmissible n (negateTriple u) ↔
        ((hNum n (negateTriple u).1 (negateTriple u).2.1
          (negateTriple u).2.2 : ℤ) : ZMod 4) = 0 := by
    exact (ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd
      (hNum n (negateTriple u).1 (negateTriple u).2.1
        (negateTriple u).2.2) 4).symm
  rw [hpos, hneg]
  exact hx'

end A354766
-- END ParityBridge.lean

-- BEGIN OddCountingBridge.lean


/-!
# The non-primitive odd-level counting bridge

This file proves the exact bijection between quads at an odd level and the
admissible half of the corresponding three-square representations, and proves
that simultaneous negation pairs the admissible and inadmissible halves.
-/

namespace A354766

def oddAdmissibleThreeSquareFinset (n : ℕ) : Finset Triple :=
  (threeSquareFinset (3 * n ^ 2)).filter
    (OddTripleAdmissible (n : ℤ))

def oddInadmissibleThreeSquareFinset (n : ℕ) : Finset Triple :=
  (threeSquareFinset (3 * n ^ 2)).filter
    fun u => ¬OddTripleAdmissible (n : ℤ) u

@[simp] theorem mem_oddAdmissibleThreeSquareFinset {n : ℕ} {u : Triple} :
    u ∈ oddAdmissibleThreeSquareFinset n ↔
      IsThreeSquare (3 * n ^ 2) u ∧ OddTripleAdmissible (n : ℤ) u := by
  simp [oddAdmissibleThreeSquareFinset]

@[simp] theorem mem_oddInadmissibleThreeSquareFinset {n : ℕ} {u : Triple} :
    u ∈ oddInadmissibleThreeSquareFinset n ↔
      IsThreeSquare (3 * n ^ 2) u ∧ ¬OddTripleAdmissible (n : ℤ) u := by
  simp [oddInadmissibleThreeSquareFinset]

theorem negateTriple_isThreeSquare {N : ℕ} {u : Triple}
    (hu : IsThreeSquare N u) :
    IsThreeSquare N (negateTriple u) := by
  rcases u with ⟨x, y, z⟩
  simpa [IsThreeSquare, negateTriple, pow_two] using hu

/-- At odd level, forward Hadamard and exact inverse division give a bijection
between all quads and the admissible three-square triples. -/
theorem quadFinset_card_eq_oddAdmissible_card
    {n : ℕ} (hn : Odd n) :
    (quadFinset n).card = (oddAdmissibleThreeSquareFinset n).card := by
  apply Finset.card_nbij'
    (forwardTriple)
    (inverseQuad (n : ℤ))
  · intro q hq
    have hquad : IsQuad (n : ℤ) q := mem_quadFinset.mp hq
    exact mem_oddAdmissibleThreeSquareFinset.mpr
      ⟨forwardTriple_isThreeSquare hquad,
        (inverseNumeratorsDivisible_forwardTriple hquad).1⟩
  · intro u hu
    have hdata := mem_oddAdmissibleThreeSquareFinset.mp hu
    have hnInt : Odd (n : ℤ) := by exact_mod_cast hn
    have huOdd := odd_threeSquare_coordinates hn hdata.1
    have hdiv := inverseNumeratorsDivisible_of_odd_admissible
      hnInt huOdd hdata.2
    exact mem_quadFinset.mpr (inverseQuad_isQuad hdata.1 hdiv)
  · intro q hq
    exact inverseQuad_forwardTriple (mem_quadFinset.mp hq)
  · intro u hu
    have hdata := mem_oddAdmissibleThreeSquareFinset.mp hu
    have hnInt : Odd (n : ℤ) := by exact_mod_cast hn
    have huOdd := odd_threeSquare_coordinates hn hdata.1
    exact forwardTriple_inverseQuad
      (inverseNumeratorsDivisible_of_odd_admissible hnInt huOdd hdata.2)

theorem oddAdmissible_card_eq_inadmissible_card
    {n : ℕ} (hn : Odd n) :
    (oddAdmissibleThreeSquareFinset n).card =
      (oddInadmissibleThreeSquareFinset n).card := by
  apply Finset.card_nbij' negateTriple negateTriple
  · intro u hu
    have hdata := mem_oddAdmissibleThreeSquareFinset.mp hu
    have huOdd := odd_threeSquare_coordinates hn hdata.1
    have hnInt : Odd (n : ℤ) := by exact_mod_cast hn
    have htoggle :=
      exactly_one_sign_oddTripleAdmissible hnInt huOdd
    have hnot : ¬OddTripleAdmissible (n : ℤ) (negateTriple u) := by
      rcases htoggle with hgood | hgood
      · exact hgood.2
      · exact (hgood.1 hdata.2).elim
    exact mem_oddInadmissibleThreeSquareFinset.mpr
      ⟨negateTriple_isThreeSquare hdata.1, hnot⟩
  · intro u hu
    have hdata := mem_oddInadmissibleThreeSquareFinset.mp hu
    have huOdd := odd_threeSquare_coordinates hn hdata.1
    have hnInt : Odd (n : ℤ) := by exact_mod_cast hn
    have htoggle :=
      exactly_one_sign_oddTripleAdmissible hnInt huOdd
    have hadm : OddTripleAdmissible (n : ℤ) (negateTriple u) := by
      rcases htoggle with hgood | hgood
      · exact (hdata.2 hgood.1).elim
      · exact hgood.2
    exact mem_oddAdmissibleThreeSquareFinset.mpr
      ⟨negateTriple_isThreeSquare hdata.1, hadm⟩
  · intro u _
    exact negateTriple_involutive u
  · intro u _
    exact negateTriple_involutive u

theorem threeSquare_card_eq_two_mul_oddAdmissible_card
    {n : ℕ} (hn : Odd n) :
    (threeSquareFinset (3 * n ^ 2)).card =
      2 * (oddAdmissibleThreeSquareFinset n).card := by
  have hpartition := Finset.card_filter_add_card_filter_not
    (s := threeSquareFinset (3 * n ^ 2))
    (p := OddTripleAdmissible (n : ℤ))
  have heq := oddAdmissible_card_eq_inadmissible_card hn
  change (oddAdmissibleThreeSquareFinset n).card +
      (oddInadmissibleThreeSquareFinset n).card =
      (threeSquareFinset (3 * n ^ 2)).card at hpartition
  omega

/-- Exact raw-count bridge at odd levels. -/
theorem r3Finite_eq_two_mul_tqFinite_of_odd
    {n : ℕ} (hn : Odd n) :
    r3Finite (3 * n ^ 2) = 2 * tqFinite n := by
  rw [r3Finite, tqFinite,
    threeSquare_card_eq_two_mul_oddAdmissible_card hn,
    quadFinset_card_eq_oddAdmissible_card hn]

end A354766
-- END OddCountingBridge.lean

-- BEGIN PrimitivityBridge.lean



/-!
# Primitivity across the odd-level Hadamard map

The main lemma works prime by prime.  A common prime divisor of the transformed
triple divides the level, and then the inverse Hadamard identities force it to
divide all four quad coordinates.
-/

namespace A354766

theorem prime_ne_two_of_dvd_odd_int {p : ℕ} {a : ℤ}
    (hp : p.Prime) (ha : Odd a) (hpa : (p : ℤ) ∣ a) :
    p ≠ 2 := by
  intro hp2
  subst p
  exact (Int.not_even_iff_odd.mpr ha) (even_iff_two_dvd.mpr hpa)

theorem prime_dvd_level_of_dvd_three_square_coordinates
    {p n : ℕ} {u : Triple}
    (hp : p.Prime)
    (hu : IsThreeSquare (3 * n ^ 2) u)
    (hpx : (p : ℤ) ∣ u.1)
    (hpy : (p : ℤ) ∣ u.2.1)
    (hpz : (p : ℤ) ∣ u.2.2) :
    (p : ℤ) ∣ (n : ℤ) := by
  have hpx2 : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ u.1 ^ 2 := pow_dvd_pow_of_dvd hpx 2
  have hpy2 : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ u.2.1 ^ 2 := pow_dvd_pow_of_dvd hpy 2
  have hpz2 : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ u.2.2 ^ 2 := pow_dvd_pow_of_dvd hpz 2
  have hu' := hu
  unfold IsThreeSquare at hu'
  norm_num [Nat.cast_mul, Nat.cast_pow] at hu'
  have hpSq :
      (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ 3 * (n : ℤ) ^ 2 := by
    rw [← hu']
    exact dvd_add (dvd_add hpx2 hpy2) hpz2
  have hpOne : (p : ℤ) ∣ 3 * (n : ℤ) ^ 2 :=
    dvd_trans (dvd_pow_self (p : ℤ) (by decide)) hpSq
  rcases Int.Prime.dvd_mul' hp hpOne with hp3 | hpnSq
  · have hpNat3 : p ∣ 3 := by
      simpa using (Int.natCast_dvd.mp hp3)
    have hpeq3 : p = 3 :=
      (Nat.dvd_prime Nat.prime_three).mp hpNat3 |>.resolve_left hp.ne_one
    subst p
    obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := hpSq
    have hcancel :
        (3 : ℤ) * (n : ℤ) ^ 2 = 3 * (3 * c) := by
      calc
        (3 : ℤ) * (n : ℤ) ^ 2 = (3 : ℤ) ^ 2 * c := hc
        _ = 3 * (3 * c) := by ring
    have hnSq : (3 : ℤ) ∣ (n : ℤ) ^ 2 := by
      refine ⟨c, ?_⟩
      exact mul_left_cancel₀ (by norm_num : (3 : ℤ) ≠ 0) hcancel
    exact Int.Prime.dvd_pow' Nat.prime_three hnSq
  · exact Int.Prime.dvd_pow' hp hpnSq

theorem prime_dvd_four_implies_eq_two {p : ℕ}
    (hp : p.Prime) (hp4 : (p : ℤ) ∣ 4) :
    p = 2 := by
  have hp4Nat : p ∣ 4 := (Int.natCast_dvd.mp hp4)
  have hp2 : p ∣ 2 := by
    apply hp.dvd_of_dvd_pow
    have hfour : 4 = 2 ^ 2 := by norm_num
    rwa [← hfour]
  exact (Nat.dvd_prime Nat.prime_two).mp hp2 |>.resolve_left hp.ne_one

theorem prime_dvd_quad_coordinates_of_dvd_forward
    {p n : ℕ} {q : QuadCoords}
    (hp : p.Prime) (hn : Odd n)
    (hq : IsQuad (n : ℤ) q)
    (hpx : (p : ℤ) ∣ x q)
    (hpy : (p : ℤ) ∣ y q)
    (hpz : (p : ℤ) ∣ z q) :
    (p : ℤ) ∣ q.h ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ q.i ∧
      (p : ℤ) ∣ q.j ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ q.k := by
  have hu : IsThreeSquare (3 * n ^ 2) (forwardTriple q) :=
    forwardTriple_isThreeSquare hq
  have hpn := prime_dvd_level_of_dvd_three_square_coordinates
    hp hu hpx hpy hpz
  have hodd := odd_threeSquare_coordinates hn hu
  have hpne2 := prime_ne_two_of_dvd_odd_int hp hodd.1 hpx
  have hdvdCoord (a : ℤ) (ha : (p : ℤ) ∣ 4 * a) :
      (p : ℤ) ∣ a := by
    rcases Int.Prime.dvd_mul' hp ha with hp4 | hpa
    · exact (hpne2 (prime_dvd_four_implies_eq_two hp hp4)).elim
    · exact hpa
  have hhEq := inverse_h q
  have hiEq := inverse_i q
  have hjEq := inverse_j q
  have hkEq := inverse_k q
  rw [hq.1] at hhEq hiEq hjEq hkEq
  refine ⟨hdvdCoord q.h ?_, hdvdCoord q.i ?_,
    hdvdCoord q.j ?_, hdvdCoord q.k ?_⟩
  · rw [← hhEq]
    exact dvd_add (dvd_add (dvd_add hpn hpx) hpy) hpz
  · rw [← hiEq]
    exact dvd_sub (dvd_sub (dvd_add hpn hpx) hpy) hpz
  · rw [← hjEq]
    exact dvd_sub (dvd_add (dvd_sub hpn hpx) hpy) hpz
  · rw [← hkEq]
    exact dvd_add (dvd_sub (dvd_sub hpn hpx) hpy) hpz

theorem quadContent_eq_one_iff_forward_tripleContent_eq_one
    {n : ℕ} {q : QuadCoords} (hn : Odd n)
    (hq : IsQuad (n : ℤ) q) :
    quadContent q = 1 ↔ tripleContent (forwardTriple q) = 1 := by
  constructor
  · intro hqPrimitive
    apply Nat.eq_one_iff_not_exists_prime_dvd.mpr
    intro p hp hpTriple
    have hpCoords :
        p ∣ (x q).natAbs ∧ p ∣ (y q).natAbs ∧ p ∣ (z q).natAbs := by
      have hpYZ : p ∣ Nat.gcd (y q).natAbs (z q).natAbs :=
        hpTriple.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_right _ _)
      exact ⟨hpTriple.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_left _ _),
        hpYZ.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_left _ _),
        hpYZ.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_right _ _)⟩
    have hpCoordsInt :
        (p : ℤ) ∣ x q ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ y q ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ z q := by
      simpa [Int.natCast_dvd] using hpCoords
    have hpQuad := prime_dvd_quad_coordinates_of_dvd_forward
      hp hn hq hpCoordsInt.1 hpCoordsInt.2.1 hpCoordsInt.2.2
    have hpQuadNat :
        p ∣ q.h.natAbs ∧ p ∣ q.i.natAbs ∧
          p ∣ q.j.natAbs ∧ p ∣ q.k.natAbs := by
      simpa [Int.natCast_dvd] using hpQuad
    have : p ∣ quadContent q := by
      exact Nat.dvd_gcd hpQuadNat.1
        (Nat.dvd_gcd hpQuadNat.2.1
          (Nat.dvd_gcd hpQuadNat.2.2.1 hpQuadNat.2.2.2))
    rw [hqPrimitive] at this
    exact hp.not_dvd_one this
  · intro htriplePrimitive
    apply Nat.eq_one_iff_not_exists_prime_dvd.mpr
    intro p hp hpQuad
    have hpQuadCoords :
        p ∣ q.h.natAbs ∧ p ∣ q.i.natAbs ∧
          p ∣ q.j.natAbs ∧ p ∣ q.k.natAbs := by
      have hpIJK :
          p ∣ Nat.gcd q.i.natAbs
            (Nat.gcd q.j.natAbs q.k.natAbs) :=
        hpQuad.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_right _ _)
      have hpJK : p ∣ Nat.gcd q.j.natAbs q.k.natAbs :=
        hpIJK.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_right _ _)
      exact ⟨hpQuad.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_left _ _),
        hpIJK.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_left _ _),
        hpJK.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_left _ _),
        hpJK.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_right _ _)⟩
    have hpQuadInt :
        (p : ℤ) ∣ q.h ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ q.i ∧
          (p : ℤ) ∣ q.j ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ q.k := by
      simpa [Int.natCast_dvd] using hpQuadCoords
    have hpx : (p : ℤ) ∣ x q := by
      exact dvd_sub (dvd_sub (dvd_add hpQuadInt.1 hpQuadInt.2.1)
        hpQuadInt.2.2.1) hpQuadInt.2.2.2
    have hpy : (p : ℤ) ∣ y q := by
      exact dvd_sub (dvd_add (dvd_sub hpQuadInt.1 hpQuadInt.2.1)
        hpQuadInt.2.2.1) hpQuadInt.2.2.2
    have hpz : (p : ℤ) ∣ z q := by
      exact dvd_add (dvd_sub (dvd_sub hpQuadInt.1 hpQuadInt.2.1)
        hpQuadInt.2.2.1) hpQuadInt.2.2.2
    have hpTriple : p ∣ tripleContent (forwardTriple q) := by
      have hpTripleNat :
          p ∣ (x q).natAbs ∧ p ∣ (y q).natAbs ∧
            p ∣ (z q).natAbs := by
        simpa [Int.natCast_dvd] using And.intro hpx (And.intro hpy hpz)
      exact Nat.dvd_gcd hpTripleNat.1
        (Nat.dvd_gcd hpTripleNat.2.1 hpTripleNat.2.2)
    rw [htriplePrimitive] at hpTriple
    exact hp.not_dvd_one hpTriple

end A354766
-- END PrimitivityBridge.lean

-- BEGIN PrimitiveOddCounting.lean


/-!
# Primitive odd-level counting bridge

This file restricts the raw odd-level bijections to content one, using the
prime-by-prime content equivalence from `PrimitivityBridge`.
-/

namespace A354766

def primitiveOddAdmissibleFinset (n : ℕ) : Finset Triple :=
  (primitiveThreeSquareFinset (3 * n ^ 2)).filter
    (OddTripleAdmissible (n : ℤ))

def primitiveOddInadmissibleFinset (n : ℕ) : Finset Triple :=
  (primitiveThreeSquareFinset (3 * n ^ 2)).filter
    fun u => ¬OddTripleAdmissible (n : ℤ) u

@[simp] theorem mem_primitiveOddAdmissibleFinset {n : ℕ} {u : Triple} :
    u ∈ primitiveOddAdmissibleFinset n ↔
      IsThreeSquare (3 * n ^ 2) u ∧ tripleContent u = 1 ∧
        OddTripleAdmissible (n : ℤ) u := by
  simp [primitiveOddAdmissibleFinset, and_assoc]

@[simp] theorem mem_primitiveOddInadmissibleFinset {n : ℕ} {u : Triple} :
    u ∈ primitiveOddInadmissibleFinset n ↔
      IsThreeSquare (3 * n ^ 2) u ∧ tripleContent u = 1 ∧
        ¬OddTripleAdmissible (n : ℤ) u := by
  simp [primitiveOddInadmissibleFinset, and_assoc]

@[simp] theorem tripleContent_negateTriple (u : Triple) :
    tripleContent (negateTriple u) = tripleContent u := by
  rcases u with ⟨x, y, z⟩
  simp [tripleContent, negateTriple]

theorem primitiveQuad_card_eq_primitiveOddAdmissible_card
    {n : ℕ} (hn : Odd n) :
    (primitiveQuadFinset n).card =
      (primitiveOddAdmissibleFinset n).card := by
  apply Finset.card_nbij'
    forwardTriple
    (inverseQuad (n : ℤ))
  · intro q hq
    have hdata := mem_primitiveQuadFinset.mp hq
    exact mem_primitiveOddAdmissibleFinset.mpr
      ⟨forwardTriple_isThreeSquare hdata.1,
        (quadContent_eq_one_iff_forward_tripleContent_eq_one
          hn hdata.1).mp hdata.2,
        (inverseNumeratorsDivisible_forwardTriple hdata.1).1⟩
  · intro u hu
    have hdata := mem_primitiveOddAdmissibleFinset.mp hu
    have hnInt : Odd (n : ℤ) := by exact_mod_cast hn
    have huOdd := odd_threeSquare_coordinates hn hdata.1
    have hdiv := inverseNumeratorsDivisible_of_odd_admissible
      hnInt huOdd hdata.2.2
    have hquad := inverseQuad_isQuad hdata.1 hdiv
    have hcontent :
        quadContent (inverseQuad (n : ℤ) u) = 1 := by
      apply (quadContent_eq_one_iff_forward_tripleContent_eq_one
        hn hquad).mpr
      rw [forwardTriple_inverseQuad hdiv]
      exact hdata.2.1
    exact mem_primitiveQuadFinset.mpr ⟨hquad, hcontent⟩
  · intro q hq
    exact inverseQuad_forwardTriple (mem_primitiveQuadFinset.mp hq).1
  · intro u hu
    have hdata := mem_primitiveOddAdmissibleFinset.mp hu
    have hnInt : Odd (n : ℤ) := by exact_mod_cast hn
    have huOdd := odd_threeSquare_coordinates hn hdata.1
    exact forwardTriple_inverseQuad
      (inverseNumeratorsDivisible_of_odd_admissible hnInt huOdd hdata.2.2)

theorem primitiveOddAdmissible_card_eq_inadmissible_card
    {n : ℕ} (hn : Odd n) :
    (primitiveOddAdmissibleFinset n).card =
      (primitiveOddInadmissibleFinset n).card := by
  apply Finset.card_nbij' negateTriple negateTriple
  · intro u hu
    have hdata := mem_primitiveOddAdmissibleFinset.mp hu
    have huOdd := odd_threeSquare_coordinates hn hdata.1
    have hnInt : Odd (n : ℤ) := by exact_mod_cast hn
    have htoggle :=
      exactly_one_sign_oddTripleAdmissible hnInt huOdd
    have hnot : ¬OddTripleAdmissible (n : ℤ) (negateTriple u) := by
      rcases htoggle with hgood | hgood
      · exact hgood.2
      · exact (hgood.1 hdata.2.2).elim
    exact mem_primitiveOddInadmissibleFinset.mpr
      ⟨negateTriple_isThreeSquare hdata.1,
        by simpa using hdata.2.1, hnot⟩
  · intro u hu
    have hdata := mem_primitiveOddInadmissibleFinset.mp hu
    have huOdd := odd_threeSquare_coordinates hn hdata.1
    have hnInt : Odd (n : ℤ) := by exact_mod_cast hn
    have htoggle :=
      exactly_one_sign_oddTripleAdmissible hnInt huOdd
    have hadm : OddTripleAdmissible (n : ℤ) (negateTriple u) := by
      rcases htoggle with hgood | hgood
      · exact (hdata.2.2 hgood.1).elim
      · exact hgood.2
    exact mem_primitiveOddAdmissibleFinset.mpr
      ⟨negateTriple_isThreeSquare hdata.1,
        by simpa using hdata.2.1, hadm⟩
  · intro u _
    exact negateTriple_involutive u
  · intro u _
    exact negateTriple_involutive u

theorem primitiveThreeSquare_card_eq_two_mul_primitiveOddAdmissible_card
    {n : ℕ} (hn : Odd n) :
    (primitiveThreeSquareFinset (3 * n ^ 2)).card =
      2 * (primitiveOddAdmissibleFinset n).card := by
  have hpartition := Finset.card_filter_add_card_filter_not
    (s := primitiveThreeSquareFinset (3 * n ^ 2))
    (p := OddTripleAdmissible (n : ℤ))
  have heq := primitiveOddAdmissible_card_eq_inadmissible_card hn
  change (primitiveOddAdmissibleFinset n).card +
      (primitiveOddInadmissibleFinset n).card =
      (primitiveThreeSquareFinset (3 * n ^ 2)).card at hpartition
  omega

/-- The fully primitive odd-level bridge, without division. -/
theorem r3PrimitiveFinite_eq_two_mul_pqFinite_of_odd
    {n : ℕ} (hn : Odd n) :
    r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * n ^ 2) = 2 * pqFinite n := by
  rw [r3PrimitiveFinite, pqFinite,
    primitiveThreeSquare_card_eq_two_mul_primitiveOddAdmissible_card hn,
    primitiveQuad_card_eq_primitiveOddAdmissible_card hn]

/-- Division form used in the OEIS statement. -/
theorem pqFinite_eq_r3PrimitiveFinite_div_two_of_odd
    {n : ℕ} (hn : Odd n) :
    pqFinite n = r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * n ^ 2) / 2 := by
  rw [r3PrimitiveFinite_eq_two_mul_pqFinite_of_odd hn]
  omega

end A354766
-- END PrimitiveOddCounting.lean

-- BEGIN DoublingBridge.lean


/-!
# Primitive quads at `m` and `2m` for odd `m`

For odd `m`, complementation followed by doubling gives a direct bijection
between primitive quads at levels `m` and `2m`:

`r ↦ (m - 2r.h, m - 2r.i, m - 2r.j, m - 2r.k)`.
-/

namespace A354766

/-- The mod-four parity classification for quads at twice an odd level. -/
theorem zmod4_twice_odd_quad_parity :
    ∀ m h i j k : ZMod 4,
      ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) m = 1 →
      h + i + j + k = 2 * m →
      h ^ 2 + i ^ 2 + j ^ 2 + k ^ 2 = (2 * m) ^ 2 →
      ((ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) h = 0 ∧
        ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) i = 0 ∧
        ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) j = 0 ∧
        ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) k = 0) ∨
       (ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) h = 1 ∧
        ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) i = 1 ∧
        ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) j = 1 ∧
        ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) k = 1)) := by
  decide

theorem primitive_quad_coordinates_odd_at_twice_odd
    {m : ℕ} {q : QuadCoords} (hm : Odd m)
    (hq : IsQuad (2 * (m : ℤ)) q) (hcontent : quadContent q = 1) :
    Odd q.h ∧ Odd q.i ∧ Odd q.j ∧ Odd q.k := by
  have hmInt : Odd (m : ℤ) := by exact_mod_cast hm
  have hm2 :
      ZMod.castHom (by decide : 2 ∣ 4) (ZMod 2) (m : ZMod 4) = 1 := by
    simpa using hmInt.intCast_zmod_two
  have hsum4 :
      (q.h : ZMod 4) + (q.i : ZMod 4) +
          (q.j : ZMod 4) + (q.k : ZMod 4) =
        2 * (m : ZMod 4) := by
    have hc := congrArg (Int.castRingHom (ZMod 4)) hq.1
    norm_num at hc ⊢
    simpa using hc
  have hnorm4 :
      (q.h : ZMod 4) ^ 2 + (q.i : ZMod 4) ^ 2 +
          (q.j : ZMod 4) ^ 2 + (q.k : ZMod 4) ^ 2 =
        (2 * (m : ZMod 4)) ^ 2 := by
    have hc := congrArg (Int.castRingHom (ZMod 4)) hq.2
    norm_num at hc ⊢
    simpa using hc
  have hp := zmod4_twice_odd_quad_parity
    (m : ZMod 4) (q.h : ZMod 4) (q.i : ZMod 4)
      (q.j : ZMod 4) (q.k : ZMod 4) hm2 hsum4 hnorm4
  rcases hp with heven | hodd
  · have hh : 2 ∣ q.h.natAbs :=
      even_iff_two_dvd.mp (Int.natAbs_even.mpr
        (ZMod.intCast_eq_zero_iff_even.mp (by simpa using heven.1)))
    have hi : 2 ∣ q.i.natAbs :=
      even_iff_two_dvd.mp (Int.natAbs_even.mpr
        (ZMod.intCast_eq_zero_iff_even.mp (by simpa using heven.2.1)))
    have hj : 2 ∣ q.j.natAbs :=
      even_iff_two_dvd.mp (Int.natAbs_even.mpr
        (ZMod.intCast_eq_zero_iff_even.mp (by simpa using heven.2.2.1)))
    have hk : 2 ∣ q.k.natAbs :=
      even_iff_two_dvd.mp (Int.natAbs_even.mpr
        (ZMod.intCast_eq_zero_iff_even.mp (by simpa using heven.2.2.2)))
    have htwo : 2 ∣ quadContent q :=
      Nat.dvd_gcd hh (Nat.dvd_gcd hi (Nat.dvd_gcd hj hk))
    rw [hcontent] at htwo
    norm_num at htwo
  · exact ⟨ZMod.intCast_eq_one_iff_odd.mp (by simpa using hodd.1),
      ZMod.intCast_eq_one_iff_odd.mp (by simpa using hodd.2.1),
      ZMod.intCast_eq_one_iff_odd.mp (by simpa using hodd.2.2.1),
      ZMod.intCast_eq_one_iff_odd.mp (by simpa using hodd.2.2.2)⟩

def doubleComplementQuad (m : ℤ) (q : QuadCoords) : QuadCoords where
  h := m - 2 * q.h
  i := m - 2 * q.i
  j := m - 2 * q.j
  k := m - 2 * q.k

def halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) (q : QuadCoords) : QuadCoords where
  h := (m - q.h) / 2
  i := (m - q.i) / 2
  j := (m - q.j) / 2
  k := (m - q.k) / 2

theorem doubleComplementQuad_isQuad {m : ℕ} {q : QuadCoords}
    (hq : IsQuad (m : ℤ) q) :
    IsQuad (2 * (m : ℤ)) (doubleComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q) := by
  rcases hq with ⟨hsum, hnorm⟩
  constructor
  · simp only [doubleComplementQuad]
    linarith
  · simp only [doubleComplementQuad]
    nlinarith

theorem halfComplementQuad_twice_eq {m : ℤ} {q : QuadCoords}
    (hodd : Odd q.h ∧ Odd q.i ∧ Odd q.j ∧ Odd q.k)
    (hm : Odd m) :
    2 * (halfComplementQuad m q).h = m - q.h ∧
    2 * (halfComplementQuad m q).i = m - q.i ∧
    2 * (halfComplementQuad m q).j = m - q.j ∧
    2 * (halfComplementQuad m q).k = m - q.k := by
  have hdiv (a : ℤ) (ha : Odd a) : (2 : ℤ) ∣ m - a := by
    exact even_iff_two_dvd.mp ((Int.even_sub').mpr
      ⟨fun _ => ha, fun _ => hm⟩)
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · simpa [halfComplementQuad, mul_comm] using
      Int.ediv_mul_cancel (hdiv q.h hodd.1)
  · simpa [halfComplementQuad, mul_comm] using
      Int.ediv_mul_cancel (hdiv q.i hodd.2.1)
  · simpa [halfComplementQuad, mul_comm] using
      Int.ediv_mul_cancel (hdiv q.j hodd.2.2.1)
  · simpa [halfComplementQuad, mul_comm] using
      Int.ediv_mul_cancel (hdiv q.k hodd.2.2.2)

theorem halfComplementQuad_isQuad {m : ℕ} {q : QuadCoords}
    (hm : Odd m) (hq : IsQuad (2 * (m : ℤ)) q)
    (hodd : Odd q.h ∧ Odd q.i ∧ Odd q.j ∧ Odd q.k) :
    IsQuad (m : ℤ) (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q) := by
  have hmInt : Odd (m : ℤ) := by exact_mod_cast hm
  have htwice := halfComplementQuad_twice_eq hodd hmInt
  constructor
  · have hsum := hq.1
    simp only at htwice
    linarith
  · have hnorm := hq.2
    have hh :
        q.h = (m : ℤ) - 2 * (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).h := by
      linarith [htwice.1]
    have hi :
        q.i = (m : ℤ) - 2 * (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).i := by
      linarith [htwice.2.1]
    have hj :
        q.j = (m : ℤ) - 2 * (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).j := by
      linarith [htwice.2.2.1]
    have hk :
        q.k = (m : ℤ) - 2 * (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).k := by
      linarith [htwice.2.2.2]
    have hsumR :
        (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).h +
          (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).i +
          (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).j +
          (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).k = (m : ℤ) := by
      linarith [hq.1, htwice.1, htwice.2.1,
        htwice.2.2.1, htwice.2.2.2]
    rw [hh, hi, hj, hk] at hnorm
    ring_nf at hnorm
    have hnormR :
        (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).h ^ 2 +
            (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).i ^ 2 +
            (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).j ^ 2 +
            (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).k ^ 2 =
          (m : ℤ) *
            ((halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).h +
              (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).i +
              (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).j +
              (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).k) := by
      nlinarith [hnorm]
    rw [hsumR] at hnormR
    simpa [pow_two] using hnormR

@[simp] theorem half_doubleComplementQuad {m : ℕ} {q : QuadCoords} :
    halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ)
      (doubleComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q) = q := by
  apply quadCoords_ext <;>
    simp [halfComplementQuad, doubleComplementQuad]

theorem double_halfComplementQuad {m : ℕ} {q : QuadCoords}
    (hm : Odd m)
    (hodd : Odd q.h ∧ Odd q.i ∧ Odd q.j ∧ Odd q.k) :
    doubleComplementQuad (m : ℤ)
      (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q) = q := by
  have hmInt : Odd (m : ℤ) := by exact_mod_cast hm
  have htwice := halfComplementQuad_twice_eq hodd hmInt
  apply quadCoords_ext <;>
    simp only [doubleComplementQuad] <;>
    linarith

theorem doubleComplementQuad_primitive {m : ℕ} {q : QuadCoords}
    (hm : Odd m) (hq : IsQuad (m : ℤ) q) (hprimitive : quadContent q = 1) :
    quadContent (doubleComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q) = 1 := by
  apply Nat.eq_one_iff_not_exists_prime_dvd.mpr
  intro p hp hpContent
  have hpCoords :
      p ∣ (doubleComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).h.natAbs ∧
      p ∣ (doubleComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).i.natAbs ∧
      p ∣ (doubleComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).j.natAbs ∧
      p ∣ (doubleComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).k.natAbs := by
    have hpIJK := hpContent.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_right _ _)
    have hpJK := hpIJK.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_right _ _)
    exact ⟨hpContent.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_left _ _),
      hpIJK.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_left _ _),
      hpJK.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_left _ _),
      hpJK.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_right _ _)⟩
  have hpCoordsInt :
      (p : ℤ) ∣ (doubleComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).h ∧
      (p : ℤ) ∣ (doubleComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).i ∧
      (p : ℤ) ∣ (doubleComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).j ∧
      (p : ℤ) ∣ (doubleComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).k := by
    simpa [Int.natCast_dvd] using hpCoords
  have hpTwoM : (p : ℤ) ∣ 2 * (m : ℤ) := by
    have hsum := (doubleComplementQuad_isQuad hq).1
    rw [← hsum]
    exact dvd_add (dvd_add (dvd_add hpCoordsInt.1 hpCoordsInt.2.1)
      hpCoordsInt.2.2.1) hpCoordsInt.2.2.2
  have hpne2 : p ≠ 2 := by
    intro hp2
    subst p
    have hmInt : Odd (m : ℤ) := by exact_mod_cast hm
    have hqOdd : Odd (doubleComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).h := by
      simp [doubleComplementQuad, Int.odd_sub, hmInt]
    exact (Int.not_even_iff_odd.mpr hqOdd)
      (even_iff_two_dvd.mpr hpCoordsInt.1)
  have hpM : (p : ℤ) ∣ (m : ℤ) := by
    rcases Int.Prime.dvd_mul' hp hpTwoM with hp2 | hpm
    · exact (hpne2 (prime_dvd_four_implies_eq_two hp
        (hp2.trans (by norm_num : (2 : ℤ) ∣ 4)))).elim
    · exact hpm
  have hpQInt :
      (p : ℤ) ∣ q.h ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ q.i ∧
      (p : ℤ) ∣ q.j ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ q.k := by
    have get (a : ℤ) (ha : (p : ℤ) ∣ (m : ℤ) - 2 * a) :
        (p : ℤ) ∣ a := by
      have hp2a : (p : ℤ) ∣ 2 * a := by
        convert dvd_sub hpM ha using 1
        ring
      rcases Int.Prime.dvd_mul' hp hp2a with hp2 | hpa
      · exact (hpne2 (prime_dvd_four_implies_eq_two hp
          (hp2.trans (by norm_num : (2 : ℤ) ∣ 4)))).elim
      · exact hpa
    exact ⟨get q.h hpCoordsInt.1, get q.i hpCoordsInt.2.1,
      get q.j hpCoordsInt.2.2.1, get q.k hpCoordsInt.2.2.2⟩
  have hpQNat :
      p ∣ q.h.natAbs ∧ p ∣ q.i.natAbs ∧
      p ∣ q.j.natAbs ∧ p ∣ q.k.natAbs := by
    simpa [Int.natCast_dvd] using hpQInt
  have hpQContent : p ∣ quadContent q :=
    Nat.dvd_gcd hpQNat.1 (Nat.dvd_gcd hpQNat.2.1
      (Nat.dvd_gcd hpQNat.2.2.1 hpQNat.2.2.2))
  rw [hprimitive] at hpQContent
  exact hp.not_dvd_one hpQContent

theorem halfComplementQuad_primitive {m : ℕ} {q : QuadCoords}
    (hm : Odd m) (hq : IsQuad (2 * (m : ℤ)) q)
    (hprimitive : quadContent q = 1) :
    quadContent (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q) = 1 := by
  have hodd := primitive_quad_coordinates_odd_at_twice_odd hm hq hprimitive
  have hrQuad := halfComplementQuad_isQuad hm hq hodd
  by_contra hrNot
  have hrNe : quadContent (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q) ≠ 1 := hrNot
  obtain ⟨p, hp, hpContent⟩ := Nat.exists_prime_and_dvd hrNe
  have hpCoords :
      p ∣ (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).h.natAbs ∧
      p ∣ (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).i.natAbs ∧
      p ∣ (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).j.natAbs ∧
      p ∣ (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).k.natAbs := by
    have hpIJK := hpContent.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_right _ _)
    have hpJK := hpIJK.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_right _ _)
    exact ⟨hpContent.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_left _ _),
      hpIJK.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_left _ _),
      hpJK.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_left _ _),
      hpJK.trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_right _ _)⟩
  have hpCoordsInt :
      (p : ℤ) ∣ (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).h ∧
      (p : ℤ) ∣ (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).i ∧
      (p : ℤ) ∣ (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).j ∧
      (p : ℤ) ∣ (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ) q).k := by
    simpa [Int.natCast_dvd] using hpCoords
  have hpM : (p : ℤ) ∣ (m : ℤ) := by
    rw [← hrQuad.1]
    exact dvd_add (dvd_add (dvd_add hpCoordsInt.1 hpCoordsInt.2.1)
      hpCoordsInt.2.2.1) hpCoordsInt.2.2.2
  have hpQInt :
      (p : ℤ) ∣ q.h ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ q.i ∧
      (p : ℤ) ∣ q.j ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ q.k := by
    have htwice := halfComplementQuad_twice_eq hodd
      (by exact_mod_cast hm : Odd (m : ℤ))
    have get (a r : ℤ) (hr : 2 * r = (m : ℤ) - a)
        (hpr : (p : ℤ) ∣ r) : (p : ℤ) ∣ a := by
      have hp2r : (p : ℤ) ∣ 2 * r := by
        simpa [mul_comm] using dvd_mul_of_dvd_right hpr 2
      have hpm2r : (p : ℤ) ∣ (m : ℤ) - 2 * r := dvd_sub hpM hp2r
      convert hpm2r using 1
      linarith
    exact ⟨get q.h _ htwice.1 hpCoordsInt.1,
      get q.i _ htwice.2.1 hpCoordsInt.2.1,
      get q.j _ htwice.2.2.1 hpCoordsInt.2.2.1,
      get q.k _ htwice.2.2.2 hpCoordsInt.2.2.2⟩
  have hpQNat :
      p ∣ q.h.natAbs ∧ p ∣ q.i.natAbs ∧
      p ∣ q.j.natAbs ∧ p ∣ q.k.natAbs := by
    simpa [Int.natCast_dvd] using hpQInt
  have hpQContent : p ∣ quadContent q :=
    Nat.dvd_gcd hpQNat.1 (Nat.dvd_gcd hpQNat.2.1
      (Nat.dvd_gcd hpQNat.2.2.1 hpQNat.2.2.2))
  rw [hprimitive] at hpQContent
  exact hp.not_dvd_one hpQContent
