theorem primitiveQuad_card_twice_odd {m : ℕ} (hm : Odd m) :
    (primitiveQuadFinset (2 * m)).card =
      (primitiveQuadFinset m).card := by
  symm
  apply Finset.card_nbij'
    (doubleComplementQuad (m : ℤ))
    (halfComplementQuad (m : ℤ))
  · intro q hq
    have hdata := mem_primitiveQuadFinset.mp hq
    exact mem_primitiveQuadFinset.mpr
      ⟨by simpa [Nat.cast_mul] using doubleComplementQuad_isQuad hdata.1,
        doubleComplementQuad_primitive hm hdata.1 hdata.2⟩
  · intro q hq
    have hdata := mem_primitiveQuadFinset.mp hq
    have hquad : IsQuad (2 * (m : ℤ)) q := by
      simpa [Nat.cast_mul] using hdata.1
    exact mem_primitiveQuadFinset.mpr
      ⟨halfComplementQuad_isQuad hm hquad
          (primitive_quad_coordinates_odd_at_twice_odd hm hquad hdata.2),
        halfComplementQuad_primitive hm hquad hdata.2⟩
  · intro q _
    exact half_doubleComplementQuad
  · intro q hq
    have hdata := mem_primitiveQuadFinset.mp hq
    have hquad : IsQuad (2 * (m : ℤ)) q := by
      simpa [Nat.cast_mul] using hdata.1
    exact double_halfComplementQuad hm
      (primitive_quad_coordinates_odd_at_twice_odd hm hquad hdata.2)

theorem pqFinite_twice_odd {m : ℕ} (hm : Odd m) :
    pqFinite (2 * m) = pqFinite m := by
  exact primitiveQuad_card_twice_odd hm

theorem pqFinite_mod_four_eq_two {n : ℕ} (hn : n % 4 = 2) :
    pqFinite n =
      r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * (n / 2) ^ 2) / 2 := by
  have hnEq : n = 2 * (n / 2) := by omega
  have hmOdd : Odd (n / 2) := by
    rw [Nat.odd_iff]
    omega
  calc
    pqFinite n = pqFinite (2 * (n / 2)) := by rw [← hnEq]
    _ = pqFinite (n / 2) := pqFinite_twice_odd hmOdd
    _ = r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * (n / 2) ^ 2) / 2 :=
      pqFinite_eq_r3PrimitiveFinite_div_two_of_odd hmOdd

end A354766
-- END DoublingBridge.lean

-- BEGIN ContentDecomposition.lean


/-!
# Content decomposition for A354766

This file develops the scaling and quotient operations needed to partition all
quads at level `n` by their (positive) content.
-/

namespace A354766

def scaleQuad (d : ℕ) (q : QuadCoords) : QuadCoords where
  h := (d : ℤ) * q.h
  i := (d : ℤ) * q.i
  j := (d : ℤ) * q.j
  k := (d : ℤ) * q.k

def divideQuad (q : QuadCoords) : QuadCoords where
  h := q.h / (quadContent q : ℤ)
  i := q.i / (quadContent q : ℤ)
  j := q.j / (quadContent q : ℤ)
  k := q.k / (quadContent q : ℤ)

@[simp] theorem scaleQuad_zero (q : QuadCoords) :
    scaleQuad 0 q = ⟨0, 0, 0, 0⟩ := by
  cases q
  simp [scaleQuad]

@[simp] theorem scaleQuad_one (q : QuadCoords) : scaleQuad 1 q = q := by
  cases q
  simp [scaleQuad]

@[simp] theorem scaleQuad_mul (a b : ℕ) (q : QuadCoords) :
    scaleQuad a (scaleQuad b q) = scaleQuad (a * b) q := by
  cases q
  simp [scaleQuad, mul_assoc]

theorem scaleQuad_injective {d : ℕ} (hd : 0 < d) :
    Function.Injective (scaleQuad d) := by
  have hdZ : (d : ℤ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast (Nat.ne_of_gt hd)
  intro q r h
  cases q with
  | mk qh qi qj qk =>
    cases r with
    | mk rh ri rj rk =>
      simp only [scaleQuad, QuadCoords.mk.injEq] at h ⊢
      exact ⟨mul_left_cancel₀ hdZ h.1,
        mul_left_cancel₀ hdZ h.2.1,
        mul_left_cancel₀ hdZ h.2.2.1,
        mul_left_cancel₀ hdZ h.2.2.2⟩

theorem isQuad_scale {n : ℕ} {q : QuadCoords} (hq : IsQuad (n : ℤ) q) (d : ℕ) :
    IsQuad ((d * n : ℕ) : ℤ) (scaleQuad d q) := by
  rcases hq with ⟨hsum, hnorm⟩
  constructor
  · simp only [scaleQuad, Nat.cast_mul]
    push_cast
    calc
      (d : ℤ) * q.h + (d : ℤ) * q.i + (d : ℤ) * q.j + (d : ℤ) * q.k =
          (d : ℤ) * (q.h + q.i + q.j + q.k) := by ring
      _ = (d : ℤ) * n := by rw [hsum]
  · simp only [scaleQuad, Nat.cast_mul]
    push_cast
    calc
      ((d : ℤ) * q.h) ^ 2 + ((d : ℤ) * q.i) ^ 2 +
          ((d : ℤ) * q.j) ^ 2 + ((d : ℤ) * q.k) ^ 2 =
          (d : ℤ) ^ 2 * (q.h ^ 2 + q.i ^ 2 + q.j ^ 2 + q.k ^ 2) := by ring
      _ = (d : ℤ) ^ 2 * (n : ℤ) ^ 2 := by rw [hnorm]
      _ = ((d : ℤ) * n) ^ 2 := by ring

@[simp] theorem quadContent_scale (d : ℕ) (q : QuadCoords) :
    quadContent (scaleQuad d q) = d * quadContent q := by
  simp only [quadContent, scaleQuad, Int.natAbs_mul, Int.natAbs_natCast,
    Nat.gcd_mul_left]

theorem quadContent_pos_of_isQuad {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) {q : QuadCoords}
    (hq : IsQuad (n : ℤ) q) :
    0 < quadContent q := by
  by_contra hnot
  have hc : quadContent q = 0 := Nat.eq_zero_of_not_pos hnot
  have hh : q.h.natAbs = 0 := by
    have := Nat.gcd_eq_zero_iff.mp hc
    exact this.1
  have hi : q.i.natAbs = 0 := by
    have := Nat.gcd_eq_zero_iff.mp hc
    exact (Nat.gcd_eq_zero_iff.mp this.2).1
  have hj : q.j.natAbs = 0 := by
    have := Nat.gcd_eq_zero_iff.mp hc
    exact (Nat.gcd_eq_zero_iff.mp (Nat.gcd_eq_zero_iff.mp this.2).2).1
  have hk : q.k.natAbs = 0 := by
    have := Nat.gcd_eq_zero_iff.mp hc
    exact (Nat.gcd_eq_zero_iff.mp (Nat.gcd_eq_zero_iff.mp this.2).2).2
  have hqzero : q = ⟨0, 0, 0, 0⟩ := by
    cases q with
    | mk qh qi qj qk =>
      simp only at hh hi hj hk
      have eh : qh = 0 := Int.natAbs_eq_zero.mp hh
      have ei : qi = 0 := Int.natAbs_eq_zero.mp hi
      have ej : qj = 0 := Int.natAbs_eq_zero.mp hj
      have ek : qk = 0 := Int.natAbs_eq_zero.mp hk
      subst qh
      subst qi
      subst qj
      subst qk
      rfl
  subst q
  have hz : (0 : ℤ) = n := by simpa [IsQuad] using hq.1
  have : n = 0 := by exact_mod_cast hz.symm
  omega

theorem quadContent_dvd_h (q : QuadCoords) : (quadContent q : ℤ) ∣ q.h := by
  rw [Int.natCast_dvd]
  exact (Nat.gcd_dvd_left _ _)

theorem quadContent_dvd_i (q : QuadCoords) : (quadContent q : ℤ) ∣ q.i := by
  rw [Int.natCast_dvd]
  exact (Nat.gcd_dvd_right _ _).trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_left _ _)

theorem quadContent_dvd_j (q : QuadCoords) : (quadContent q : ℤ) ∣ q.j := by
  rw [Int.natCast_dvd]
  exact (Nat.gcd_dvd_right _ _).trans
    ((Nat.gcd_dvd_right _ _).trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_left _ _))

theorem quadContent_dvd_k (q : QuadCoords) : (quadContent q : ℤ) ∣ q.k := by
  rw [Int.natCast_dvd]
  exact (Nat.gcd_dvd_right _ _).trans
    ((Nat.gcd_dvd_right _ _).trans (Nat.gcd_dvd_right _ _))

theorem quadContent_dvd_level {n : ℕ} {q : QuadCoords} (hq : IsQuad (n : ℤ) q) :
    quadContent q ∣ n := by
  have hz : (quadContent q : ℤ) ∣ (n : ℤ) := by
    rw [← hq.1]
    exact dvd_add (dvd_add (dvd_add (quadContent_dvd_h q) (quadContent_dvd_i q))
      (quadContent_dvd_j q)) (quadContent_dvd_k q)
  exact_mod_cast hz

theorem isQuad_of_scale {d m : ℕ} (hd : 0 < d) {q : QuadCoords}
    (hq : IsQuad ((d * m : ℕ) : ℤ) (scaleQuad d q)) :
    IsQuad (m : ℤ) q := by
  have hdZ : (d : ℤ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast (Nat.ne_of_gt hd)
  rcases hq with ⟨hsum, hnorm⟩
  constructor
  · apply mul_left_cancel₀ hdZ
    calc
      (d : ℤ) * (q.h + q.i + q.j + q.k) =
          (scaleQuad d q).h + (scaleQuad d q).i +
            (scaleQuad d q).j + (scaleQuad d q).k := by
              simp only [scaleQuad]
              ring
      _ = ((d * m : ℕ) : ℤ) := hsum
      _ = (d : ℤ) * (m : ℤ) := by push_cast; rfl
  · apply mul_left_cancel₀ (pow_ne_zero 2 hdZ)
    calc
      (d : ℤ) ^ 2 * (q.h ^ 2 + q.i ^ 2 + q.j ^ 2 + q.k ^ 2) =
          (scaleQuad d q).h ^ 2 + (scaleQuad d q).i ^ 2 +
            (scaleQuad d q).j ^ 2 + (scaleQuad d q).k ^ 2 := by
              simp only [scaleQuad]
              ring
      _ = ((d * m : ℕ) : ℤ) ^ 2 := hnorm
      _ = (d : ℤ) ^ 2 * (m : ℤ) ^ 2 := by push_cast; ring

theorem scaleQuad_divideQuad {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) {q : QuadCoords}
    (hq : IsQuad (n : ℤ) q) :
    scaleQuad (quadContent q) (divideQuad q) = q := by
  have hh := quadContent_dvd_h q
  have hi := quadContent_dvd_i q
  have hj := quadContent_dvd_j q
  have hk := quadContent_dvd_k q
  cases q with
  | mk h i j k =>
    simp only [scaleQuad, divideQuad, QuadCoords.mk.injEq]
    constructor
    · rw [mul_comm]
      exact Int.ediv_mul_cancel hh
    · constructor
      · rw [mul_comm]
        exact Int.ediv_mul_cancel hi
      · constructor
        · rw [mul_comm]
          exact Int.ediv_mul_cancel hj
        · rw [mul_comm]
          exact Int.ediv_mul_cancel hk

theorem divideQuad_isQuad {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) {q : QuadCoords}
    (hq : IsQuad (n : ℤ) q) :
    IsQuad ((n / quadContent q : ℕ) : ℤ) (divideQuad q) := by
  have hcpos := quadContent_pos_of_isQuad hn hq
  have hcd := quadContent_dvd_level hq
  apply isQuad_of_scale hcpos
  have hlevel : quadContent q * (n / quadContent q) = n :=
    Nat.mul_div_cancel' hcd
  rw [hlevel, scaleQuad_divideQuad hn hq]
  exact hq

theorem divideQuad_primitive {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) {q : QuadCoords}
    (hq : IsQuad (n : ℤ) q) :
    quadContent (divideQuad q) = 1 := by
  have hcpos := quadContent_pos_of_isQuad hn hq
  have heq := congrArg quadContent (scaleQuad_divideQuad hn hq)
  rw [quadContent_scale] at heq
  apply mul_left_cancel₀ (Nat.ne_of_gt hcpos)
  simpa using heq

def quadContentFinset (n d : ℕ) : Finset QuadCoords :=
  (quadFinset n).filter fun q => quadContent q = d

@[simp] theorem mem_quadContentFinset {n d : ℕ} {q : QuadCoords} :
    q ∈ quadContentFinset n d ↔ IsQuad (n : ℤ) q ∧ quadContent q = d := by
  simp [quadContentFinset]

theorem card_quadContentFinset {n d : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (hdvd : d ∣ n) :
    (quadContentFinset n d).card = pqFinite (n / d) := by
  have hdpos : 0 < d := Nat.pos_of_dvd_of_pos hdvd hn
  symm
  apply Finset.card_bij'
      (fun q _ => scaleQuad d q)
      (fun q _ => divideQuad q)
  · intro q hq
    rw [mem_quadContentFinset]
    rw [mem_primitiveQuadFinset] at hq
    constructor
    · rw [← Nat.mul_div_cancel' hdvd]
      exact isQuad_scale hq.1 d
    · simpa [hq.2] using quadContent_scale d q
  · intro q hq
    rw [mem_primitiveQuadFinset]
    rw [mem_quadContentFinset] at hq
    constructor
    · simpa [hq.2] using divideQuad_isQuad hn hq.1
    · exact divideQuad_primitive hn hq.1
  · intro q hq
    rw [mem_primitiveQuadFinset] at hq
    apply scaleQuad_injective hdpos
    have hs : IsQuad (n : ℤ) (scaleQuad d q) := by
      rw [← Nat.mul_div_cancel' hdvd]
      exact isQuad_scale hq.1 d
    simpa [hq.2] using scaleQuad_divideQuad hn hs
  · intro q hq
    rw [mem_quadContentFinset] at hq
    simpa [hq.2] using scaleQuad_divideQuad hn hq.1

theorem tqFinite_eq_sum_pqFinite (n : ℕ) (hn : 0 < n) :
    tqFinite n = ∑ d ∈ n.divisors, pqFinite d := by
  unfold tqFinite
  calc
    (quadFinset n).card =
        ∑ d ∈ n.divisors, ((quadFinset n).filter fun q => quadContent q = d).card := by
      apply Finset.card_eq_sum_card_fiberwise
      intro q hq
      rw [Finset.mem_coe, Nat.mem_divisors]
      exact ⟨quadContent_dvd_level (mem_quadFinset.mp hq), hn.ne'⟩
    _ = ∑ d ∈ n.divisors, pqFinite (n / d) := by
      apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
      intro d hd
      change (quadContentFinset n d).card = pqFinite (n / d)
      exact card_quadContentFinset hn (Nat.dvd_of_mem_divisors hd)
    _ = ∑ d ∈ n.divisors, pqFinite d := by
      rw [Nat.sum_div_divisors]

end A354766
-- END ContentDecomposition.lean

-- BEGIN SignOrbit.lean



/-!
# Sign orbits and divisibility by four

At an odd three-square level all three coordinates are odd, hence nonzero.
Independent sign changes therefore split the primitive representations into
eight equipotent octants.  Combined with the factor-two Hadamard bridge, this
proves that every primitive quad count is divisible by four.
-/

namespace A354766

def flipX (u : Triple) : Triple := (-u.1, u.2.1, u.2.2)
def flipY (u : Triple) : Triple := (u.1, -u.2.1, u.2.2)
def flipZ (u : Triple) : Triple := (u.1, u.2.1, -u.2.2)

@[simp] theorem flipX_involutive (u : Triple) : flipX (flipX u) = u := by
  rcases u with ⟨x, y, z⟩
  simp [flipX]

@[simp] theorem flipY_involutive (u : Triple) : flipY (flipY u) = u := by
  rcases u with ⟨x, y, z⟩
  simp [flipY]

@[simp] theorem flipZ_involutive (u : Triple) : flipZ (flipZ u) = u := by
  rcases u with ⟨x, y, z⟩
  simp [flipZ]

@[simp] theorem isThreeSquare_flipX {N : ℕ} {u : Triple} :
    IsThreeSquare N (flipX u) ↔ IsThreeSquare N u := by
  rcases u with ⟨x, y, z⟩
  simp [IsThreeSquare, flipX]

@[simp] theorem isThreeSquare_flipY {N : ℕ} {u : Triple} :
    IsThreeSquare N (flipY u) ↔ IsThreeSquare N u := by
  rcases u with ⟨x, y, z⟩
  simp [IsThreeSquare, flipY]

@[simp] theorem isThreeSquare_flipZ {N : ℕ} {u : Triple} :
    IsThreeSquare N (flipZ u) ↔ IsThreeSquare N u := by
  rcases u with ⟨x, y, z⟩
  simp [IsThreeSquare, flipZ]

@[simp] theorem tripleContent_flipX (u : Triple) :
    tripleContent (flipX u) = tripleContent u := by
  rcases u with ⟨x, y, z⟩
  simp [tripleContent, flipX]

@[simp] theorem tripleContent_flipY (u : Triple) :
    tripleContent (flipY u) = tripleContent u := by
  rcases u with ⟨x, y, z⟩
  simp [tripleContent, flipY]

@[simp] theorem tripleContent_flipZ (u : Triple) :
    tripleContent (flipZ u) = tripleContent u := by
  rcases u with ⟨x, y, z⟩
  simp [tripleContent, flipZ]

theorem card_eq_two_mul_card_filter_pos
    {α : Type*} [DecidableEq α]
    (s : Finset α) (coord : α → ℤ) (flip : α → α)
    (hmem : ∀ a, a ∈ s → flip a ∈ s)
    (hinv : ∀ a, flip (flip a) = a)
    (hneg : ∀ a, coord (flip a) = -coord a)
    (hne : ∀ a, a ∈ s → coord a ≠ 0) :
    s.card = 2 * (s.filter fun a => 0 < coord a).card := by
  have heq :
      (s.filter fun a => 0 < coord a).card =
        (s.filter fun a => ¬0 < coord a).card := by
    apply Finset.card_nbij' flip flip
    · intro a ha
      have ha' : a ∈ s ∧ 0 < coord a := by simpa using ha
      have hout : flip a ∈ s ∧ ¬0 < coord (flip a) :=
        ⟨hmem a ha'.1, by rw [hneg]; omega⟩
      simpa using hout
    · intro a ha
      have ha' : a ∈ s ∧ ¬0 < coord a := by simpa using ha
      have haNeg : coord a < 0 := by
        have := hne a ha'.1
        omega
      have hout : flip a ∈ s ∧ 0 < coord (flip a) :=
        ⟨hmem a ha'.1, by rw [hneg]; omega⟩
      simpa using hout
    · intro a _
      exact hinv a
    · intro a _
      exact hinv a
  have hpartition := Finset.card_filter_add_card_filter_not
    (s := s) (p := fun a => 0 < coord a)
  omega

def primitiveXPositiveFinset (m : ℕ) : Finset Triple :=
  (primitiveThreeSquareFinset (3 * m ^ 2)).filter fun u => 0 < u.1

def primitiveXYPositiveFinset (m : ℕ) : Finset Triple :=
  (primitiveXPositiveFinset m).filter fun u => 0 < u.2.1

def primitiveXYZPositiveFinset (m : ℕ) : Finset Triple :=
  (primitiveXYPositiveFinset m).filter fun u => 0 < u.2.2

theorem primitiveThreeSquare_card_eq_eight_mul_positive
    {m : ℕ} (hm : Odd m) :
    (primitiveThreeSquareFinset (3 * m ^ 2)).card =
      8 * (primitiveXYZPositiveFinset m).card := by
  let s0 := primitiveThreeSquareFinset (3 * m ^ 2)
  have hx : s0.card = 2 * (primitiveXPositiveFinset m).card := by
    change s0.card = 2 * (s0.filter fun u => 0 < u.1).card
    apply card_eq_two_mul_card_filter_pos s0 (fun u => u.1) flipX
    · intro u hu
      rw [mem_primitiveThreeSquareFinset] at hu ⊢
      exact ⟨isThreeSquare_flipX.mpr hu.1, by simpa using hu.2⟩
    · exact flipX_involutive
    · intro u
      rfl
    · intro u hu
      have hdata := mem_primitiveThreeSquareFinset.mp hu
      have hodd := (odd_threeSquare_coordinates hm hdata.1).1
      intro hzero
      simp [hzero] at hodd
  have hy :
      (primitiveXPositiveFinset m).card =
        2 * (primitiveXYPositiveFinset m).card := by
    change (primitiveXPositiveFinset m).card =
      2 * ((primitiveXPositiveFinset m).filter fun u => 0 < u.2.1).card
    apply card_eq_two_mul_card_filter_pos
      (primitiveXPositiveFinset m) (fun u => u.2.1) flipY
    · intro u hu
      simp only [primitiveXPositiveFinset, Finset.mem_filter] at hu ⊢
      rw [mem_primitiveThreeSquareFinset] at hu ⊢
      exact ⟨⟨isThreeSquare_flipY.mpr hu.1.1, by simpa using hu.1.2⟩, hu.2⟩
    · exact flipY_involutive
    · intro u
      rfl
    · intro u hu
      have hdata := (mem_primitiveThreeSquareFinset.mp
        (Finset.mem_filter.mp hu).1)
      have hodd := (odd_threeSquare_coordinates hm hdata.1).2.1
      intro hzero
      simp [hzero] at hodd
  have hz :
      (primitiveXYPositiveFinset m).card =
        2 * (primitiveXYZPositiveFinset m).card := by
    change (primitiveXYPositiveFinset m).card =
      2 * ((primitiveXYPositiveFinset m).filter fun u => 0 < u.2.2).card
    apply card_eq_two_mul_card_filter_pos
      (primitiveXYPositiveFinset m) (fun u => u.2.2) flipZ
    · intro u hu
      simp only [primitiveXYPositiveFinset, primitiveXPositiveFinset,
        Finset.mem_filter] at hu ⊢
      rw [mem_primitiveThreeSquareFinset] at hu ⊢
      exact ⟨⟨⟨isThreeSquare_flipZ.mpr hu.1.1.1,
        by simpa using hu.1.1.2⟩, hu.1.2⟩, hu.2⟩
    · exact flipZ_involutive
    · intro u
      rfl
    · intro u hu
      have hdata := (mem_primitiveThreeSquareFinset.mp
        (Finset.mem_filter.mp (Finset.mem_filter.mp hu).1).1)
      have hodd := (odd_threeSquare_coordinates hm hdata.1).2.2
      intro hzero
      simp [hzero] at hodd
  rw [hx, hy, hz]
  omega

theorem four_dvd_pqFinite_of_odd {m : ℕ} (hm : Odd m) :
    4 ∣ pqFinite m := by
  let positiveCount := (primitiveXYZPositiveFinset m).card
  have h8 :
      r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * m ^ 2) = 8 * positiveCount := by
    exact primitiveThreeSquare_card_eq_eight_mul_positive hm
  have h2 := r3PrimitiveFinite_eq_two_mul_pqFinite_of_odd hm
  use positiveCount
  omega

theorem four_dvd_pqFinite (n : ℕ) : 4 ∣ pqFinite n := by
  by_cases hnOdd : Odd n
  · exact four_dvd_pqFinite_of_odd hnOdd
  · have hnEven : n % 2 = 0 := Nat.not_odd_iff.mp hnOdd
    by_cases hnTwo : n % 4 = 2
    · have hmOdd : Odd (n / 2) := by
        rw [Nat.odd_iff]
        omega
      have hnEq : n = 2 * (n / 2) := by omega
      rw [hnEq, pqFinite_twice_odd hmOdd]
      exact four_dvd_pqFinite_of_odd hmOdd
    · have hnFour : 4 ∣ n := by
        rw [Nat.dvd_iff_mod_eq_zero]
        have hlt := Nat.mod_lt n (by omega : 0 < 4)
        omega
      rw [pqFinite_eq_zero_of_four_dvd hnFour]
      exact dvd_zero 4

end A354766
-- END SignOrbit.lean

-- BEGIN NormalizedDecomposition.lean



/-!
# Normalized divisor decomposition

The raw content decomposition and sign-orbit divisibility combine here to
justify division by four term by term.
-/

namespace A354766

theorem tqFinite_div_four_eq_sum_pqFinite_div_four (n : ℕ) (hn : 0 < n) :
    tqFinite n / 4 = ∑ d ∈ n.divisors, pqFinite d / 4 := by
  rw [tqFinite_eq_sum_pqFinite n hn]
  exact Nat.sum_div fun d _ => four_dvd_pqFinite d

end A354766
-- END NormalizedDecomposition.lean

-- BEGIN A278085Complement.lean



/-!
The primary A278085 equations have sum 3n and norm 3n². The complement
q ↦ (n-h,n-i,n-j,n-k) is an explicit involution identifying them with
the sum-n, norm-n² model used throughout the arithmetic proof.
-/

namespace A354766

def IsA278085Quad (n : ℤ) (q : QuadCoords) : Prop :=
  q.h + q.i + q.j + q.k = 3 * n ∧
    q.h ^ 2 + q.i ^ 2 + q.j ^ 2 + q.k ^ 2 = 3 * n ^ 2

def complementQuad (n : ℤ) (q : QuadCoords) : QuadCoords :=
  ⟨n - q.h, n - q.i, n - q.j, n - q.k⟩

theorem complementQuad_sum (n : ℤ) (q : QuadCoords) :
    (complementQuad n q).h + (complementQuad n q).i +
      (complementQuad n q).j + (complementQuad n q).k =
      4 * n - (q.h + q.i + q.j + q.k) := by
  simp only [complementQuad]
  ring

theorem complementQuad_norm (n : ℤ) (q : QuadCoords) :
    (complementQuad n q).h ^ 2 + (complementQuad n q).i ^ 2 +
      (complementQuad n q).j ^ 2 + (complementQuad n q).k ^ 2 =
      (q.h ^ 2 + q.i ^ 2 + q.j ^ 2 + q.k ^ 2) -
        2 * n * (q.h + q.i + q.j + q.k) + 4 * n ^ 2 := by
  simp only [complementQuad]
  ring

theorem complementQuad_involutive (n : ℤ) : Function.Involutive (complementQuad n) := by
  intro q
  cases q
  dsimp [complementQuad]
  congr 1 <;> ring

theorem complementQuad_primary {n : ℤ} {q : QuadCoords} (hq : IsQuad n q) :
    IsA278085Quad n (complementQuad n q) := by
  constructor
  · rw [complementQuad_sum, hq.1]
    ring
  · rw [complementQuad_norm, hq.1, hq.2]
    ring

theorem complementQuad_standard {n : ℤ} {q : QuadCoords} (hq : IsA278085Quad n q) :
    IsQuad n (complementQuad n q) := by
  constructor
  · rw [complementQuad_sum, hq.1]
    ring
  · rw [complementQuad_norm, hq.1, hq.2]
    ring

def a278085QuadEquiv (n : ℤ) :
    {q : QuadCoords // IsQuad n q} ≃ {q : QuadCoords // IsA278085Quad n q} where
  toFun q := ⟨complementQuad n q.val, complementQuad_primary q.property⟩
  invFun q := ⟨complementQuad n q.val, complementQuad_standard q.property⟩
  left_inv q := Subtype.ext (complementQuad_involutive n q.val)
  right_inv q := Subtype.ext (complementQuad_involutive n q.val)

def finiteA278085QuadEquiv (n : ℕ) :
    {q : QuadCoords // q ∈ quadFinset n} ≃
      {q : QuadCoords // IsA278085Quad (n : ℤ) q} :=
  (Equiv.subtypeEquivRight (fun _ => mem_quadFinset)).trans (a278085QuadEquiv (n : ℤ))

instance finite_A278085_quads (n : ℕ) :
    Finite {q : QuadCoords // IsA278085Quad (n : ℤ) q} :=
  Finite.of_equiv {q : QuadCoords // q ∈ quadFinset n} (finiteA278085QuadEquiv n)

theorem a278085_quad_count (n : ℕ) :
    Nat.card {q : QuadCoords // IsA278085Quad (n : ℤ) q} = tqFinite n := by
  have h := Nat.card_congr (finiteA278085QuadEquiv n)
  simpa only [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, Fintype.card_coe, tqFinite] using h.symm

noncomputable def A278085ComplementTotal (n : ℕ) : ℕ :=
  Nat.card {q : QuadCoords // IsA278085Quad (n : ℤ) q} / 4

theorem A278085ComplementTotal_eq_normalized_count (n : ℕ) :
    A278085ComplementTotal n = tqFinite n / 4 := by
  rw [A278085ComplementTotal, a278085_quad_count]

/-- A common prime divisor of all four coordinates. -/
def PrimeDividesQuad (p : ℕ) (q : QuadCoords) : Prop :=
  (p : ℤ) ∣ q.h ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ q.i ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ q.j ∧ (p : ℤ) ∣ q.k

theorem quadContent_eq_one_iff_no_prime (q : QuadCoords) :
    quadContent q = 1 ↔ ∀ p : ℕ, p.Prime → ¬ PrimeDividesQuad p q := by
  rw [Nat.eq_one_iff_not_exists_prime_dvd]
  simp only [quadContent, PrimeDividesQuad, Nat.dvd_gcd_iff, Int.natCast_dvd]

theorem primeDividesQuad_norm (p : ℕ) (q : QuadCoords) (hq : PrimeDividesQuad p q) :
    (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ q.h ^ 2 + q.i ^ 2 + q.j ^ 2 + q.k ^ 2 := by
  rcases hq with ⟨⟨a, ha⟩, ⟨b, hb⟩, ⟨c, hc⟩, ⟨d, hd⟩⟩
  rw [ha, hb, hc, hd]
  refine ⟨a ^ 2 + b ^ 2 + c ^ 2 + d ^ 2, ?_⟩
  ring

theorem primary_prime_divisor_dvd_level (p n : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (q : QuadCoords)
    (hprimary : IsA278085Quad (n : ℤ) q) (hq : PrimeDividesQuad p q) : p ∣ n := by
  have hInt : (p : ℤ) ^ 2 ∣ 3 * (n : ℤ) ^ 2 := by
    rw [← hprimary.2]
    exact primeDividesQuad_norm p q hq
  have hNat : p ^ 2 ∣ 3 * n ^ 2 := by exact_mod_cast hInt
  exact (A354766Neighbors.prime_sq_dvd_three_mul_sq_iff p n hp).mp hNat

theorem standard_quad_divisor_dvd_level (p n : ℕ) (q : QuadCoords)
    (hstandard : IsQuad (n : ℤ) q) (hq : PrimeDividesQuad p q) : p ∣ n := by
  have hsum : (p : ℤ) ∣ q.h + q.i + q.j + q.k :=
    dvd_add (dvd_add (dvd_add hq.1 hq.2.1) hq.2.2.1) hq.2.2.2
  rw [hstandard.1] at hsum
  exact Int.natCast_dvd_natCast.mp hsum

theorem complement_prime_divisibility_iff (n : ℕ) (q : QuadCoords)
    (hstandard : IsQuad (n : ℤ) q) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) :
    PrimeDividesQuad p (complementQuad (n : ℤ) q) ↔ PrimeDividesQuad p q := by
  constructor
  · intro hc
    have hpn : (p : ℤ) ∣ (n : ℤ) := Int.natCast_dvd_natCast.mpr
      (primary_prime_divisor_dvd_level p n hp (complementQuad (n : ℤ) q)
        (complementQuad_primary hstandard) hc)
    exact ⟨by simpa [complementQuad] using dvd_sub hpn hc.1,
      by simpa [complementQuad] using dvd_sub hpn hc.2.1,
      by simpa [complementQuad] using dvd_sub hpn hc.2.2.1,
      by simpa [complementQuad] using dvd_sub hpn hc.2.2.2⟩
  · intro hq
    have hpn : (p : ℤ) ∣ (n : ℤ) := Int.natCast_dvd_natCast.mpr
      (standard_quad_divisor_dvd_level p n q hstandard hq)
    exact ⟨dvd_sub hpn hq.1, dvd_sub hpn hq.2.1,
      dvd_sub hpn hq.2.2.1, dvd_sub hpn hq.2.2.2⟩

theorem complement_primitive_iff (n : ℕ) (q : QuadCoords)
    (hstandard : IsQuad (n : ℤ) q) :
    quadContent (complementQuad (n : ℤ) q) = 1 ↔ quadContent q = 1 := by
  rw [quadContent_eq_one_iff_no_prime, quadContent_eq_one_iff_no_prime]
  constructor
  · intro h p hp hq
    exact h p hp ((complement_prime_divisibility_iff n q hstandard p hp).mpr hq)
  · intro h p hp hc
    exact h p hp ((complement_prime_divisibility_iff n q hstandard p hp).mp hc)

theorem complement_primary_primitive_iff (n : ℕ) (q : QuadCoords)
    (hprimary : IsA278085Quad (n : ℤ) q) :
    quadContent (complementQuad (n : ℤ) q) = 1 ↔ quadContent q = 1 := by
  have h := complement_primitive_iff n (complementQuad (n : ℤ) q)
    (complementQuad_standard hprimary)
  simpa only [complementQuad_involutive (n : ℤ) q] using h.symm

def a278085PrimitiveQuadEquiv (n : ℕ) :
    {q : QuadCoords // IsQuad (n : ℤ) q ∧ quadContent q = 1} ≃
      {q : QuadCoords // IsA278085Quad (n : ℤ) q ∧ quadContent q = 1} where
  toFun q := ⟨complementQuad (n : ℤ) q.val, complementQuad_primary q.property.1,
    (complement_primitive_iff n q.val q.property.1).mpr q.property.2⟩
  invFun q := ⟨complementQuad (n : ℤ) q.val, complementQuad_standard q.property.1,
    (complement_primary_primitive_iff n q.val q.property.1).mpr q.property.2⟩
  left_inv q := Subtype.ext (complementQuad_involutive (n : ℤ) q.val)
  right_inv q := Subtype.ext (complementQuad_involutive (n : ℤ) q.val)

def finiteA278085PrimitiveQuadEquiv (n : ℕ) :
    {q : QuadCoords // q ∈ primitiveQuadFinset n} ≃
      {q : QuadCoords // IsA278085Quad (n : ℤ) q ∧ quadContent q = 1} :=
  (Equiv.subtypeEquivRight (fun _ => mem_primitiveQuadFinset)).trans
    (a278085PrimitiveQuadEquiv n)

instance finite_A278085_primitive_quads (n : ℕ) :
    Finite {q : QuadCoords // IsA278085Quad (n : ℤ) q ∧ quadContent q = 1} :=
  Finite.of_equiv {q : QuadCoords // q ∈ primitiveQuadFinset n}
    (finiteA278085PrimitiveQuadEquiv n)

theorem a278085_primitive_quad_count (n : ℕ) :
    Nat.card {q : QuadCoords // IsA278085Quad (n : ℤ) q ∧ quadContent q = 1} = pqFinite n := by
  have h := Nat.card_congr (finiteA278085PrimitiveQuadEquiv n)
  simpa only [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, Fintype.card_coe, pqFinite] using h.symm

/-- The genuine OEIS primary definition counts primitive complementary quads. -/
noncomputable def A278085Primary (n : ℕ) : ℕ :=
  Nat.card {q : QuadCoords // IsA278085Quad (n : ℤ) q ∧ quadContent q = 1} / 4

theorem A278085Primary_eq_normalized_count (n : ℕ) :
    A278085Primary n = pqFinite n / 4 := by
  rw [A278085Primary, a278085_primitive_quad_count]

end A354766

#print axioms A354766.complement_primitive_iff
#print axioms A354766.a278085_primitive_quad_count

-- END A278085Complement.lean

-- BEGIN Blueprint.lean









/-!
# OEIS A354766: arithmetic conclusions

The primitive representation formula is supplied by the explicit, instantiated
lattice-neighbor double count. The density theorem uses the finite CRT sieve.
-/

open Set Filter
open scoped BigOperators Topology

namespace A354766

def content (q : QuadCoords) : ℕ :=
  quadContent q

def quadSet (n : ℕ) : Set QuadCoords :=
  {q | IsQuad (n : ℤ) q}

def primitiveQuadSet (n : ℕ) : Set QuadCoords :=
  {q | q ∈ quadSet n ∧ content q = 1}

def tq (n : ℕ) : ℕ := tqFinite n
def pq (n : ℕ) : ℕ := pqFinite n
def T (n : ℕ) : ℕ := tq n / 4
def P (n : ℕ) : ℕ := pq n / 4

theorem A278085Primary_eq_P (n : ℕ) : A278085Primary n = P n := by
  simpa only [P, pq] using A278085Primary_eq_normalized_count n

def IsMultiplicative (f : ℕ → ℕ) : Prop :=
  f 1 = 1 ∧ ∀ ⦃m n : ℕ⦄, Nat.Coprime m n → f (m * n) = f m * f n

def primitiveThreeSquareSet (N : ℕ) : Set (ℤ × ℤ × ℤ) :=
  {u | u.1 ^ 2 + u.2.1 ^ 2 + u.2.2 ^ 2 = (N : ℤ) ∧
    Nat.gcd u.1.natAbs (Nat.gcd u.2.1.natAbs u.2.2.natAbs) = 1}

def r3Primitive (N : ℕ) : ℕ := r3PrimitiveFinite N

/-- The elementary counting bridge. This is the first substantial target. -/
theorem primitive_quad_count_bridge (n : ℕ) (hn : 0 < n) :
    pq n =
      if Odd n then r3Primitive (3 * n ^ 2) / 2
      else if n % 4 = 2 then r3Primitive (3 * (n / 2) ^ 2) / 2
      else 0 := by
  have _hnUsed : n ≠ 0 := Nat.ne_of_gt hn
  by_cases hodd : Odd n
  · rw [if_pos hodd]
    exact pqFinite_eq_r3PrimitiveFinite_div_two_of_odd hodd
  · rw [if_neg hodd]
    by_cases hmod : n % 4 = 2
    · rw [if_pos hmod]
      exact pqFinite_mod_four_eq_two hmod
    · rw [if_neg hmod]
      have heven : n % 2 = 0 := Nat.not_odd_iff.mp hodd
      have hmodZero : n % 4 = 0 := by
        have hlt := Nat.mod_lt n (by omega : 0 < 4)
        omega
      have hfour : 4 ∣ n := Nat.dvd_iff_mod_eq_zero.mpr hmodZero
      exact pqFinite_eq_zero_of_four_dvd hfour

def chiMinusThree (p : ℕ) : ℤ :=
  if p % 6 = 1 then 1 else if p % 6 = 5 then -1 else 0

noncomputable def cooperHirschhornRhs (m : ℕ) : ℚ :=
  (8 : ℚ) * m *
    ∏ p ∈ m.primeFactors,
      (1 - (chiMinusThree p : ℚ) / (p : ℚ))

/--
The primitive three-square product follows from the concrete lattice-neighbor
double count and the prime-power recurrence, including the ramified prime three.
-/
theorem cooper_hirschhorn_specialization (m : ℕ) (hm : Odd m) :
    (r3Primitive (3 * m ^ 2) : ℚ) = cooperHirschhornRhs m := by
  have hmPos : 0 < m := by
    rcases hm with ⟨a, ha⟩
    omega
  simpa only [r3Primitive, cooperHirschhornRhs, chiMinusThree,
    A354766Research.residueCharacter] using
    A354766Neighbors.primitive_three_squares_euler_product m hmPos hm

noncomputable def primitiveOddRhs (m : ℕ) : ℚ :=
  (m : ℚ) *
    ∏ p ∈ m.primeFactors,
      (1 - (chiMinusThree p : ℚ) / (p : ℚ))

theorem cooperHirschhornRhs_eq (m : ℕ) :
    cooperHirschhornRhs m = 8 * primitiveOddRhs m := by
  simp only [cooperHirschhornRhs, primitiveOddRhs]
  ring

theorem primitiveOddRhs_mul_of_coprime {m n : ℕ} (h : Nat.Coprime m n) :
    primitiveOddRhs (m * n) = primitiveOddRhs m * primitiveOddRhs n := by
  rw [primitiveOddRhs, primitiveOddRhs, primitiveOddRhs,
    h.primeFactors_mul, Finset.prod_union h.disjoint_primeFactors]
  push_cast
  ring

theorem P_cast_eq_primitiveOddRhs {m : ℕ} (hm : Odd m) :
    (P m : ℚ) = primitiveOddRhs m := by
  have hfour : 4 ∣ pqFinite m := four_dvd_pqFinite m
  have htwo := r3PrimitiveFinite_eq_two_mul_pqFinite_of_odd hm
  have htwoQ :
      (r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * m ^ 2) : ℚ) = 2 * (pqFinite m : ℚ) := by
    exact_mod_cast htwo
  calc
    (P m : ℚ) = (pqFinite m : ℚ) / 4 := by
      exact Nat.cast_div_charZero hfour
    _ = (r3PrimitiveFinite (3 * m ^ 2) : ℚ) / 8 := by
      rw [htwoQ]
      ring
    _ = cooperHirschhornRhs m / 8 := by
      have hCH := cooper_hirschhorn_specialization m hm
      simpa only [r3Primitive] using congrArg (fun x : ℚ => x / 8) hCH
    _ = primitiveOddRhs m := by
      rw [cooperHirschhornRhs_eq]
      ring

theorem P_mul_of_odd {m n : ℕ} (hm : Odd m) (hn : Odd n)
    (hcop : Nat.Coprime m n) :
    P (m * n) = P m * P n := by
  have hQ :
      (P (m * n) : ℚ) = (P m : ℚ) * (P n : ℚ) := by
    rw [P_cast_eq_primitiveOddRhs (hm.mul hn),
      P_cast_eq_primitiveOddRhs hm, P_cast_eq_primitiveOddRhs hn,
      primitiveOddRhs_mul_of_coprime hcop]
  exact_mod_cast hQ

theorem P_twice_odd {m : ℕ} (hm : Odd m) : P (2 * m) = P m := by
  simp only [P, pq]
  rw [pqFinite_twice_odd hm]

theorem P_eq_zero_of_four_dvd {n : ℕ} (hn : 4 ∣ n) : P n = 0 := by
  simp [P, pq, pqFinite_eq_zero_of_four_dvd hn]

theorem P_one : P 1 = 1 := by
  have h := P_cast_eq_primitiveOddRhs (by simp : Odd 1)
  norm_num [primitiveOddRhs] at h
  exact_mod_cast h

theorem P_zero : P 0 = 0 := by
  exact P_eq_zero_of_four_dvd (by simp)

theorem P_prime_pow_cast {p k : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime) (hp2 : p ≠ 2)
    (hk : k ≠ 0) :
    (P (p ^ k) : ℚ) =
      (p ^ k : ℚ) *
        (1 - (chiMinusThree p : ℚ) / (p : ℚ)) := by
  have hodd : Odd (p ^ k) := (hp.odd_of_ne_two hp2).pow
  rw [P_cast_eq_primitiveOddRhs hodd, primitiveOddRhs,
    Nat.primeFactors_prime_pow hk hp]
  simp

theorem P_prime_pow_mod_six_one {p k : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime)
    (hk : k ≠ 0) (hpmod : p % 6 = 1) :
    P (p ^ k) = p ^ (k - 1) * (p - 1) := by
  have hp2 : p ≠ 2 := by omega
  have hQ := P_prime_pow_cast hp hp2 hk
  rw [chiMinusThree, if_pos hpmod] at hQ
  have hkEq : k - 1 + 1 = k := by omega
  have hpPow : (p : ℚ) ^ k = (p : ℚ) ^ (k - 1) * p := by
    rw [← pow_succ, hkEq]
  have hpQ : (p : ℚ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hp.ne_zero
  have htarget :
      (P (p ^ k) : ℚ) = (p ^ (k - 1) * (p - 1) : ℕ) := by
    rw [hQ]
    push_cast
    rw [Nat.cast_sub (by omega : 1 ≤ p), hpPow]
    field_simp [hpQ]
    ring
  exact_mod_cast htarget

theorem P_prime_pow_mod_six_five {p k : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime)
    (hk : k ≠ 0) (hpmod : p % 6 = 5) :
    P (p ^ k) = p ^ (k - 1) * (p + 1) := by
  have hp2 : p ≠ 2 := by omega
  have hQ := P_prime_pow_cast hp hp2 hk
  rw [chiMinusThree, if_neg (by omega : ¬p % 6 = 1), if_pos hpmod] at hQ
  have hkEq : k - 1 + 1 = k := by omega
  have hpPow : (p : ℚ) ^ k = (p : ℚ) ^ (k - 1) * p := by
    rw [← pow_succ, hkEq]
  have hpQ : (p : ℚ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hp.ne_zero
  have htarget :
      (P (p ^ k) : ℚ) = (p ^ (k - 1) * (p + 1) : ℕ) := by
    rw [hQ]
    push_cast
    rw [hpPow]
    field_simp [hpQ]
    ring
  exact_mod_cast htarget

theorem P_three_pow {k : ℕ} (hk : k ≠ 0) :
    P (3 ^ k) = 3 ^ k := by
  have hQ := P_prime_pow_cast Nat.prime_three
    (by norm_num) hk
  norm_num [chiMinusThree] at hQ
  exact_mod_cast hQ

theorem P_multiplicative : IsMultiplicative P := by
  constructor
  · exact P_one
  · intro m n hcop
    rcases m.eq_zero_or_pos with rfl | hmPos
    · have hnOne : n = 1 := by
        simpa [Nat.coprime_zero_left] using hcop
      simp [hnOne, P_zero, P_one]
    rcases n.eq_zero_or_pos with rfl | hnPos
    · have hmOne : m = 1 := by
        simpa [Nat.coprime_zero_right] using hcop
      simp [hmOne, P_zero, P_one]
    by_cases hmOdd : Odd m
    · by_cases hnOdd : Odd n
      · exact P_mul_of_odd hmOdd hnOdd hcop
      · have hnEven : n % 2 = 0 := Nat.not_odd_iff.mp hnOdd
        by_cases hnFour : 4 ∣ n
        · have hprodFour : 4 ∣ m * n := dvd_mul_of_dvd_right hnFour m
          simp [P_eq_zero_of_four_dvd hnFour, P_eq_zero_of_four_dvd hprodFour]
        · have hnMod : n % 4 = 2 := by
            rw [Nat.dvd_iff_mod_eq_zero] at hnFour
            have hlt := Nat.mod_lt n (by omega : 0 < 4)
            omega
          have hnEq : n = 2 * (n / 2) := by omega
          have hnHalfOdd : Odd (n / 2) := by
            rw [Nat.odd_iff]
            omega
          have hcopHalf : Nat.Coprime m (n / 2) :=
            hcop.coprime_dvd_right (Nat.div_dvd_of_dvd (by omega : 2 ∣ n))
          have hmnEq : m * n = 2 * (m * (n / 2)) := by
            calc
              m * n = m * (2 * (n / 2)) := congrArg (m * ·) hnEq
              _ = 2 * (m * (n / 2)) := by ring
          have hPn : P n = P (n / 2) := by
            calc
              P n = P (2 * (n / 2)) := congrArg P hnEq
              _ = P (n / 2) := P_twice_odd hnHalfOdd
          calc
            P (m * n) = P (2 * (m * (n / 2))) := by rw [hmnEq]
            _ = P (m * (n / 2)) := P_twice_odd (hmOdd.mul hnHalfOdd)
            _ = P m * P (n / 2) := P_mul_of_odd hmOdd hnHalfOdd hcopHalf
            _ = P m * P n := by rw [hPn]
    · have hmEven : m % 2 = 0 := Nat.not_odd_iff.mp hmOdd
      by_cases hnOdd : Odd n
      · by_cases hmFour : 4 ∣ m
        · have hprodFour : 4 ∣ m * n := dvd_mul_of_dvd_left hmFour n
          simp [P_eq_zero_of_four_dvd hmFour, P_eq_zero_of_four_dvd hprodFour]
        · have hmMod : m % 4 = 2 := by
            rw [Nat.dvd_iff_mod_eq_zero] at hmFour
            have hlt := Nat.mod_lt m (by omega : 0 < 4)
            omega
          have hmEq : m = 2 * (m / 2) := by omega
          have hmHalfOdd : Odd (m / 2) := by
            rw [Nat.odd_iff]
            omega
          have hcopHalf : Nat.Coprime (m / 2) n :=
            hcop.coprime_dvd_left (Nat.div_dvd_of_dvd (by omega : 2 ∣ m))
          have hmnEq : m * n = 2 * ((m / 2) * n) := by
            calc
              m * n = (2 * (m / 2)) * n := congrArg (· * n) hmEq
              _ = 2 * ((m / 2) * n) := by ring
          have hPm : P m = P (m / 2) := by
            calc
              P m = P (2 * (m / 2)) := congrArg P hmEq
              _ = P (m / 2) := P_twice_odd hmHalfOdd
          calc
            P (m * n) = P (2 * ((m / 2) * n)) := by rw [hmnEq]
            _ = P ((m / 2) * n) := P_twice_odd (hmHalfOdd.mul hnOdd)
            _ = P (m / 2) * P n := P_mul_of_odd hmHalfOdd hnOdd hcopHalf
            _ = P m * P n := by rw [hPm]
      · have hnEven : n % 2 = 0 := Nat.not_odd_iff.mp hnOdd
        have htwoM : 2 ∣ m := Nat.dvd_iff_mod_eq_zero.mpr hmEven
        have htwoN : 2 ∣ n := Nat.dvd_iff_mod_eq_zero.mpr hnEven
        have htwoGcd : 2 ∣ Nat.gcd m n := Nat.dvd_gcd htwoM htwoN
        rw [hcop.gcd_eq_one] at htwoGcd
        norm_num at htwoGcd

/-- Every quad decomposes uniquely into its content times a primitive quad. -/
theorem total_as_divisor_sum (n : ℕ) (hn : 0 < n) :
    T n = ∑ d ∈ n.divisors, P d := by
  exact tqFinite_div_four_eq_sum_pqFinite_div_four n hn

theorem T_one : T 1 = 1 := by
  rw [total_as_divisor_sum 1 (by omega)]
  simp [P_one]

theorem T_prime_pow_succ {p : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime) (k : ℕ) :
    T (p ^ (k + 1)) = T (p ^ k) + P (p ^ (k + 1)) := by
  rw [total_as_divisor_sum _ (pow_pos hp.pos _),
    total_as_divisor_sum _ (pow_pos hp.pos _),
    Nat.sum_divisors_prime_pow hp, Nat.sum_divisors_prime_pow hp]
  simp only [Finset.sum_range_succ]

theorem T_prime_pow_mod_six_one {p k : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime)
    (hpmod : p % 6 = 1) :
    T (p ^ k) = p ^ k := by
  induction k with
  | zero => simpa using T_one
  | succ k ih =>
      rw [T_prime_pow_succ hp k, ih,
        P_prime_pow_mod_six_one hp (by omega) hpmod]
      simp only [Nat.add_sub_cancel]
      have hpone : 1 ≤ p := hp.one_le
      rw [pow_succ]
      calc
        p ^ k + p ^ k * (p - 1) =
            p ^ k * 1 + p ^ k * (p - 1) := by simp
        _ = p ^ k * (1 + (p - 1)) := by rw [Nat.mul_add]
        _ = p ^ k * p := by
          congr
          omega

theorem T_three_pow {k : ℕ} :
    T (3 ^ k) = (3 ^ (k + 1) - 1) / 2 := by
  induction k with
  | zero =>
      norm_num
      exact T_one
  | succ k ih =>
      rw [T_prime_pow_succ Nat.prime_three k, ih,
        P_three_pow (by omega)]
      rw [pow_succ, pow_succ]
      omega

theorem T_two_pow_succ {k : ℕ} :
    T (2 ^ (k + 1)) = 2 := by
  induction k with
  | zero =>
      rw [T_prime_pow_succ Nat.prime_two 0]
      have h := P_twice_odd (by norm_num : Odd 1)
      norm_num only [Nat.mul_one] at h
      norm_num only [pow_zero, pow_one, zero_add]
      rw [T_one, h, P_one]
  | succ k ih =>
      rw [T_prime_pow_succ Nat.prime_two (k + 1), ih]
      have hfour : 4 ∣ 2 ^ (k + 1 + 1) := by
        use 2 ^ k
        ring
      rw [P_eq_zero_of_four_dvd hfour]

theorem prime_eq_two_or_three_or_mod_six {p : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime) :
    p = 2 ∨ p = 3 ∨ p % 6 = 1 ∨ p % 6 = 5 := by
  by_cases hp2 : p = 2
  · exact Or.inl hp2
  right
  by_cases hp3 : p = 3
  · exact Or.inl hp3
  right
  have hoddmod : p % 2 = 1 := Nat.odd_iff.mp (hp.odd_of_ne_two hp2)
  have hmod3 : p % 3 ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    have hdvd : 3 ∣ p := Nat.dvd_iff_mod_eq_zero.mpr h
    have heq : 3 = p :=
      (Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq Nat.prime_three hp).mp hdvd
    exact hp3 heq.symm
  omega

theorem three_dvd_P_prime_pow_succ {p k : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime)
    (hp2 : p ≠ 2) :
    3 ∣ P (p ^ (k + 1)) := by
  rcases prime_eq_two_or_three_or_mod_six hp with
      rfl | rfl | hpmod | hpmod
  · exact False.elim (hp2 rfl)
  · rw [P_three_pow (by omega)]
    exact dvd_pow_self 3 (by omega)
  · rw [P_prime_pow_mod_six_one hp (by omega) hpmod]
    apply dvd_mul_of_dvd_right
    rw [Nat.dvd_iff_mod_eq_zero]
    omega
  · rw [P_prime_pow_mod_six_five hp (by omega) hpmod]
    apply dvd_mul_of_dvd_right
    rw [Nat.dvd_iff_mod_eq_zero]
    omega

theorem T_prime_pow_mod_three {p k : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime)
    (hp2 : p ≠ 2) :
    T (p ^ k) % 3 = 1 := by
  induction k with
  | zero =>
      simpa using congrArg (fun x : ℕ => x % 3) T_one
  | succ k ih =>
      rw [T_prime_pow_succ hp k, Nat.add_mod, ih]
      have hdiv := three_dvd_P_prime_pow_succ hp hp2 (k := k)
      rw [Nat.dvd_iff_mod_eq_zero] at hdiv
      omega

theorem even_P_prime_pow_succ {p k : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime)
    (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (hp3 : p ≠ 3) :
    Even (P (p ^ (k + 1))) := by
  rcases prime_eq_two_or_three_or_mod_six hp with
      rfl | rfl | hpmod | hpmod
  · exact False.elim (hp2 rfl)
  · exact False.elim (hp3 rfl)
  · rw [P_prime_pow_mod_six_one hp (by omega) hpmod]
    rcases hp.odd_of_ne_two hp2 with ⟨a, ha⟩
    apply Even.mul_left
    use a
    omega
  · rw [P_prime_pow_mod_six_five hp (by omega) hpmod]
    rcases hp.odd_of_ne_two hp2 with ⟨a, ha⟩
    apply Even.mul_left
    use a + 1
    omega

theorem odd_T_prime_pow {p k : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime)
    (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (hp3 : p ≠ 3) :
    Odd (T (p ^ k)) := by
  induction k with
  | zero =>
      simpa [T_one]
  | succ k ih =>
      rw [T_prime_pow_succ hp k]
      exact ih.add_even (even_P_prime_pow_succ hp hp2 hp3)

theorem T_prime_pow_ge {p k : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime)
    (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (hp3 : p ≠ 3) :
    p ^ k ≤ T (p ^ k) := by
  rcases prime_eq_two_or_three_or_mod_six hp with
      rfl | rfl | hpmod | hpmod
  · exact False.elim (hp2 rfl)
  · exact False.elim (hp3 rfl)
  · rw [T_prime_pow_mod_six_one hp hpmod]
  · induction k with
    | zero =>
        simpa using le_of_eq T_one.symm
    | succ k ih =>
        rw [T_prime_pow_succ hp k,
          P_prime_pow_mod_six_five hp (by omega) hpmod]
        simp only [Nat.add_sub_cancel, pow_succ]
        calc
          p ^ k * p ≤ p ^ k * (p + 1) := Nat.mul_le_mul_left _ (by omega)
          _ ≤ T (p ^ k) + p ^ k * (p + 1) := Nat.le_add_left _ _

theorem T_prime_pow_eq_iff_mod_six_one {p k : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime)
    (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (hp3 : p ≠ 3) (hk : k ≠ 0) :
    T (p ^ k) = p ^ k ↔ p % 6 = 1 := by
  rcases prime_eq_two_or_three_or_mod_six hp with
      rfl | rfl | hpmod | hpmod
  · exact False.elim (hp2 rfl)
  · exact False.elim (hp3 rfl)
  · simp [hpmod, T_prime_pow_mod_six_one hp hpmod]
  · have hgt : p ^ k < T (p ^ k) := by
      obtain ⟨j, rfl⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_succ_of_ne_zero hk
      rw [T_prime_pow_succ hp j,
        P_prime_pow_mod_six_five hp (by omega) hpmod]
      simp only [Nat.add_sub_cancel, pow_succ]
      calc
        p ^ j * p < p ^ j * (p + 1) :=
          Nat.mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left (by omega) (pow_pos hp.pos j)
        _ ≤ T (p ^ j) + p ^ j * (p + 1) := Nat.le_add_left _ _
    constructor
    · intro heq
      omega
    · intro h
      omega

/-- Main multiplicativity conclusion. -/
theorem multiplicative_conclusion :
    IsMultiplicative P ∧ IsMultiplicative T := by
  refine ⟨P_multiplicative, ?_⟩
  have hTone : T 1 = 1 := by
    rw [total_as_divisor_sum 1 (by omega)]
    simp [P_one]
  let pAF : ArithmeticFunction ℕ := ⟨P, P_zero⟩
  have hpAF : pAF.IsMultiplicative := by
    exact P_multiplicative
  have hconv : (ArithmeticFunction.zeta * pAF).IsMultiplicative :=
    ArithmeticFunction.isMultiplicative_zeta.mul hpAF
  have hT {k : ℕ} (hk : 0 < k) :
      T k = (ArithmeticFunction.zeta * pAF) k := by
    rw [total_as_divisor_sum k hk, ArithmeticFunction.zeta_mul_apply]
    rfl
  constructor
  · exact hTone
  · intro m n hcop
    rcases m.eq_zero_or_pos with rfl | hm
    · have hn : n = 1 := by
        simpa [Nat.coprime_zero_left] using hcop
      simp [hn, hTone]
    rcases n.eq_zero_or_pos with rfl | hn
    · have hmOne : m = 1 := by
        simpa [Nat.coprime_zero_right] using hcop
      simp [hmOne, hTone]
    rw [hT (Nat.mul_pos hm hn), hT hm, hT hn]
    exact hconv.map_mul_of_coprime hcop

theorem T_factorization {n : ℕ} (hn : n ≠ 0) :
    T n = n.factorization.prod fun p k => T (p ^ k) := by
  exact Nat.multiplicative_factorization T
    (fun _ _ h => multiplicative_conclusion.2.2 h) T_one hn

theorem T_odd_no_three_eq_iff {m : ℕ} (hm : Odd m)
    (h3 : ¬3 ∣ m) :
    T m = m ↔ ∀ p ∈ m.primeFactors, p % 6 = 1 := by
  have hm0 : m ≠ 0 := by
    rcases hm with ⟨a, ha⟩
    omega
  have hT := T_factorization hm0
  have hmprod := Nat.factorization_prod_pow_eq_self hm0
  rw [hT]
  conv_lhs =>
    rhs
    rw [← hmprod]
  rw [eq_comm]
  have hle : ∀ p ∈ m.factorization.support,
      p ^ m.factorization p ≤ T (p ^ m.factorization p) := by
    intro p hpMem
    have hpPrimeMem : p ∈ m.primeFactors := by simpa using hpMem
    have hp : p.Prime := Nat.prime_of_mem_primeFactors hpPrimeMem
    have hp2 : p ≠ 2 := by
      intro hpEq
      subst p
      have hdiv : 2 ∣ m := (Nat.mem_primeFactors.mp hpPrimeMem).2.1
      rw [Nat.dvd_iff_mod_eq_zero] at hdiv
      have hoddmod : m % 2 = 1 := Nat.odd_iff.mp hm
      omega
    have hp3 : p ≠ 3 := by
      intro hpEq
      subst p
      exact h3 (Nat.mem_primeFactors.mp hpPrimeMem).2.1
    exact T_prime_pow_ge hp hp2 hp3
  constructor
  · intro hprod p hpMem
    have hpSupp : p ∈ m.factorization.support := by simpa using hpMem
    have hp : p.Prime := Nat.prime_of_mem_primeFactors hpMem
    have hp2 : p ≠ 2 := by
      intro hpEq
      subst p
      have hdiv : 2 ∣ m := (Nat.mem_primeFactors.mp hpMem).2.1
      rw [Nat.dvd_iff_mod_eq_zero] at hdiv
      have hoddmod : m % 2 = 1 := Nat.odd_iff.mp hm
      omega
    have hp3 : p ≠ 3 := by
      intro hpEq
      subst p
      exact h3 (Nat.mem_primeFactors.mp hpMem).2.1
    have hk : m.factorization p ≠ 0 :=
      Finsupp.mem_support_iff.mp hpSupp
    have heqLocal :
        p ^ m.factorization p = T (p ^ m.factorization p) := by
      by_contra hne
      have hlt :
          p ^ m.factorization p < T (p ^ m.factorization p) :=
        lt_of_le_of_ne (hle p hpSupp) hne
      have hprodlt := Finset.prod_lt_prod
        (s := m.factorization.support)
        (f := fun q => q ^ m.factorization q)
        (g := fun q => T (q ^ m.factorization q))
        (fun q hq => by
          have hqMem : q ∈ m.primeFactors := by simpa using hq
          exact pow_pos (Nat.prime_of_mem_primeFactors hqMem).pos _)
        hle ⟨p, hpSupp, hlt⟩
      exact (ne_of_lt hprodlt) hprod
    exact (T_prime_pow_eq_iff_mod_six_one hp hp2 hp3 hk).mp
      heqLocal.symm
  · intro h
    apply Finset.prod_congr rfl
    intro p hpSupp
    have hpMem : p ∈ m.primeFactors := by simpa using hpSupp
    have hp : p.Prime := Nat.prime_of_mem_primeFactors hpMem
    exact (T_prime_pow_mod_six_one hp (h p hpMem)).symm

theorem prod_mod_three_eq_one {s : Finset ℕ} {f : ℕ → ℕ}
    (h : ∀ p ∈ s, f p % 3 = 1) :
    (∏ p ∈ s, f p) % 3 = 1 := by
  classical
  induction s using Finset.induction_on with
  | empty => simp
  | @insert p s hpMem ih =>
      rw [Finset.prod_insert hpMem, Nat.mul_mod]
      rw [h p (Finset.mem_insert_self p s),
        ih (fun q hq => h q (Finset.mem_insert_of_mem hq))]

theorem prod_mod_two_eq_one {s : Finset ℕ} {f : ℕ → ℕ}
    (h : ∀ p ∈ s, f p % 2 = 1) :
    (∏ p ∈ s, f p) % 2 = 1 := by
  classical
  induction s using Finset.induction_on with
  | empty => simp
  | @insert p s hpMem ih =>
      rw [Finset.prod_insert hpMem, Nat.mul_mod]
      rw [h p (Finset.mem_insert_self p s),
        ih (fun q hq => h q (Finset.mem_insert_of_mem hq))]

theorem T_mod_three_eq_one_or_two {n : ℕ} (hn : n ≠ 0) :
    T n % 3 = if 2 ∣ n then 2 else 1 := by
  rw [T_factorization hn]
  classical
  by_cases h2 : 2 ∣ n
  · rw [if_pos h2]
    change
      (∏ p ∈ n.factorization.support,
        T (p ^ n.factorization p)) % 3 = 2
    have hfacPos : 0 < n.factorization 2 :=
      Nat.Prime.factorization_pos_of_dvd Nat.prime_two hn h2
    have h2Mem : 2 ∈ n.factorization.support :=
      Finsupp.mem_support_iff.mpr (by omega)
    rw [← Finset.mul_prod_erase n.factorization.support
      (fun p => T (p ^ n.factorization p)) h2Mem, Nat.mul_mod]
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_succ_of_ne_zero (by omega :
      n.factorization 2 ≠ 0)
    rw [hk, T_two_pow_succ]
    have hrest :
        (∏ p ∈ n.factorization.support.erase 2,
          T (p ^ n.factorization p)) % 3 = 1 := by
      apply prod_mod_three_eq_one
      intro p hpMem
      have hpSupport : p ∈ n.factorization.support :=
        (Finset.mem_erase.mp hpMem).2
      have hpPrimeMem : p ∈ n.primeFactors := by simpa using hpSupport
      have hp : p.Prime := Nat.prime_of_mem_primeFactors hpPrimeMem
      have hp2 : p ≠ 2 := (Finset.mem_erase.mp hpMem).1
      exact T_prime_pow_mod_three hp hp2
    rw [hrest]
  · rw [if_neg h2]
    change
      (∏ p ∈ n.factorization.support,
        T (p ^ n.factorization p)) % 3 = 1
    apply prod_mod_three_eq_one
    intro p hpMem
    have hpPrimeMem : p ∈ n.primeFactors := by simpa using hpMem
    have hp : p.Prime := Nat.prime_of_mem_primeFactors hpPrimeMem
    have hp2 : p ≠ 2 := by
      intro hpEq
      subst p
      exact h2 (Nat.mem_primeFactors.mp hpPrimeMem).2.1
    exact T_prime_pow_mod_three hp hp2

theorem T_even_no_three_mod_four {n : ℕ} (hn : n ≠ 0)
    (h2 : 2 ∣ n) (h3 : ¬3 ∣ n) :
    T n % 4 = 2 := by
  rw [T_factorization hn]
  classical
  change
    (∏ p ∈ n.factorization.support,
      T (p ^ n.factorization p)) % 4 = 2
  have hfacPos : 0 < n.factorization 2 :=
    Nat.Prime.factorization_pos_of_dvd Nat.prime_two hn h2
  have h2Mem : 2 ∈ n.factorization.support :=
    Finsupp.mem_support_iff.mpr (by omega)
  rw [← Finset.mul_prod_erase n.factorization.support
    (fun p => T (p ^ n.factorization p)) h2Mem]
  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_succ_of_ne_zero (by omega :
    n.factorization 2 ≠ 0)
  rw [hk, T_two_pow_succ]
  have hrestOdd :
      (∏ p ∈ n.factorization.support.erase 2,
        T (p ^ n.factorization p)) % 2 = 1 := by
    apply prod_mod_two_eq_one
    intro p hpMem
    have hpSupport : p ∈ n.factorization.support :=
      (Finset.mem_erase.mp hpMem).2
    have hpPrimeMem : p ∈ n.primeFactors := by simpa using hpSupport
    have hp : p.Prime := Nat.prime_of_mem_primeFactors hpPrimeMem
    have hp2 : p ≠ 2 := (Finset.mem_erase.mp hpMem).1
    have hp3 : p ≠ 3 := by
      intro hpEq
      subst p
      exact h3 (Nat.mem_primeFactors.mp hpPrimeMem).2.1
    exact Nat.odd_iff.mp (odd_T_prime_pow hp hp2 hp3)
  omega

/-- Exact characterization that refutes the positive-density conjecture. -/
theorem equality_characterization (n : ℕ) (hn : 0 < n) :
    T n = n ↔
      ∃ ε ≤ 1, ∃ m,
        n = 2 ^ ε * m ∧
        Odd m ∧
        ∀ p ∈ m.primeFactors, p % 6 = 1 := by
  have hn0 : n ≠ 0 := Nat.ne_of_gt hn
  have hT2 : T 2 = 2 := by
    simpa using (T_two_pow_succ (k := 0))
  constructor
  · intro hEq
    have h3 : ¬3 ∣ n := by
      intro h3div
      have hmod := T_mod_three_eq_one_or_two hn0
      rw [hEq] at hmod
      have hnmod : n % 3 = 0 := Nat.dvd_iff_mod_eq_zero.mp h3div
      split at hmod <;> omega
    by_cases hodd : Odd n
    · refine ⟨0, by omega, n, by simp, hodd, ?_⟩
      exact (T_odd_no_three_eq_iff hodd h3).mp hEq
    · have hevenmod : n % 2 = 0 := Nat.not_odd_iff.mp hodd
      have h2 : 2 ∣ n := Nat.dvd_iff_mod_eq_zero.mpr hevenmod
      by_cases h4 : 4 ∣ n
      · have hTmod := T_even_no_three_mod_four hn0 h2 h3
        rw [hEq] at hTmod
        have hnmod : n % 4 = 0 := Nat.dvd_iff_mod_eq_zero.mp h4
        omega
      · have hnmod4 : n % 4 = 2 := by
          rw [Nat.dvd_iff_mod_eq_zero] at h4
          have hlt := Nat.mod_lt n (by omega : 0 < 4)
          omega
        let m := n / 2
        have hnEq : n = 2 * m := by
          dsimp [m]
          omega
        have hmOdd : Odd m := by
          rw [Nat.odd_iff]
          dsimp [m]
          omega
        have hmPos : 0 < m := by
          dsimp [m]
          omega
        have h3m : ¬3 ∣ m := by
          intro h
          apply h3
          rw [hnEq]
          exact dvd_mul_of_dvd_right h 2
        have hmul := multiplicative_conclusion.2.2 hmOdd.coprime_two_left
        have hmEq : T m = m := by
          rw [← hnEq, hEq] at hmul
          rw [hT2] at hmul
          omega
        refine ⟨1, by omega, m, ?_, hmOdd, ?_⟩
        · simpa using hnEq
        · exact (T_odd_no_three_eq_iff hmOdd h3m).mp hmEq
  · rintro ⟨ε, hε, m, hnEq, hmOdd, hprimes⟩
    have hm0 : m ≠ 0 := by
      rcases hmOdd with ⟨a, ha⟩
      omega
    have h3m : ¬3 ∣ m := by
      intro h3div
      have h3Mem : 3 ∈ m.primeFactors :=
        Nat.mem_primeFactors.mpr ⟨Nat.prime_three, h3div, hm0⟩
      have := hprimes 3 h3Mem
      norm_num at this
    have hmEq : T m = m :=
      (T_odd_no_three_eq_iff hmOdd h3m).mpr hprimes
    interval_cases ε
    · simpa [hnEq] using hmEq
    · rw [pow_one] at hnEq
      rw [hnEq, multiplicative_conclusion.2.2 hmOdd.coprime_two_left,
        hT2, hmEq]

def equalitySet : Set ℕ := {n | 0 < n ∧ T n = n}

/-- The equality set has natural density zero. -/
theorem equalitySet_density_zero :
    Tendsto
      (fun N : ℕ =>
        (((equalitySet ∩ Icc 1 N).ncard : ℕ) : ℝ) / N)
      atTop (𝓝 0) := by
  apply A354766Sieve.density_zero_of_avoidsBadPrimes
  intro n hn hnPos p hp hp5 hpn
  rcases (equality_characterization n hnPos).mp hn.2 with
    ⟨ε, hε, m, hnForm, hmOdd, hmPrimes⟩
  have hpNeTwo : p ≠ 2 := by
    intro h
    subst p
    norm_num at hp5
  have hpNotPow : ¬p ∣ 2 ^ ε := by
    intro h
    have hd : p ∣ 2 := hp.dvd_of_dvd_pow h
    exact hpNeTwo ((Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq hp Nat.prime_two).mp hd)
  have hpm : p ∣ m := by
    rw [hnForm] at hpn
    exact (hp.dvd_mul.mp hpn).resolve_left hpNotPow
  have hm0 : m ≠ 0 := by
    rcases hmOdd with ⟨a, ha⟩
    omega
  have hpMem : p ∈ m.primeFactors := Nat.mem_primeFactors.mpr ⟨hp, hpm, hm0⟩
  have hp1 := hmPrimes p hpMem
  omega

theorem A278085Primary_multiplicative : IsMultiplicative A278085Primary := by
  have h : A278085Primary = P := funext A278085Primary_eq_P
  rw [h]
  exact P_multiplicative

end A354766
